Explanatory Remarks:

1. Structural elements are enclosed in <>.
2. Editorial notes and additions are enclosed in () or [].

Text:

<1>
pippalyo dīpyakaścaiva tathā mūṣikakarṇikāḥ|
bilvasya pallavāḥ śvetā haridrā madhukaṃ tathā||
ete kuṣṭhaṃ pratighnanti yuktāḥ pānapralepanaiḥ|
gavāṃ pittaṃ śunaḥ pittamalāvūṃ kaṭukāṃ tathā||
nimbaṃ nataṃ śarkarāṃ ca dadyāttadvatpralepanam|
yātuṇḍamaṇḍalīdvīpāt mūlānyeḍagajasya ca||
surādanīṃ sugandhāṃ ca dadyāttadvatpralepanam|
suvarṇapuṣpīṃ kaṭukāṃ śyāmāṃ tejovatīṃ tathā||
trivṛtsañjātakaṃ? caiva kuṣṭhe saṃśodhanaṃ viduḥ|
āragvadhaṃ ca bhārgīṃ ca sāśvagandhāṃ gavādanīm||
śvetāṃ jyotiṣmatīṃ caiva sūkṣmacūrṇāni kārayet|
gavāṃ pittena taccūrṇaṃ saptakṛtvaḥ subhāvitam||
yojyaṃ sarṣapatailena saindhavena ca yuktitaḥ|
etadvai hṛtadoṣasya mitadadhyannabhojinaḥ||
tanulepanamuddiṣṭaṃ kṛmikuṣṭhavināśanam|
gavedhukamakuṣṭhau ca khadirakvāthapācitau||
tāvapyubhau prayuñjīta kuṣṭhināṃ kuṣṭhaśāntaye|
atha kaṇḍūghnavṛkṣāṇāṃ rasāḥ syuḥ kuṣṭhināṃ hitāḥ||
pralepapariṣekeṣu khadirastu praśasyate|
khadirodaka pāyī syātkhadirodakabhojanaḥ||
bhūyiṣṭhamudakārthe ca kurvīta khadirodaram|
sāravantau mahāvṛkṣau dhavarohita kāvubhau||
śiṃśupā cāśvakarṇaśca khadiro bilvamāśritaḥ|
kadalīmuṣkakau hiṃsrā bhadrodumbarikāphalam||
etairapyuṣitaṃ kuṣṭhe gomūtraṃ pariṣecanam|
sālāśvakarṇaniryāso niryāsaḥ kuliśasya ca||
ete kuṣṭhanimittānāṃ vraṇānāṃ syuḥ prapīḍakāḥ|
puṇḍarīkasya vakṣyāmi kriyāṃ rogavināśinīm||
āvartāṃ śālmalīmūlaṃ malayūkāṃ sudhāṃ varīm|
bhadrodumbarikāmūlaṃ phalānyāvalgujāni ca||
rātrau yantreṇa niṣpīḍya danticitrakameva ca|
pāṭhāṃ haridre ca tathā gavāṃ mūtreṇa yojayet||
aṣṭabhāgāvaśiṣṭaṃ tatsādhitaṃ supariṣkṛtam|
pibetkālyaṃ samutthāya śvitrī śītodakaṃ pibet||
sarvataḥ parirakṣettu sthāpayeccainamātape|
bhaiṣajyapīḍitā doṣāstvacamasyānusaṃśritāḥ||
maṇḍaleṣu tapasphoṭaṃ janayantyarkaraśmibhiḥ|
sphoṭeṣvatha tu jāteṣu chāyāyāmupaveśayet||
śītābhiradbhiḥ prakṣālya bhojayedrasabhojanam|
saṃkṣubhyante'tha vai toyaparipūrṇā ivāmbudāḥ||
sūcyagreṇātha tīkṣṇena vyathayetkaṇṭakena vā|
candanasya ca yatsāraṃ badarāt khadirasya ca||
etaiḥ pānīyapiṣṭaistu sarpiḥkṣaudrasamanvitaiḥ|
patreṣu puṣkariṇyāstu saṃviṣṭasyātha dehinaḥ||
tatastvālepayetsphoṭān śītodakapariplutān|
kṣīriṇāṃ cāpi vṛkṣāṇāṃ tvacassaṃkṣodayed bhiṣak||
taiḥ kaṣāyeiśca kalkaiśca siñcayellepayecca tam|
kharjūrasya ca bījāni śaṅkhanābhiḥ ca dāpayet||
añjanaṃ tagaraṃ patraṃ gairikaṃ nīlamutpalam|
eṣāṃ sūkṣmeṇa cūrṇena sravantamavacūrṇayet||
śakṛddrākṣākṣaudrayukto dātavyaḥ paripoṭane|
āhārotsādanābhyaṅgaṃ pūrveṇa vidhinā bhiṣak||
kārayetkuṣṭhināmevaṃ tataḥ sampadyate sukhī|
na ca vyādhimupekṣeta śarīraṃ patitaṃ budhaḥ|
tvareta śamane tasya pradīptasyeva veśmanaḥ||
yathā hyekāntato vṛkṣo vardhate sukhataśśanaiḥ|
tathā śarīramāsādya rogo vardhata eva tu||
tasmādrakṣanmahāvegādakṣīṇasyaiva dehinaḥ|
prāgevopacayādrogānhanyādvaidyo vicakṣaṇaḥ||
tryahāttryahāccāvapīḍaḥ pakṣānmāsādvamettathā|
recayedayane pūrṇe sirāmokṣo vidhīyate||
śyāmākāśca makuṣṭhāni caṇakāḥ kuṣṭhināṃ hitāḥ|
evaṃ hi vartamānānāṃ kuṣṭhaṃ praśamameti vai|| ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele caturtho'dhyāyaḥ||
||pañcamo'dhyāyaḥ||
athātyāśitīyaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
athāśitena pītena khāditena ca yuktitaḥ|
balamāpyāyate jantoḥ sadyo vahnirivendhanaiḥ||
tenaivātyādṛteneha bhojanena śarīrajāḥ|
vyādhayassaṃpravardhante kāyāgnāvapi dūṣite||
dvividhaṃ tu bhiṣagvidyādāhārasyaiva lāghavam|
dvividhaṃ gauravaṃ bhukte procyamānaṃ nibodhata||
mātrālaghussyādāhāraḥ kaściddravyalaghuḥ smṛtaḥ|
mātrāgurustathaiva syāddravyataśca tathā guruḥ||
purāṇaśālayo mudgāḥ śaśatittirilāvukāḥ|
evaṃprakāraṃ yaccānyat taddravyaṃ laghu saṃsmṛtam||
grāmyānūpaudakaṃ māṃsaṃ dadhi piṣṭaṃ tilāhvayam|
evaṃprakāraṃ yaccānyattaddravyaṃ gurviti smṛtam||
tatra yo mātrayā bhuṅkte dravyaṃ gurvapi mānavaḥ|
āhāraṃ tasya paśyanti laghumeva cikitsakāḥ||
śālyādīnyapi yo'tyarthamaśnāti sulaghūnyapi|
āhārassa tathārūpo vyaktaṃ sampadyate guruḥ||
dravyasya laghuno yuktyā sauhityaṃ yo'dhigacchati|
ekāntapathyaṃ taṃ vidyādāhāraṃ kuśalo laghum||
yadi yuktyāpi sauhityaṃ dravyasyālaghuno vrajet|
tathāvidhamihāhāraṃ gurumeva bravīmyaham||
tasmāttribhāgasasauhityamardhasauhityameva vā|
āhāraṃ laghumanvicchet gurūṇāṃ sevitaṃ sadā||
laghu vāpi samāsādya dravyaṃ yo hyatisevate|
tallaghvapyatisaṃyuktaṃ koṣṭhe sampadyate guru||
gurulāghavavidvaidyo narāṇāṃ vardhayatyasūn|
tasmādevaṃ vijānīyāddravyāṇāṃ gurulāghavam||
evameva ca bhojyena pathyenāpyāyate naraḥ|
hīyate cāpyapathyena praduṣṭairmārutādibhiḥ||
svasthasyānnena vardhante dhātavaḥ śoṇitādayaḥ|
vyādhitasyāpi doṣāya bhojyaṃ pariṇamatyatha||
yathā hyakālavṛṣṭyā ca bhūmau bījaṃ vipadyate|
vardhate kālavṛṣṭyā ca dehino'pi tathā rasaiḥ||
yaśca nityamudāraḥ syātsukhaniścalamānasaḥ|
laghupānarataḥ sātmye'pi yathāgasi dūragaḥ||
snāto'nuliptaḥ sragvī syāddhautadantassvalaṃkṛtaḥ|
dharmassatyamahiṃsā ca prāyo hyannaṃ samāśritam||
vamayecchaleṣmarogāṃśca paittikāṃśca virecayet|
nirūhayedvātarogān raktajāṃścāvasecayet||
raktaśālīn samudgān vā ṣaṣṭikānathavā punaḥ|
susvinnānmātrayāśnīyāt ghṛtasaindhavasaṃyutān||
īṣatpippalikaṃ yūṣamīṣacca madhunānvitam|
īṣatsalavaṇaṃ cāpi payaścārdhakṛtaṃ pibet||
viṣkirānpratudāṃścājān jāṅgalāṃśca mṛgadvijān|
yaccānyatkaṭukaṃ pāke bhojyaṃ tannityamācaret||
pādābhyāṃ na caretkṛṣṭe viṣame kaṇṭakeṣu ca|
gahanaṃ paradārāṃśca nadīḥ pūrṇāśca na vrajet||
kalahaṃ cāpi kopaṃ ca rātricaryāṃ vivarjayet|
gajasya na syādāsannaḥ toyārdrasya ca vājinaḥ||
janavādāṃśca śauṇḍāṃśca dviṣataścāpi mānavān|
mlecchānnikṛtibuddhīṃśca na seveta kadācana||
sarvān sarīsṛpāṃścaiva kṣudrapādāṃśca na spṛśet|
mahiṣān gavayān khaḍgān vyāghrān siṃhāṃśca varjayet||
nārohetpādapān veśma na dhāvedvarṣadurjale|
na gavāṃ pratilomaṃ ca na madhye nāgrato vrajet||
sadyaḥ pravṛṣṭau nāroheddhimavantaṃ ca parvatam|
ghṛtamuṣṇāmbu ca bhajedetadārogyamuttamam|| ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele pañcamo'dhyāyaḥ||
||ṣaṣṭho'dhyāyaḥ||
atha na vegāndhāraṇīyaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
na vegāndhārayeddhīraḥ prāptānmūtrapurīṣayoḥ|
na vātasya na pittasya na śuklasya kaphasya ca||
udgāracchardijṛmbhāṇāṃ tathā ca kṣavathorapi|
na bāṣpasya na nidrādeḥ prāptaṃ vegaṃ vidhārayet||
rogasañjanakā hyete hiṃsyurāyurvidhāritāḥ|
etāneva prakuryuśca tathā'prāptāssamīritāḥ||
vātamūtrapurīṣāṇāṃ dhāraṇādanilādayaḥ|
kupyanti retasaścāpi śarkarā sāśmarī tathā||
tvagdoṣo vā jvaro vā syāt gātre śophādi vā punaḥ|
chardipittakaphānāṃ tu dhāraṇena samīraṇāt||
jṛmbhādidhāraṇādāndhyaṃ timiraṃ bāṣpadhāraṇāt|
kṣavathūdgāranidrādeḥ dhāraṇātsyācchirorujā||
tasmānmūtrapurīṣaṃ vai visṛjetkālyamutthitaḥ|
uktā hyadhāraṇīyāstu dhāraṇīyānnibodhata||
lobhamohabhiyāṃ tṛṣṇācintayormadamānayoḥ|
śauryasya ca tathā vidvān prāptaṃ vegaṃ vidhārayet||
etaddhārayato hyasya balamāyuśca vardhate|
udaṅmukhaśśucau deśe prāṅmukhaścā'vakuṇṭhitaḥ||
āvaśyakamupāsīta kṛte cādbhirupaspṛśet|
kṛtaśaucastato janturbhakṣayeddantadhāvanam||
dhavaṃ palāśaw nyagrodhaw naktamālamathāpi vā|| śirīṣaṃ karavīraṃ ca jātivṛkṣamathārjunam||
eteṣāṃ dantadhavanaṃ śreṣṭhamāha punarvasuḥ|
āpāṭya vidalīkṛtya dantamāṃsamabādhayan||
tadasya śodhayedvaktraṃ pittasya ca kaphasya ca|
śamanaṃ vātaraktādeḥ kurute dantadhāvanam||
mustakalkaṃ satriphalaṃ madhu tailaṃ sukhodakam|
pratyekaṃ kabalagrāhaṃ dhārayedanupūrvaśaḥ||
vātapittakaphā duṣṭāḥ śāmyanti kabalagrahāt|
mādhūkaṃ copadehaghnaṃ svararaktaprasādanam||
tailaṃ dantyaṃ surabhikṛttadabhiṣyandanāśanam|
kṣataṃ saṃrohayedvaktraṃ śīryamāṇaṃ niyacchati||
indriyāṇāṃ mato'bhyaṅgo gurudoṣavimokṣaṇaḥ|
tvagdoṣaśamano balyo vātaraktaprasādanaḥ||
grāmyadharmātigatyā ca rathāśvagajapātanāt|
daṇḍamuṣṭihatānāṃ ca rujāśramavināśanaḥ||
tvacaḥ prasādano'bhyaṅgaḥ śāmyatyutsādanātklamaḥ|
praharṣaṃ kurute sthairyaṃ malaghnaṃ cāvasecanam||
vilepanaṃ hlādayati kaṇḍūṃ hanti prasādanam|
śiśiraṃ hyañjanaṃ dṛṣṭeḥ timiraṃ ca praṇaśyati||
tanūruhendriyabalaṃ nasyatailādupāgatāt|
svararaktaprasādaśca naśyanti palitāni ca||
svaktaḥ sūtsāditaścaiva praghṛṣṭaśca yathābalam|
yathartuvāriṇā snātastvanuliptastataḥ param||
prasādhitottamāṅgaśca tato nasyaṃ pradāpayet|
tatassaṃśuddhadoṣasya dhūmamasya prakalpayet||
kuṭannaṭailāhrīberaṃ dhyāmakāgarucandanam|
kālānusāryāṃ naladaṃ pṛthvīkāmatha guggulam||
tālīsaṃ padmakaṃ mustaṃ priyaṃguṃ sahareṇukām|
haridrāṃ ca sugandhāṃ ca saralāṃ devadāru ca||
śrīveṣṭakaṃ sarjarasaṃ corakaṃ ceti saṃharet|
vaktraṃ ca sarpiṣāktaṃ syāddhūmametaṃ tataḥ pibet||
aṣṭāṃgulapramāṇā vāpyathavā dvādaśāṃgulā|
ṣaḍaṃgulapramāṇā vā dhūmavartiḥ praśasyate||
sauvarṇaṃ rājataṃ tāmraṃ gajadantasya vā punaḥ|
vṛkṣasāramayaṃ vāpi dhūmanetraṃ praśasyate||
caturvā pañcakṛtvo vā dhūmaṃ srotasyavācaret|
vātānulomanaṃ kuryācchleṣmāṇaṃ cāpakarṣati|
balaṃ dadāti dṛṣṭeśca samyagdhūmo niṣevitaḥ||
aṣṭau dhūmasya kālāḥ syuryeṣāmante praśasyate|
utthitasyaiva śayanāddantaprakṣālane kṛte||
jalakrīḍānivṛttasya tathā bhuktavato'śanam|
kṣutoccāravyavāyānte bhuktavāntavatastathā||
guṇāṃścaikaikaśasteṣāṃ dhūmapānakṛtān śṛṇu|
tatra śayyotthitasyaiva vakṣyāmyatha yathākramam||
hantyutsannaṃ kaphaṃ jantoṣṣaḍindriyavibodhanaḥ|
pratibuddhasya ceddhūmo mārutasyānulomanaḥ||
dantaprakṣālanādūrdhvaṃ rogānsamyagvyapohati|
cyutaśleṣmāpanayanaḥ sugandhiviśadāsyakṛt||
dantamāṃsakṣayakṛtavyādhayo vadaneṣu ye|
doṣāḥ kāsaprabhṛtayastāṃśca dhūmo vyapohati||
śirogatā yāśca rujāḥ salilakrīḍanodbhavāḥ|
karṇaśūlaṃ pratiśyāyaṃ cāśu dhūmo vyapohati||
vātapittakaphān dhūmaḥ śamayedbhojanodgatān|
śiroviśuddhiṃ kurute bhuktaṃ ca sthāpayatyapi||
dhamanipratipannastu vāyuḥ kṣavathuneritaḥ|
vyāpnoti sarvasrotāṃsi sa dhūmenānulomyate||
varcaḥkarmoddhato vāyurgalamūrdhaśirogataḥ|
prāṇānādhmāpayati vā sa dhūmenānulomyate||
śuklasrotogato vāyurūrdhvameva prapadyate|
sa ghorān hi sṛjedrogāṃstaṃ dhūmenānulomayet||
rogādhipataye chardyā sthānebhyo'bhisamīritāḥ|
anārogyāya kalpante teṣāṃ dhūmaḥ praśasyate||
hṛteṣu doṣeṣvanilaḥ śūnyasthānāni saṃcaran|
sammūrcchati śiro gatvā taṃ dhūmenānulomayet||
pūrvaṃ pītodako bhuktvā labhate kṛśatāṃ naraḥ|
madhye bhaktasya pibato bhojanaṃ nāpakṛṣyate||
bhuktavānatha pānīyaṃ paścādanupibecca yaḥ|
tadāpyānugataṃ bhuktaṃ samatvāyopapadyate||
agnau pratāpayetpāṇī syātāṃ yāvattu nirjalau|
tataḥ padaśataṃ gacchedbhaktaṃ hyevaṃ prayātyadhaḥ||
na rātrau dadhi bhuñjīta nāghṛtaṃ nāpyamākṣikam|
kuṣṭhau patepi vāṃchaṃ kiṃ kaṇṭharogācca buddhimān||
toyakṣiptaṃ spṛśennānnaṃ na ca paryuṣitāśanaḥ|
na cājīrṇāśano janturjighāṃsurvyādhisaṃbhavam||
sopānatkastathā daṇḍī chatreṇa vyajanena ca|
ābādhān varjayetsarvānvicaredāpariśramāt||
tapasvinaḥ pitṛṛndevānbrāhmaṇāṃśca samāhitaḥ|
arcayanvidhivannityaṃ jīvedvarṣaśataṃ naraḥ||
vāto hi śūlaṃ vihataḥ karoti mūtrapradoṣaṃ janayecca mūtram|
kāsaḥ pratiśyāyamatho kṣayaṃ ca śvāsaṃ ca kuryādvihate tu vege||
purīṣamānāhamatīva kuryād reto'varodhaḥ kurute'tha ṣāṇḍyam|
tasmāddhi vegaṃ na vidhārayeta naro ya icchediha dīrghamāyuḥ|| ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele ṣaṣṭho'dhyāyaḥ||
||saptamo'dhyāyaḥ||
athāta indriyopakramaṇīyaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
indriyāṇi hi sarvāṇi parijīryanti jīryataḥ|
tasmātsarvatra vayasi yathākālaṃ striyaṃ vrajet||
na tvanāpyāyitabalaḥ samāgacchettu yoṣitaḥ|
nāyoniṣu prayuñjīta tiryagyonīśca varjayet||
na prāptaṃ dhārayedvegaṃ gatvā śītodakaṃ spṛśet|
na tu śrānto na vā bhukto na kliṣṭo'pyāvilo na ca||
kṛśo nātyaśito vāpi dīno vā maithunaṃ vrajet|
mitavāk satataṃ yaḥ syātsnigdhānnāśī yuvā''ācaret||
subahusnigdharetāśca yathartuśayanāśanaḥ|
jīrṇāmāsannasatvāṃ ca vyādhitāṃ malināṃ kṛśām||
vyaṅginīṃ parakāmāṃ ca pūtikoṣṭhāṃ ca varjayet|
balamārogyamāyuśca vapustejaśca dehinām||
hīyate hīdṛśīṃ gatvā prāpyākālajarāmiva|
ṛtāvṛtau yathākālaṃ maithunaṃ nā samācaret||
varṣāsu navarātrāttu daśarātrāccharadyapi|
pañcāhāddhemasamaye saptāhācchiśire tathā||
pakṣādvasante grīṣme tu māsi māsi samācaret|
nidāghe paścime māse maithunaṃ caiva varjayet||
athavā yauvanotsāhabalasnehasamanvitaḥ|
śiśirāmbukṛtasnāno hṛdyasraganulepanaḥ||
nātivyāyāmanirataḥ kṣīramāṃsaghṛtāśanaḥ|
hṛdyamālyāmbaradharā hṛdyābharaṇabhūṣitāḥ||
hṛdyayauvanasaṃpannāḥ priyāḥ priyakathānugāḥ|
hṛṣṭāḥ sarvatra kāle a śaktyardhena vrajetstriyaḥ||
caturdaśīṃ pañcadaśīmaṣṭamīṃ ca vivarjayet|
pibetkṣīraṃ ghṛtaṃ nityamāyuṣyakaraṇaṃ hitam||
balavarṇakaraṃ hyetadārogyakaraṇaṃ tathā|
saṃhārayedromanakhaṃ trirmāsasya ca mānavaḥ||
oṣadhīśca maṇīṃścaiva maṅgalyān dhārayetsadā|
mantrānāvartayeccāpi brahmaproktaṃ sanātanam||
sa me mādyāpagāddehādvāyuḥ prāṇāśca ye sadā|
indro me balamādadyād śivaṃ cāpo diśantu naḥ||
ityevaṃ mantramārṣaṃ vai bhuktvā gatvāthavā striyaḥ|
saṃjapanvai spṛśan vāri tathā'syāyurna hīyate|| ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele saptamo'dhyāyaḥ||
||aṣṭamo'dhyāyaḥ||
athāto mātrāśitīyaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
mātrāśī syādvipakvāśī daṇḍapāṇirmitādhvagaḥ|
yathartubhojanaṃ kṛtvā janturdhūmamimaṃ pibet||
kaiḍaryaśśatapuṣpā ca tālīsāguruguggulu|
priyaṅgavaśca kṣaumaṃ ca sarpiṣā snehitaṃ pibet||
bhojanenerito vāyuḥ koṣṭhācchirasi tiṣṭhati|
prasidhyati sa dhūmena kaphaścāpaiti cakṣuṣaḥ||
taṇḍulān pṛthukāṃścāpi sarvān piṣṭamayāṃstilān|
na khāded bhuktavān janturmātrāṃ khādedbubhukṣitaḥ||
adhvanā na śramaṃ gacchetsarvānvegānna dhārayet|
hitvāpi sarvakāryāṇi tathāsyāyurna hīyate||
varjayedviṣamaṃ duḥkhamāsanaṃ pādalambanam|
rakṣeccharīraṃ vātebhyo nityameva durāsanāt||
atyāsanamatisthānamaticaṅkramaṇāni ca|
atisvapnamaśayyāṃ ca tathā cāpyatibhāṣaṇam||
yacca kiṃcidviceṣṭābhiratyarthaṃ kurute naraḥ|
sarvametadanāyuṣyaṃ bhiṣajaḥ paricakṣate||
samasthānāsanagatissamaśayyāśanastathā|
samajalpaviceṣṭaśca ciraṃ jīvati mānavaḥ||
nivātamupaseveta sukhavātaṃ kadācana|
nivātamāyuṣaḥ pathyamārogyāya ca sarvadā||
ātape nācaretkarma śirastrāṇamṛte tathā|
nidāghavarjaṃ ca sadā nāvaśyā ye samācaret||
śaradvasantaprāvṛṭsu kārayeccānuvāsanam|
rogāśca vastidāpyāḥ syuryasya nityaṃ ca dāpayet||
svapnāntasandhyayoścāpi na khādenna ca saṃviśet|
na rātrau vicaredeko nāgāraṃ śūnyamāviśet||
catuṣpathaṃ pariharetparvate na ciraṃ vaset|
rātrau ca vṛkṣamūlāni paradārāṃtathaiva ca||
rājāniṣṭaṃ prajābhaṅgaṃ dāvāgniṃ kalahaṃ nṛṇām|
mattonmattāṃśca saṃlakṣya dūrādeva vivarjayet||
vairiṇo nopaseveta bāhubhyāṃ na nadīṃ taret|
hastyaśvagāśca sarpaṃ ca dūrataḥ parivarjayet||
madhumehaśca śoṣaśca tṛṣṇā vātādyupadrutiḥ|
madyapānāttu na bhavettasmāttadvidhivatpibet||
ākvāthitajalaṃ māsāṃścaturo mātrayā pibet|
śrāvaṇaprabhṛtīneva śeṣānaṣṭa yathāsukham||
abhuktvāmalakaṃ khāded bhuktvā cāpi harītakīm|
pariṇāme ca bhaktasya khādeccāpi vibhītakam||
kaṣāyabhāvācchaleṣmāṇamamlabhāvācca mārutam|
pittaṃ madhurabhāvācca samyagāmalakaṃ jayet||
uṣṇabhāvātkṣaṇādeti pittaṃ cāśayamūrcchitam|
samyaṅnayatyataḥ khādedabhayāṃ bhuktavānnaraḥ||
āhārapariṇāmastu pittaśleṣmavivardhakaḥ|
tasmātsāmyaṃ tayoricchannādadīta vibhītakam||
kālyaṃ vidhiṃ vai kṛtvā ca snāyādṛtusukhairjalaiḥ|
susthaśśucisamācāraḥ sadācārapade sthitaḥ||
mūtramapsu na kurvīta purīṣaṃ ca kadācana|
na niṣṭhīvettathā prājño rakṣañjīvitamātmanaḥ||
na haredviṣamaṃ grīvāṃ na vāpi viṣamaṃ kṣuyāt|
ṛjvāsīno naraḥ khādyamādadīta vicakṣaṇaḥ||
mātaraṃ pitaraṃ bhāryāmācāryaṃ vānupālayet|
abhivādanayogācca vardhayedāyurātmanaḥ||
rājāpathyacarā ye vai ye ca taddrohiṇo narāḥ|
vidharmiṇaścānṛtāśca na tairekībhavetkvacit||
etadapyāhurārogyaṃ dhanyaṃ pūjyaṃ yaśaskaram|
sadbhirācaritaṃ pūrvaṃ vṛttamātreyasammatam|| ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele aṣṭamo'dhyāyaḥ||
||navamo'dhyāyaḥ||
athātaścatuṣpādabhiṣagjitīyaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
bheṣajaṃ hi catuṣpādamāturaṃ kurute'gadam|
yunakti yaśasā vaidyamarthamapyasya yacchati||
tasmādauṣadhayogasya vaidyasyāpyāturasya ca|
upasthātuśca vakṣyāmi sampadaṃ siddhikārikām||
prativātaṃ yathākṣiptaḥ pāṃsumuṣṭirvinaśyati|
tathā hi nāśamāgacchedṛte karma bhiṣakkṛtāt||
nadyāṃ prasyandamānāyāṃ śvabhrasthāne sthaleṣu ca|
praṇaśyati yathā vṛṣṭaṃ tathā duryojitāḥ kriyāḥ||
iṣvāsastu yathā yukto dharmātmā khaḍgapāṇidhṛt|
parebhyaḥ kṣobhamādhatte pātyātmīyāṃśca hantyarīn||
evaṃ cikitsakaḥ pādairbheṣajaiścopabṛṃhitaḥ|
rakṣatyakīrterātmānaṃ vyādhiṃ cāpi niyacchati||
sidhyati pratikurvāṇa ityātreyasya śāsanam|
api cāpratikurvāṇa ityākhyadbhadraśaunakaḥ||
guṇayuktāturadravyabhiṣakparicarānvitā|
dṛśyate viphalā yasmānnāphalā tu viparyaye||
tasmānnaikāntikī siddhiścatuṣpāde cikitsite|
na tvetāṃ buddhimātreyaśśaunakasyānumanyate||
pratikurvati siddhirhi varṇotsāhabalānvitā|
na ca syādvyādhibahutā na tvevāpratikurvati||
durvarṇo durbalaśca syād vyādhibhiścāpyupadrutaḥ|
vikalo vā bhavatyajñairupakrānta ivāturaḥ||
na sā siddhirasiddhiḥ syādyāṃ dṛṣṭvā nābhipadyate|
tasmāt jñānavatāṃ siddhiṃ vindeta matimān bhiṣak||
tatra prathamataḥ pādamauṣadhaṃ tu nibodha me|
khaṃ vāyuranalastoyaṃ bhūmiścaiveha pañcamī||
guṇottarāṇi sarvāṇi yathāsaṃkhyaṃ vibhāvayet|
teṣāntu ye bhūtaguṇāḥ pṛthagbhāvanidarśakāḥ||
parasparasamāyogāddravyeṣveva bhavanti te|
lakṣaṇaṃ khasya sauṣiryaṃ raukṣyaṃ cāpyanilātmakam||
auṣṇyamagnerapāṃ śaityaṃ kharatvaṃ pārthivo guṇaḥ|
yasminyasmin bhavelliṅgametaddravye viśeṣataḥ||
yasya bhūtasya taddravyaṃ tattadātmakamiṣyate|
sauṣiryaṃ lāghavaṃ yatra bhaiṣajyaṃ cāpi khātmakam||
kharatvaṃ ca vivekaśca raukṣyaṃ cāpyanilātmakam|
snehaḥ kledaśca śaityaṃ ca mṛdutvaṃ cāpi vārijam||
paicchilyaṃ gauravaṃ mūrtisthairyaṃ gandhaśca pārthivam|
etairbhūtaguṇairyuktaṃ yaddravyaṃ tattadātmakam||
pañcabhūtātmakaissarvairguṇaistatra samanvitam|
(prativāsaṃ raso gandhastadā praduvanāni ca||
śitamuṣṇodakaṃ caiva raidya cedipriyaṃ rathāḥ|
sajjopakaraṇatvaṃ ca mūlatvakphalasañcayaḥ)||
mūlādīnāṃ sugandhitvaṃ kalyāṇasparśavattathā|
anyūnānatiriktatvarūpavacca praśasyate||
sthalātmatākanimneṣu saikateṣūpaleṣu ca|
suṣiraṃ nātiviṣṭaṃ ca tiryagyātaṃ ca garhitam||
yenauṣadhārthassadyassyāttena cāpratibadhnatā|
bheṣajaṃ prathamaḥ pādaḥ iti jñeyaṃ cikitsite||
pratiśrāvī dvitīyaḥ syāttasya pratyekaśo guṇāḥ|
balavān dakṣiṇo dakṣaḥ priyavāgajugupsakaḥ||
vīraścāpratikūlaśca raktaścitrakathastathā|
prajñāvāṃścāpyupasthāyī tathā śāstrānusārataḥ||
na cātipratibadhnīyātsarveṣvauṣadhakarmasu|
praśasyate kleśasahaḥ pratiśrāvī yathāguṇaḥ||
tṛtīyaścāturaḥ pādaḥ satyāśaṃsī praśasyate|
lakṣaṇaścātmavāṃścaiva kṛtajñassumanāśca yaḥ||
auṣadhasya pratigrāhī na ca rogānnigūhayet|
dṛḍhabhakto'nasūyaśca yaścāpi na viṣīdati||
sarvatrāpratikūlaśca na kriyāmativartate|
ebhirguṇaissamāyuktaṃ jānīyātsādhyamāturam||
bhiṣak caturthaḥ pādastu sasūtrārthaviśāradaḥ|
arogo dṛṣṭakarmā ca kṛtakṛtyeṣu kovidaḥ||
prayoktā ca pragalbhaśca kṣamāvānpratibhānavān|
buddhimāṃstarkakuśalo vīryaudāryabalānvitaḥ||
astabdhaścāpramattaśca satataṃ saṃyatendriyaḥ|
ojasvī cauṣadhajñaśca deśakālaprayogavit||
bhiṣak caturthapādaḥ syādevaṃguṇasamanvitaḥ|
bheṣajaṃ ca pratiśrāvī bhiṣagātura eva ca||
anyonyaguṇasaṃyogādete syussiddhikārakāḥ|
eta eva ca vauguṇyāttasya vyādhivivardhanāḥ||
tasmāccaturṣu pādeṣu cikitsāsiddhiriṣyate|
paktaye kāraṇaṃ pakturyathā pātrendhanānalāḥ||
vijeturvijaye bhūmiścamūḥ praharaṇāni ca|
āturādyāstathā pādāḥ siddhau kāraṇasaṃjñitāḥ||
mṛddaṇḍacakrasūtrādyāḥ kumbhakārādṛte yathā|
nāvahanti guṇān vaidyādṛte pādatrayaṃ tathā|
vidyāttasmāccikitsāyāṃ pradhānaṃ kāraṇaṃ bhiṣak||
ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ
iti bhele navamo'dhyāyaḥ||
||daśamo'dhyāyaḥ||
athāta āmapradoṣīyaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
athāha tailātpiṣṭānnāt kṛsarāt pāyasādapi|
viruddhādhyaśanādāmācchālūkācchuṣkaśākataḥ||
hrīśokakrodhakāmerṣyā lobhodvegādbhayādapi|
anyasmādīdṛśādvāpi rātrau jāgaraṇena vā||
jantośśāmyati kāyāgnirathavā duṣṭaśayyayā|
udveṣṭyante'sya gātrāṇi kaṭipṛṣṭhaṃ ca dūyate||
jaṅghe śūlāyataścāsya jvaraścāsyopajāyate|
śiro guru bhavatyasya tathā netre śarīriṇaḥ||
sodgāraṃ savidāhaṃ ca kaphapittaṃ prasicyate|
stimitaṃ cāsya hṛdayamādhmātamiva codaram||
dṛtiḥ pūrṇaṃ ivānaddhaḥ kṣubhyate doṣasaṃcayāt|
saṃchardayati cābhīkṣṇaṃ mūrchāṃ cāpi niyacchati||
api tṛṣyatyayaṃ gāḍhamāsyaṃ hyasyopaśuṣyati|
svarabhedo'sya bhavati kaṇṭhaśśuṣka ivodgataḥ||
manye ca paridahyete śraddhā cāsya na jāyate|
yadāsya vāyuḥ pittaṃ ca śleṣmā cāpi samudgataḥ||
āmena saṃyutaścaiva sāvaśeṣaḥ pravartate|
sa ubhābhyāṃ tathābhīkṣṇaṃ srotobhyāmatirecitaḥ||
kaphapitte kṣayaṃ prāpte vāte hi kila mūrcchite|
śuddhakoṣṭhe hṛte doṣe śūnyakāye ca mārutaḥ||
ekasthāne tvanāsakto vidhāvati tatastataḥ|
vaṅkṣaṇaṃ hṛdayaṃ nābhivastimarmāṇi cāviśan||
saṃjñāṃ hṛtvaiva kurute muhurmuhuracetanam|
ityetadrūpamāmasya yathāvadupalakṣayet||
ata ūrdhvaṃ pravakṣyāmi jīrṇasyānnasya lakṣaṇam|
madhuraḥ pūrvamudgāro madhye cāmlo yadā bhavet||
paścātsakaṭukaścāpi nirvidāho bhavatyatha|
lāghavaṃ vīkṣyate kāye viśuddhaṃ viśadaṃ mukham||
praguṇaṃ kurute vātamūrdhvaṃ vā yadi vāpyadhaḥ|
bhoktuṃ śrāddhā bhavatyasya rujā cānyopaśāmyati||
ityebhirlakṣaṇairjīrṇaṃ vidyādannaṃ śarīriṇām|
ajīrṇaṃ laṅghayedāme pācanīyastato bhavet||
yavāgvādibhirāhāraissaṃsṛjedvā tamuttaram|
śuṇṭhīprativiṣāmustākvāthaḥ syādāmapācanaḥ||
śuṇṭhīnīrakadhānyākaistulyaiḥ kvātho rucipradaḥ|
mustaṃ hiṅgu trikaṭukaṃ pāṭhā vatsā harītakī||
cūrṇaḥ prativiṣā ceti citrakaścāmanāśanaḥ|
pippalīnāgarakṣārassukhodakasamāyutaḥ||
svedanaṃ kaphavartiśca śūleṣu stimiteṣu ca|
agāradhūmaḥ pippalyo madanaṃ rājasarṣapāḥ|
gomūtrapiṣṭāssaguḍāḥ phalavartiḥ praśasyate||
ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ
iti bhele daśamo'dhyāyaḥ||
||ekādaśo'dhyāyaḥ||
athātassamaśayanaparighanīyaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
āhāraṃ praghasaṃ nyaṇāmupayuktaṃ caturvidham|
pacyamānaṃ dvidhā kāye koṣṭhe rasamalākhyayā||
svedamūtraśakṛdrūpairniṣkrāmati malaḥ punaḥ|
annasya pākatastejo raso nirvartyate nṛṇām||
rasādraktaṃ tato māṃsaṃ māṃsānmedastato'sthi ca|
asthno majjā tataḥ śuklaṃ śuklād garbhasya saṃbhavaḥ||
evaṃ pūrvātparaṃ yāti dhātuṃ dhāturyathākramam|
mātrāpathyaṃ yadā bhuktaṃ rasarūpaṃ gataṃ punaḥ||
kuryānna rogān dīptāgneḥ rasavyāpattisambhavān|
śoṇitādyātmatāṃ gacchet pariṇāmavaśāṃ tadā||
yasmin vyāpadyate dhātau tasmin vyādhīn karotyatha|
viṣūcikāṃ sālasakāṃ pittadāhaṃ vilambikām||
anyedyuṣkaṃ satatakaṃ tṛtīyakacaturthakam|
pittaṃ lohitapittaṃ ca raktārśāṃsi pralepakam||
vipāṭikāṃśca tān vyādhīn rasavyāpattijān viduḥ|
kacchūṃ carmadalaṃ pāmāṃ carmakīlaṃ vicarcikām||
vyaṅgavīsarpakuṣṭhāni raktavyāpattijānviduḥ|
māṃsakīlārbudaṃ gaṇḍamajakāṃ jātulāni ca||
pūtimāṃsālajīścaiva māṃsavyāpattijān viduḥ|
daurgandhyaṃ svedanaṃ sthaulyaṃ pipāsāṃ bahunidratām||
pramehān viṃśatiṃ cāpi medovyāpattijānviduḥ|
dantarogo nakhaśmaśrukeśaromābhipātanam||
ativṛddhistathā vāsthnāmasthivyāpattijān viduḥ|
vidāhaṃ ca madaṃ caiva sonmādaṃ cāpatānakam||
tamodarśanamūrchāyau majjāvyāpattijān viduḥ|
retaḥ kṣārāgnimañjiṣṭhānīlahāridrakaṃ pṛthak||
mehane bhasmavarṇaṃ ca śuklavyāpattijānviduḥ|
narasya strīsvaratvaṃ vā strī vāpi puruṣasvarā||
āmagarbhacyutirvāpi garbhavyāpattijānviduḥ|
ityete āmajā nyaṇāṃ viruddhādhyaśanātmakāḥ|
viruddhāśanavarjī syādetadārogyamuttamam|| ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ| iti bhele ekādaśo'dhyāyaḥ||
||dvādaśo'dhyāyaḥ||
athātaḥ ātreyakhaṇḍakāpyīyaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
ātreyaḥ khaṇḍakāpyaśca maitreyo'nye ca tadvidhāḥ|
rasasaṃkhyākathāṃ cakrurvane caitrarathe dvijāḥ||
tatra kaścidrasaikatvaṃ rasasāmānyato'bravīt|
sātmyāsātmyāttathā dvitvaṃ traividhyaṃ sthānato'pare||
svādurasvāduramṛdurmṛduśceti catuṣṭayam|
mahābhūtavibhāgena pañcetyāhustathāpare||
tadetadevaṃ jānīyādyathoktādeva kāraṇāt|
ṣaṭtvaṃ saptatvamathavā brūyustadrucibhedataḥ||
madhuro lavaṇāmlau ca kaṣāyakaṭutiktakāḥ|
kṣāraśca tān rasān sapta nātirekaṃ samācaret||
rasātisevanājjantussadyo mṛtyumavāpnuyāt|
bhaveyurvyādhayaścāsya rasavyāsaṅgasaṃbhavāḥ||
vṛttarohitarājībhiḥ vṛttākṣaśśakalī tathā|
matsyaścilicimo nāma kūle carati vāriṇaḥ||
taṃ matsyaṃ varjayedekaṃ dugdhenānyāṃstu bhakṣayet|
payasā bhakṣyamāṇastu nararaktaṃ pradūṣayan||
raktavyāpattijānvyādhīn mṛtyuṃ cāpi sa yacchati|
śvāvidvarāhaṃ caikatra payasā parivarjayet||
vanyamūlaphalāhārastadvidhena viruddhyate|
aśnīyātpayasā yastu sadyo mṛtyumavāpnuyāt||
bhaveyurvyādhayaścāsya śaṅkharogagalagrahāḥ|
madhunā gorasenāpi likucaṃ na ca bhakṣayet||
bādhiryaṃ vā'pyathāndhyaṃ vā mṛtyuṃ vā bhakṣayan vrajet|
kapotān sarṣapairbhṛṣṭān payasā na tu bhakṣayet||
majjāvyāpattijān vyādhīnprāpnuyāddhi tathā param|
payasā sarpadaṣṭassan khādyaissiddhyati tādṛśaiḥ||
krimijā vyādhayaścāsya dṛśyante yadi jīvati|
iha yā strī pumāṃścaiva hitajīrṇamitāśanau||
anudāvartinau caiva syātāṃ garbhastayoḥ khalu|
balavān varṇavāṃścaiva cakṣuṣmāṃśca bhavatyatha||
viparyaye vartamānau viparītamiharcchataḥ|
ajīrṇe tvaśanaṃ yasya jīrṇe cānaśanaṃ bhavet||
sa tathobhayathā rogān dāruṇān prāpyānnaraḥ|
tasmājjīrṇe ca pathyāśca mātrāvanto'virodhinaḥ|
sarva eva rasā bhojyāḥ yuktyā saṃvṛtadhātubhiḥ|| ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele dvādaśo'dhyāyaḥ||
||trayodaśo'dhyāyaḥ||
athāto janapadavibhaktīyaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
kasmin janapade rogāḥ ke bhavantyadhikā iti|
gurdālubhekinā pṛṣṭo vyācacakṣe punarvasuḥ||
matsyānnabhojino nityaṃ prācyāḥ syuḥ kaphapittanaḥ|
ślīpadaṃ galagaṇḍaṃ ca prāyaśasteṣu dṛśyate||
nadītoyagatān matsyān bhakṣayanti samudrajān|
prāyaśaḥ kuṣṭhikāstena manuṣyā dakṣiṇādiśi||
masūrayavagodhūmatilakoddālasevinaḥ|
bhūyiṣṭhamarśasāstena kāmbhojādantajāḥ smṛtāḥ||
māṃsakāmāḥ surākāmāḥ strīkāmāssāhasapriyāḥ|
prīcyāstena bhūyiṣṭhaṃ dṛśyante rājayakṣmiṇaḥ||
tīkṣṇoṣṇāni hi bāhlīkāḥ prāyeṇānnāni bhuñjate|
abhiṣyandīni māṃsāni pānakānyaudakāni ca||
prakṛtyā cāpyabhiṣyaṇṇāḥ pārvatopatyakāssadā|
tena bāhlīkadeśeṣu prāyo vyādhirbalāsakaḥ||
meghe'varṣati varṣāsu hemante yatra varṣati|
ṛtuvyāpattisamaye janamāraḥ pravartate||
tatropavāsī dhṛtimān rato viprābhivādane|
mantrauṣadharataścāpi janamārāt pramucyate||
sūryopatāpātsahasā varṣānte pittamīryate|
śaradi prāyaśastena jvaro bhavati dehinām||
gavāṃ ca cāraṇākhyaśca catuṣpācchvāpadeṣu ca|
indrajālaśca matsyeṣu śakunānāṃ pramīlakaḥ||
citrakassarvadhānyeṣu davo mūlaphaleṣu ca|
hastināṃ pākalaścaiva jvara ityabhidhīyate||
utkarṇakastathāśveṣu tejassarvāmayeṣu ca|
sarvatra vasati hyeṣa eṣa cāntaka ucyate||
taṃ dhūmaketuṃ pratyakṣaṃ kṣipraṃ praśamayejjvaram|
asātmyaṃ gandhamādāya vāto yatrātivījyate||
tatra martyeṣu sāmānyaḥ pratiśyāyaḥ pravartate|
tathā batālikā nāma piṭakā cāsya jāyate||
kakṣāyāmūrumūle ca pāṇipādataleṣu ca|
kaṇṭhe vā śrotramāśritya vastau vā hṛdaye'pi vā||
tvaramāṇaścikitseta pravṛddhā mārayennaram|
pittaśleṣmasamutthānā vātaśoṇitmūrcchitā||
batāliketi tāmāhuryatnavāṃstatra jīvati|
ityetatsamyaguddiṣṭaṃ sarvaṃ yadyacca coditam|
ṛṣibhyaḥ paripṛcchadbhyaḥ praśnajātaṃ yathākramam|| ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele trayodaśo'dhyāyaḥ||
||caturdaśo'dhyāyaḥ||
athātaḥ cikitsāprābhṛtīyaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
cikitsāprābhṛto vidvān vidyāt saṃśodhane vidhim|
na ca dhāmārgavaṃ dadyātsnehasvedānupaskṛte||
yo hi saṃśodhanaṃ pītvā sakaṣāyāṇi sevate|
tasya tāni tadā jīvaṃ harantyanu hutāśanam||
dāru śuṣkamivāsvinnaṃ nāmyamānaṃ yathā bhevet|
tathā virecanaṃ hanyātsnehasvedānupaskṛte||
snehasvedopapannaṃ tu charditaṃ bhāvitaṃ punaḥ|
virecanasya siddhyarthaṃ pūrvamāsthāpayed bhiṣak||
ajīryadatha rūkṣaṃ hi virecanamathollikhet|
oṣadhīrhīnavīryāśca mātrāhīnāśca varjayet||
atimātraṃ hi bhaiṣajyaṃ jīvaṃ harati dehinām|
tasmātkālaṃ balaṃ mātrāmṛtuṃ vidyādvirecane||
yathā vṛkṣasya puṣpeṣu palāśeṣu phaleṣu ca|
śīrṇeṣvanyāni rohanti tathā vyādhiranirhṛtaḥ||
yathā mūle tarośchanne mlāyatyagraṃ na saṃśayaḥ|
evaṃ virecite vyādhirupaśāmyati dehinām||
udakaṃ codamanthaṃ ca matsyānmāṃsatilānapi|
guru cānnaṃ na bhuñjīta snehasantarpaṇāni ca||
virecanaṃ tu yaḥ pītvā dāruṇaṃ rūkṣite gude|
dāruṇaṃ bhaktamaśnāti tamāmiṣarasena vai||
tadyathā hyudakaṃ nimnaṃ sthalādadhyavarohati|
snehitasya tathā doṣāḥ kukṣimāyānti sarvaśaḥ||
snehanairvyādhitasthānādrasaiścābhyadhikīkṛtāḥ|
svedairmṛdūkṛtāssantaḥ srotasā ca samāgatāḥ||
mṛdusarvāṅgakoṣṭhasya bheṣajaiśśucibhistadā|
doṣāssunirharā jantorbhavantīti viniścayaḥ||
eṣa heturvirekeṣu chardaneṣveṣa eva tu|
eṣa heturnirūheṣu śirasaśca virecane||
bahusnigdho viriktaśca yo rasairnopapādyate|
bheṣajairapyupastabdhaḥ so'timātraṃ vipadyate||
vakṣyāmi ca kriyāmanyāmajātavyādhināśinīm|
madhu seveta varṣāsu sarpiḥ śaradi cācaret||
sammiśraṃ gaṇḍakenaiva vāruṇīṃ śiśire pibet|
mārdvīkaṃ tu vasante vai nidāghe kṣīramācaret||
kalmāṣā vātalā rūkṣā guravo bhinnavarcasaḥ|
śiraso'ntastarpayantaḥ śleṣmaṇaśca viśoṣaṇāḥ||
udāvartāñjayantyete snigdhatvādgauravādapi|
raukṣyāttridhā sā jananāt śleṣmannākalāstathā||
evaṃ tu vartamānānāmāyurvṛddhimavāpnuyāt|
nyaṇāṃ samādhipūrṇānāmadīnamanasāṃ tathā|| ityāha bhgavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele caturdaśo'dhyāyaḥ||
||pañcadaśo'dhyāyaḥ||
athātastisraiṣaṇīyamadhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
prāṇaiṣaṇā syātprathamā dvitīyā tu dhanaiṣaṇā|
dharmaiṣaṇā tṛtīyā tu puruṣasya bhavatyatha||
tatra prāṇaiṣaṇāṃ pūrvaṃ samāpadyeta mānavaḥ|
dharmārthakāmaprāptirhi sadbhiḥ prāṇaiḥ prapadyate||
dharmadīnāmavāptiśca puruṣārthaḥ paraḥ smṛtaḥ|
tasmāccharīraṃ prayataḥ parirakṣeddhi śāstrataḥ||
dhanaiṣaṇāṃ dvitīyāṃ tu samāpadyeta mānavaḥ|
pāpīyo nāstyataḥ kiñcidyathā jīvannṛte dhanāt||
dharmakāmau na saṃpīḍya tasmādvittamupārjayet|
jvararogādikā yena nirasyedāpado budhaḥ||
dharmaiṣaṇāṃ tṛtīyāṃ tu samāpadyeta mānavaḥ|
avaśyamādhigantavyā dehabhede varā gatiḥ||
prāṇādyanuparodhena tasmāddharmaṃ caredbudhaḥ|
trivargānuparodhena tatprāptirhi nare hitā||
ityetāssammatā loke vyākhyātāstisra eṣaṇāḥ|
yathoktāstāḥ prayuñjānaḥ paratreha ca modate||
upastambhāstu catvāro yadāhāraścaturvidhaḥ|
śarīre ca balaṃ caiva malaṃ tebhyaḥ prajāyate||
śarīramātmano marma malamātmabalaṃ punaḥ|
rogāstasyātivṛddhyāṃ ca vātapittakaphātmakāḥ||
malaṃ tu rogo bhavati vaiṣamyaṃ dhātubhirgataiḥ|
malāccāpyeti vaiṣamyaṃ tasmāttamabhinirharet|| ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele pañcadaśo'dhyāyaḥ||
||ṣoḍaśo'dhyāyaḥ||
athāto vātakalākalīyaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
katamo'bhyadhiko doṣo nyūnaścaiveti tatvataḥ|
kṛṣṇātreyaṃ puraskṛtya kathāścakrurmaharṣayaḥ||
vātaṃ tatrādhikaṃ kaścitprāhānyamupahanti yaḥ|
yāvattiṣṭhati vāto hi dehī tāvattu jīvati||
sa hi garbhāśaye śuklaṃ visraṃsayati yoṣitaḥ|
vātaḥ striyā rajo hanti vandhyā bhavati tena sā||
na striyaḥ puruṣā vāpi vandhyāssantīha janmanā|
āśaye śītalo vāpi vātaśśuṣkendriyastathā||
vātaḥ sadindriyo vāpi vāto hīnendriyastathā|
ālokitaṃ nimiṣitaṃ yacca kiṃcidviceṣṭitam||
aśītiścāmayā nyaṇāṃ nāvātātsaṃbhavanti hi|
vātāddhi kubjāḥ kuṇinaḥ khaṃjā gṛdhrasinastathā||
hīnāṅgā hyadhikāṅgāśca bhavantyanye ca tadvidhā|
sthitiḥ prāṇabhṛtāṃ caiva saritāṃ caiva niḥsvanāḥ||
pṛthivyāścalanaṃ caiva vātādeva pravartate|
vātena dhūmo bhavati dhūmādabhraṃ prajāyate||
abhrādvimucyte vāri bījānāṃ sambhavastataḥ|
ulkānipātaḥ stanitaṃ vidhutārāgaṇasya ca||
vidyutāṃ ca balaṃ caiva vātādeva pravartate|
agnirjvalati vātena puṇyānāṃ haviṣāṃ patiḥ||
sravanti cāpagāstena pṛthivīṃ plāvayanti ca|
vāyustatrādhiko devaḥ prabhavassarvadehinām||
yonyāṃ retaḥ prasiktaṃ ca vāyunā yujyate guṇaiḥ|
eṣa pittaṃ kaphaṃ caiva hanyādanyataraṃ tayoḥ||
ādāya kupito vāyuḥ dehe vipratipadyate|
evaṃ vyānamudānaṃ ca samānaṃ ca pracakṣate||
dehe bahuviśeṣitvādapānaṃ prāṇameva ca|
ūrdhvago'trāpyudānaḥ syād vyānastiryagvivṛddhikṛt||
prāṇo nyasyatyapānaṃ tu pravartayati dehinām|
samāno dhāraṇaścaiva saṃgṛhṇāti sa dehinām||
indriyāṇi manaścaiva bhūtātmānaṃ ca sammataḥ|
viśeṣastvaparo vāyurapāna iti saṃjñitaḥ||
śoṣaṇaścaraṇaścaiva tathā vicaraṇaḥ smṛtaḥ|
vāyuḥ pañcasu bhūteṣu bhūtaṃ paramamucyate||
tāni vāyunibaddhāni vāyussarvatra sarvadā|
mūtrendriyapurīṣāṇāṃ vāyorutsarga eva ca||
pācanāccaiva bhuktasya karmāpānasya śiṣyate|
prāṇaḥ prīṇāti bhūtāni prāṇo jīva iti smṛtaḥ||
vyānāccharīraceṣṭā ca nimeṣonmeṣaṇāni ca|
yaccānyadūrdhvagaṃ jantostadudānasya ceṣṭitam||
kṣavathuṃ hikkitaṃ kāsamucchvāsaṃ bhāṣaṇaṃ tathā|
gudamādhmāpayatyeṣa dehaṃ yassa ca sarvaśaḥ||
eṣu pratiṣṭhito vāyurapāna iti saṃjñitaḥ|
ityebhirīdṛśaiścānyairhetubhistattvadarśibhiḥ|
bhūtebhyo'bhyadhikaṃ vātaṃ lakṣayāma śarīriṇām|| ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele ṣoḍaśo'dhyāyaḥ||
||saptadaśo'dhyāyaḥ||
athāto daśaprāṇāyatanīyaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
caturvidhamatho bhuktaṃ daśadhā prāṇamṛcchati|
ūṣmasvedaśakṛnmūtraistathā vātādibhistribhiḥ||
striyāḥ stanyena śuklena śoṇitena ca vāpyatha|
ityebhirdaśabhiḥ prāṇaḥ sthirībhavati dehinām||
īhate'mṛtavadvṛttiṃ vātapittakaphaistribhiḥ|
āhārasāmyayogāddhi vaiṣamye vyādhayaḥ smṛtāḥ||
iha dvau bhiṣajau vidyāt jñaścaivājñaśca dehinām|
tatra jñaḥ sūtravānyaḥ syātsūtrārthena vicakṣaṇaḥ||
pañcāśrayānkaṣāyāṃśca bhojanaṃ caturāśrayam|
catuḥśreṣṭhāṃstathā snehānāmayāṃścāturāśrayān||
cūrṇapradehānvividhān yavāgūścāṣṭadhā smṛtāḥ|
dvādaśānnāni pathyāni prāyaścittadvayaṃ tathā||
triṃśataṃ copanāhānāṃ sthāneṣvaṣṭāsu tattvataḥ|
sūtrasthānaṃ cikitsā ca triṃśatriṃśadihocyate||
aṣṭau nidānānyuktāni vimānāni tathaiva ca|
śārīrāṇyaṣṭa gātrāṅgapratyaṅgaṃ yatra varṇitam||
siddhayo dvādaśa proktāstathā kalpendriyāṇi ca|
etadviṃśaśataṃ proktaṃ mūlinyo yatra ṣoḍaśa||
ekonaviṃśatirjñeyāḥ phalinyaḥ kṣīriṇītrayam|
āyurjñānamariṣṭibhyaḥ sādhyāsādhyacikitsitāḥ||
rogajñānaṃ nidānebhyo deśakālau vimānataḥ|
śārīrāddhātusaṃghātaṃ prakṛtiṃ dhātusātmyatām||
anyadevaṃvidhaṃ yacca śāstre kiṃcidapi śrutam|
bhiṣagyo vai vijānāti sajñaḥ ajñaṃ tu nibodhata||
yo'nnakāle yavāgūṃ tu sūpakāle tathā rasam|
yūṣakāle'tha māṃsaṃ tu śodhyeṣu stambhanāni ca||
virecanīye vamanaṃ nirūhye cānuvāsanam|
viparyayeṇa yo'nyadvā kurute so'jña ucyate||
yogena vidyāmādadyāttasmāttattvadidṛkṣayā|
yathā tamasi dīpaḥ syāttathā śāstraṃ prakāśakṛt|| ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele saptadaśo'dhyāyaḥ||
||aṣṭādaśo'dhyāyaḥ||
athāto'nnarakṣīyaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
gāndhārabhūmau rājarṣirnagnajitsvargamārgadaḥ|
saṃgṛhya pādau papraccha cāndrabhāgaṃ punarvasum||
na ca strībhyo na cāstrebhyo na bhṛtyebhyo'sti me bhayam|
anyatra viṣayogebhyaḥ so'tra me śaraṇaṃ bhavān||
evamuktastathā tasmai maharṣiḥ pārthivarṣaye|
viṣayogeṣu vijñānaṃ provāca vadatāṃ varaḥ||
viṣeṇa saha saṃsṛṣṭamannapānaṃ caturvidham|
yāni duṣṭāni rūpāṇi tāni me gadataḥ śṛṇu||
pratikūlamivābhāti phenilaṃ viplutākṛti|
śuklaṃ bhavati yatkṣipraṃ nainaṃ śliṣyanti makṣikāḥ||
dhūmaścāsya gataḥ śyāmo raktaḥ kṛṣṇo'tha pītakaḥ|
prādurbhavati tadvidyād duṣṭamannaṃ pracakṣaṇaḥ||
nīlapītāsitā rājyo bhantīndrāyudhopamāḥ|
madhye madyeṣu sarveṣu ye cānye pānasaṃśrayāḥ||
anulepanadoṣe tu tvagasya bahulībhavet|
sarvavyāpattimāpnoti klidyate māṃsameva ca||
mālyaṃ tu śyāvapuṣpaṃ syānmlānaṃ kālaṃ ca dṛśyate|
cūrṇayogāvakīrṇaṃ tu reṇunevāvṛtaṃ bhavet||
romāṇi pariśīryante keśabhūmiśca dhūpyate|
kūrcasparśaśca duḥkhaḥ syājjaṭilācāsya mūrdhajāḥ||
vedanāṃ caiva kaṇḍūṃ ca kurute duṣṭamañjanam|
aśru sravati nātyarthamandhakāraṃ ca paśyati||
dhūmadoṣe virekaḥ syādatyarthaṃ śirasaśca ruk|
paridāhaśca bhedaśca gandhajñānamasādhu ca||
na samyagvetti ca rasānaraṇīva ca mathyate|
iti dhūmasya rūpāṇi sarvāṇyetāni lakṣayet||
duṣṭe vāsasi liṅgāni sūcībhiriva tudyate|
dahyate muñcati svedaṃ mūrchatyapyavalīyate||
duṣṭe tvābharaṇe dāhaḥ svedaḥ kaṇḍūśca jāyate|
kurute gātravaivarṇyaṃ svayaṃ cābhāti niṣprabhaḥ||
duṣṭamāsanamāsthāyī vimūḍho vipatennaraḥ|
sīdatyucchūnavṛṣaṇo na cotthātuṃ sahī bhavet||
ūrū cāsya pravepete sadoṣāsanasevayā|
duṣṭe tu śayane rūpaṃ suptiḥ kaṇḍūyate bhṛśam||
svedaśca viṣamatyeti romakūpaiḥ samantataḥ|
tudyamānaḥ punaḥ śayyāṃ manyate jantubhirvṛtām||
sudine durdine cāpi durdine sudinaṃ tathā|
syād bhūśca duṣṭaparyantā duṣṭacchatraniṣevaṇāt||
kurute rūpavaivarṇyaṃ praduṣṭaśmaśruvardhakaḥ|
śiśire muñcati svedaṃ grīṣme śītāturo bhavet||
pravepate pravyathate gadgadā ca sarasvatī|
syandanau ca vivarṇau ca paruṣau daśanacchadau||
upānahau yadā duṣṭau tadā kaṇḍūḥ prajāyate|
prasvidyate pādatalaṃ tathā cimacimāyate||
atheha viṣapītasya viṣadagdhasya vā punaḥ|
digdhaviddhasya vā rājan rūpāṇīmāni lakṣayet||
gātrodveṣṭanamāpnoti vepate vyathate naraḥ|
kampate cāpi kaṇṭho'sya niḥsvanaṃ ṣṭhīvate muhuḥ||
jihvā cāsya jaḍībhūtā śūlairiva ca tudyate|
vyāhartuṃ ca na śaknoti śarma caiva na vindati||
keśāntaṃ śīryate cāsya nakhāḥ śyāmībhavanti ca|
aṅguṣṭhāṅgulayaḥ kṛṣṇāḥ hastapādatalaiḥ saha||
śarīraṃ pāṇḍutāmeti viṣārtasyaiva dehinaḥ|
so'kṛtyakaratāmeti bhagno ratha ivāhave||
tasmātkuryātkriyāṃ vaidyaḥ ṣoḍaśāgadakovidaḥ|
hriyā yuktaḥ kulīnaśca dharmajño dīrghadarśanaḥ||
arthāścāsya pratiṣṭheyā vidhivatsaṃbhṛtā gṛhe|
cikitsiteṣu yā proktā viṣapraśamane hitā|| ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele'ṣṭādaśodhyāyaḥ||
||ekonaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ||
athāto vidhiśoṇitīyaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
vidhinā śoṇitaṃ jātaṃ sevamānasya vāruṇīm|
praduṣyati vidāhena tato rogāya kalpate||
sarvaṃ madyamacakṣuṣyamapauruṣyaṃ tathaiva ca|
upahanti balaṃ nyaṇāmanāyuṣyaṃ ca kīrtyate||
yastu tadbhajate yuktyā na taṃ vyādhiḥ prasajjate|
atipītaṃ ca tadviddhi tatrāhṛdyaṃ balāya na||
na naktaṃ tu, pibedyuktyā pānaṃ tasmādvicakṣaṇaḥ|
balavarṇakaraṃ hyetadārogyakaraṇaṃ param||
snehapāne tathā mātrāvastikarmaṇi cāpyatha|
kṣāre ca lavaṇe caiva na surā prātiṣidhyate||
bṛṃhaṇaṃ nedṛśaṃ kijcidyathānnasahitā surā|
tāṃ tu rūkṣāṃ viśeṣeṇa nātimātraṃ samācaret||
vātena śleṣmaṇā caiva sampraduṣṭāṃ suśītalā|
viśoṣya dīpayatyeṣā grahaṇīṃ vai śarīriṇām||
yasya koṣṭhagato vāyurduḥkhamutpādayed bhṛśam|
udāvartaṃ sahṛdrogaṃ vātagulmamathāpi ca||
athavā sarvagātreṣu mārutaḥ pratyavasthitaḥ|
yuktāṃ prasannāṃ pibataḥ sa kṣipramupaśāmyati||
hantyāsavaḥ kaphaṃ pittaṃ purīṣaṃ ca bhinattyatha|
madhu pittamariṣṭastu vātārtiṃ śamayatyatha||
vastiḥ śuddhyati pānena śarkarā cāpi bhidyate|
tejo balaṃ ca harṣaśca yuktyā pānena vardhate||
kāmaṃ dīpayate svādurbṛṃhaṇo na vipadyate|
tasmātpānaguṇān buddhvā yathāyogaṃ samācaret|| ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele ekonaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ||
||viṃśo'dhyāyaḥ||
athāto'rthedaśamūlīyaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
artha ityāha hṛdayaṃ tasmin dhamanayo daśa|
ūrdhvaṃ catasro dve tiryak catasraścāpyadhaḥ kramāt||
tābhyo mūlasirāstiryagbhidyante naikadhā sirāḥ|
tasmāttadarthavijñānaṃ smṛtiyogaṃ ca mānasam||
hṛdo raso niḥsarati tasmādeti ca sarvaśaḥ|
sirābhirhṛdayaṃ vaiti tasmāttatprabhavāḥ sirāḥ||
hṛdi prāṇāśca cakrasthāḥ vimāne karṇikā yathā|
marmābhighātaṃ tenātra sahate hṛdaye na tu||
puṇḍarīkasya saṃsthānaṃ kumbhikāyāḥ phalasya ca|
etayoreva varṇaṃ ca vibharti hṛdayaṃ nṛṇām||
yathā hi saṃvṛtaṃ padmaṃ rātrāvahani puṣyati|
hṛttathā saṃvṛtaṃ svapne vivṛtaṃ jāgrataḥ smṛtam||
āpo mahārasā vidyādannaṃ caiva mahauṣadham|
mahāpralepanaṃ tatra tejaścaiva mahatparam||
rasānāmudakaṃ yonirannaṃ vṛttiḥ parā smṛtā|
viklinnamupasandhatte punaḥ kāyaṃ śarīriṇām||
sūryāṃśaṃ dhūmamityāhuryato'bhraprabhavaḥ smṛtaḥ|
abhrādvarṣaṃ tato'nnāni, tasmāttejo mahatparam||
svedaścāsraṃ ca mūtraṃ ca phenaṃ cāmyantaraṃ malam|
avaśyāyahimāmbūni bāhmāni paricakṣate|| hṛdayo ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... |
... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ...
iti bhele viṃśo'dhyāyaḥ||
||ekaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ||
... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... virecyāṃścaiva mānavān|
āsthāpyānanuvāsyāṃśca vātavyādhīṃśca sarvaśaḥ||
virūkṣayedatisnigdhaṃ kṛmikoṣṭhinameva ca|
rūkṣastambhyān pramehāṃśca kapharogāṃśca sarvaśaḥ||
vāmayecchaleṣmakāse ca pratiśyāye ca mānavān|
hṛdroge caiva kuṣṭhe ca sarvaśleṣmāmayeṣu ca||
vorecayeddhi kuṣṭhāni jvarānvisphoṭakāni ca|
dāhaṃ vicarcikāṃ caiva vātavyādhīṃśca sarvaśaḥ||
anvāsayetkaṭīpṛṣṭhapārśvarogān vicakṣaṇaḥ|
udveṣṭanaṃ ca gulmaṃ ca vātavyādhīṃśca sarvaśaḥ||
āsthāpayeddhi gulmāṃśca mūtrāghātodarāṇi ca|
apasmārāṃśca kuṣṭhaṃ ca vātavyādhīṃśca sarvaśaḥ||
svedayeta hanustambhamāyāmamapi cārditam|
pārśvarogaṃ ca gulmaṃ ca vātavyādhīṃśca sarvaśaḥ||
stambhayedraktapittaṃ ca visarpaṃ ca vicakṣaṇaḥ|
dāhaṃ visphoṭakāṃścaiva pittavyādhīṃśca sarvaśaḥ||
iti pittakaphotthānvai vidyādāmāśayātmakān|
pakvāśayasamutthāṃśca vātarogānvinirdiśet||
vāmayedvamanīyāṃstu samīkṣyāmāśayānnarān|
virecayedvirecyāṃstu tathā pakvāśayāśrayān|| ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele ekaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ||
||dvāviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ||
athātaḥ svedādhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
saṃkaraṃ prastaraṃ sekaṃ nāḍīṃ droṇiṃ jalāni ca|
udakoṣṭhaṃ kuṭiṃ caiva svedamaṣṭavidhaṃ viduḥ||
pāṣāṇabhasmasikatātuṣapāṃsubusāni ca|
ajāvigopurīṣāṇi prāvārāṇi pravātikāḥ||
payāṃsyamlāni dhānyāni girisādhāraṇānica|
sarvāṇi madyamāṃsāni lavaṇāni tathaiva ca||
vidhivadyojayetsvede kāleyāgarurūṣitān|
bhiṣag balābalaṃ dṛṣṭvā balino durbalānapi||
kuthakambalakauśeyaiḥ praveṇyajinakādibhiḥ|
kiṭijairāvijairvastraiḥ pracchādya svedayennarān||
sparśasaukhyamavaivarṇyaṃ svedo'ṅgeṣu ca mārdavam|
śītābhinirgamaścaiva samyak svinnasya lakṣaṇam||
pittaprakopo dāhaśca tṛṣṇā mūrchā vilāyanam|
svedadveṣo'ṅgarāgaścetyatisvinnasya lakṣaṇam||
udveṣṭanaṃ dāruṇatā gurutvaṃ stabdhagātratā|
marutaścāprasiddhiśca na tatsvinnasya lakṣaṇam||
asvinne snehanaṃ kṛtvā tataḥ svedairupācaret|
snehapūrvaprayukto hi svedo jayati mārutam||
śuṣkāṇyapi hi kāṣṭhāni snehasvedopapādanaiḥ|
namayanti yathānyāyaṃ kiṃ punarjīvato narān||
atisvinne viśeṣeṇa kārayecchītalāṃ kriyām|
athavā śītalairvātha kṣīraiḥ siṃced ghṛtena vā||
pipāsuṃ vā na śaktaṃ ca garbhiṇīṃ kukṣirogiṇīm|
sarvāṃśca pittajān vyādhīn kaḥ svedaiḥ samupācaret||
ye svedāḥ pūrvamuddiṣṭāstānapyekaikaśaḥ śṛṇu|
bilvāgnimanthaśyonākaṃ kāśmarīṃ pāṭalāṃ tathā||
yavān kulutthān kolāni sthālyāmāsicya sādhayet|
yavapākāni vijñāya sukhoṣmāṇyavatārayet||
tato'bhyaktasya vidhivatsvedanīyasya dehinaḥ|
gātraṃ gātraikadeśaṃ vā sukhoṣṇaiśchādayecchanaiḥ||
tataḥ pracchādayed bhūyaḥ kauśeyājinakāmbalaiḥ|
saṅkarastveṣa vijñeyaḥ svedānāmagrya ucyate||
etānyanyāni ca bhiṣak svedadravyāṇi saṃharet||
prakīrya tāni śayane kalbalenāvaguṇṭhitam|
svabhyaktaṃ śāyayejjantuṃ svedaṃ prastaramādiśet||
vasātailaghṛtakṣīramūtramastvamlakāñjikaiḥ|
sukhauṣṇaiḥ secayetsvedyaṃ sekaḥ sa parikīrtitaḥ||
nāḍīṃ gajakarākārāṃ trihastāṃ tādṛśīṃ tathā|
dhānyāmladravapūrṇāyāṃ dhānyāṃ sandhāya lepayet||
tāṃ trihastāṃ samāhṛtya svabhyaktāṅgasya dehinaḥ|
pracchannagātrasya tu tāṃ nāḍīmudghāṭayed bhiṣak||
nāḍīsvedaḥ samākhyātaḥ yāvatprasvedanāditi|
kaṇṭhāvagāhāmacchidrāṃ tāvadevāyatāṃ samām||
droṇiṃ pāyasaniṣkvāthakṛsarakṣīrapūritām|
kṛtvā tasyāṃ sukhoṣṇāyāṃ svabhyaktaṃ vātarogiṇam||
jñātvāvagāhayettāvadyāvatsvedodgamo bhavet|
taptaiḥ pātrayutairvāpi śuddhairvā salilairbhiṣak||
svamyaktagātrasya tataḥ salilasvedamācaret|
īdṛśaireva salilaiḥ kaṭāhe cārdhapūrite||
praveśya svedayetsvedyamudakoṣṭhaḥ prakīrtitaḥ|
suliptāṃ saṃvṛtadvārāṃ yantritāṃ kārayetkuṭim||
hrasvāgāramitāṃ hṛdyāṃ śilāstīrṇatalāṃ dṛḍhām|
tāsyāścullyau ca kartavye adhastātpārśvasaṃvṛte||
tatra sthālyau pratiṣṭhāpya sarvataḥ paribṛṃhayet|
sthālyau tapte kuṭiścāpi yadā tenoṣmaṇā tadā||
sarvāṅgarogipuruṣaḥ praviśetsa kuṭiṃ tataḥ|
svabhyaktagātraḥ susvinnaḥ kṣaumaprāvṛtalocanaḥ||
tataḥ praveśayettatra śītāmbumaṇikāṃ śubhām|
klāntaḥ śītāmvunā tatra sparśasaukhyamavāpnuyāt||
kuṭisveda iti jñeyaḥ sadyo roganivartakaḥ|
svedo'gnihīnaḥ kṣuttṛṣṇābhayaṃ krodhastathāhavaḥ||
nivātamadhvasantāpau guruprāvaraṇaṃ tathā|
svedyāstu vātakaphajā vātajāḥ kaphajāstathā||
rogāstatroṣṇalavaṇasnigdhāmlaireva vātajāḥ|
karīṣabusapāṣāṇabāṣpāṅgāraiḥ kaphātmakāḥ|
svedyāstu snigdharūkṣābhyāṃ jñātvā vyādhibalābalam|| ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele dvāviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ||
||trayoviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ||
athāto gāḍhapurīṣīyaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
gāḍhaṃ purīṣaṃ grathitaṃ śuskaṃ vātasamīritam|
tenāsyāpihitaṃ srotaḥ śvayathuṃ kurute gude||
tasyaiva kupito vāyurūrdhvaṃ jantoḥ prapadyate|
pārśve śroṇyāṃ ca pṛṣṭhe ca hṛdaye cāvatiṣṭhate||
śiraścāpyavasaṃgṛhya vikārān kurute bahūn|
bādhiryaṃ timiraṃ cāpi pratiśyāyamathāpi ca||
atha pittaṃ kaphaṃ vā svātsthānāccyāvayate'nilaḥ|
tato'sya dhāvato doṣairduṣyanti ca rasādayaḥ||
tato jvaro'sya balavān pāṇḍutvaṃ cāpi jāyate|
śvayathuśca bhavedasya grahaṇī ca praduṣyati||
tasmāddāruṇakoṣṭhastu gāḍhavarcāśca yo bhavet|
rūkṣamannaṃ pariharenna cainamavasraṃsayet||
snigdhānyannāni hṛdyāni māṃsāni vividhāni ca|
kuluttharasayuktāni pradadyāddehine sadā||
āsthāpanaṃ vātaharaṃ tailaṃ cāpyanuvāsanam|
na bhavanti vikārā hi narasya pratikāriṇaḥ||
atha yaḥ kalilaśca syātprajākāmaśca mānavaḥ|
utkṛṣṭatāradāriśca karmahinastathaiva ca||
atha sthūlakṛśaścaiva vaidyamāni ca yo bhavet|
tathaikalokoyatikāṃ motriyānna ca tadvijān||
sabhaṃgān sukumārāṃśca garbhiṇīṃ taruṇi tadā|
durdilāṃśca yadu prāptān kāsinaḥ sāti nāriṇaḥ||
pāṇḍurājaiva karṇaṃ ca satataṃ yaśca kāsati|
pāyayecca bhiṣaktāpairvamanaṃ savirecanam|| ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele trayoviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ||
||pañcaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ||
athātaḥ ṛtuvibhāgīyaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
suśrotā nāma medhāvī cāndrabhāgamuvāca ha|
na pātavyamarogeṇa sukhakāmena recanam||
anīritānāṃ doṣāṇāṃ haraṇaṃ na praśasyate|
avasthitānāṃ cyavanaṃ prāṇāpāyāya me matam||
yathā vyālāgniśastrāṇāṃ sparśaḥ svasthe tathauṣadham|
tasmānna pāyayetsvasthaṃ prakṛtyarthaṃ hi dhīyate||
acākṣuṣatvātkoṣṭhasya pītaṃ svasthena cauṣadham|
yāti naikāntataḥ siddhiṃ tasmātsvasthaṃ na payayet||
ityetadvacanaṃ śrutvā pratyuvāca punarvasuḥ|
iha doṣaviśeṣeṇa naro bhaiṣajyamācaret||
vāte tu bṛṃhaṇaṃ kuryātpitte tu pariśodhanam|
kaphe prarcchadanaṃ sādhu sannipāte virecanam||
vātātapaviśuṣkāśca ye ca karmaparā narāḥ|
adhvayānaviśuṣkāśca tānna saṃśodhayedbhiṣak||
tailamāṣāṃstilaghṛtaṃ phāṇitaṃ piṣṭamodakān|
ājaṃ gavyamathauramraṃ matsyānmāṃāsavaṃ dadhi||
atyaśnano'tipibataḥ sadā ca svapato divā|
doṣā ghorāḥ prakupyanti teṣāṃ sādhu virecanam||
kiñcānyadvidhivatkāle snigdhasvinnena bheṣajam|
mātrāvallaghu pītaṃ hi na kadācidvipadyate||
nācaredatimātraṃ hi bahuśo'pi pibenmṛdu|
kaṣāyeṇa tu dātavyaṃ kaṣāyaiḥ sādhu sidhyati||
yathā hyannamakāle syādviṣaṃ kāle'mṛtaṃ bhavet|
tathauṣadhamakāle syāt viṣaṃ kāle'mṛtaṃ bhavet||
tasmādyadā vai vasudhā tṛptā puṣpavatī bhavet|
tadā prāvṛṣi saṃprāptavamanādīni kārayet||
madhyacārī yadā sūryo dhātūnutkleśayenmṛṇām|
tadā vasante saṃprāpte vamanādīni kārayet||
tadā varṣāsu dātavyaṃ yadā hyeṣa ṛtuḥ sukhaḥ|
ṛtavo yuktaśītoṣṇāḥ praśasyante hi karmasu||
haimantepyanyathā kāryo grīṣme bhavati cānyathā|
varṣāsu cāpyanyathā ca durdine cāpyato'nyathā||
tasmiṃstasmiṃśca kāle vai bhavetkāryo'nyathānyathā|
sātmyāsātmyaṃ manuṣyāṇāmanyathā parivartate||
tryahaṃ tu dāpayet snehaṃ deye snehavirecane|
nātiśīte na cātyuṣṇe peyaṃ snehavirecanam||
tṛṣṇābādhā bhavatyuṣṇe śīte tu vyapalīyate|
atha grīṣme tu santaptairdhātubhirdehino bhavet||
atiyogaḥ pipāsā ca tadā tasmānna dāpayet|
hemante cāpi tadvīryāddoṣā dāruṇatāṃ gatāḥ||
bhavantyakāle snehasya tadāpyatha na dāpayet|
iha bhaiṣajyamuṣṇaṃ hi vikriyāmeti kālataḥ||
mandī bhavati vaitattu grīṣme taikṣṇyaṃ niyacchati|
yanmaithunācchuklavāhā vastisakthitrikādi ca||
hṛdayaṃ durbalaṃ cāsya samudīrṇaśca mārutaḥ|
brahmacārī bhavettasmāt paittikān bhajate'nyathā||
mukhapākaṃ madaṃ mūrchāṃ pralāpaṃ pākavibhramau|
dāhaṃ pipāsāṃ santāpaṃ vaktrasyākṣṇośca pītatām||
dhūmābhidūṣite pittavikārā ye prakīrtitāḥ|
bhramaḥ klamaḥ pramohaśca mado mūrcchā vikampanam||
santāpo'bhyantare caiva rasasthānagate smṛtāḥ|
aruṣāṃ vivṛtānāṃ vā sphoṭānāṃ vā prapācanam||
raktasthānagate vidyātpitte vaivarṇyameva ca|
māṃsasthānagate svedaḥ sadanaṃ copajāyate||
medodhātugate vidyāt plāvanaṃ pītamūtratā|
asthimajjagate śoṣo nakhadantavivarṇate||
meḍhremuṣkaparītāpaḥ klaibyaṃ vāpyatha śuklage|
balavadvigrahād bhārāt pīḍanāllaṅghanādapi||
maithunasyātiyogācca jāyate manasaḥ klamaḥ|
trāsodvegātpratīghātādrūkṣapānānnasevanāt||
ityebhirīdṛśaiścānyairhetubhiḥ kupito'nilaḥ|
vikārāñjanayecchavāsakāsaviṣṭambhasaṃjñakān||
śūlavepanamādhmānaṃ nistodaṃ suptatāṃ tathā|
sphoṭanaṃ cālanaṃ raukṣyaṃ veṣṭanotkṣepaṇāni ca||
śoṣaṇaṃ kārṣṇyaviṣyandau viśleṣastu paraḥ smṛtaḥ|
dvividhā vyādhayaḥ proktāḥ śarīre vātasaṃbhavāḥ||
koṣṭhānusāriṇastatra jñeyāḥ sarvāṅgarogiṇaḥ|
pakṣāṅgarogiṇaścaiva jñeyāḥ sarvāṅgasāriṇaḥ||
śūlavepanasaṃkocagātrastambhaiśca pīḍitāḥ|
asthigrahagṛhītāśca sarve sarvāṅgarogiṇaḥ||
tatra pādatalāṅguṣṭhajaṅghājānūruvaṃkṣaṇaiḥ|
gudameḍhrakaṭīpṛṣṭhayonigarbhāśayairapi||
yakṛtplīhāṃsahṛdayapārśvavakṣobhujāntaraiḥ|
grīvāgaṇḍaharūśaṅkhanāsākṣidaśanacchadaiḥ||
lalāṭakarṇasīmantaiḥ pratyekamanilārditaiḥ|
dūyadbhirvikalaiścāpi jñeyāstvekāṅgarogiṇaḥ||
ityetanmūlamuddiṣṭaṃ rogāṇāṃ ca yathāvidhi|
bhūyastvidamadhiṣṭhānaṃ pratirogaṃ pratīkṣata|| ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele pañcaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ||
||ṣaḍviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ||
athātaḥ aṣṭodarīyaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
athāṣṭāvudarāṇīha mūtrāghātāṃśca nirdiśet|
ṣaṭ kācāṃlliṅganāśāṃśca jānīyāttimirāṇi ca||
hikkāśvāsāṃśca kāsāṃśca khālityaṃ palitāni ca|
śirorogārbudān gulmān chardīnatha bhagandarān||
unmādānmukharogāṃśca sthānānyanaśanasya ca|
granthīṃśca pañca pañcaiva kuṣṭhānyaṣṭau vinirdiśet||
hṛdrogān pāṇḍurogāṃśca sāsrāvān śvayathūṃstathā|
abhiṣyandānadhīmanthānklībasthānāni cāpyatha||
akṣirogānapasmārān raktarohiṇikāṃ tathā|
pipāsāścopadaṃśāṃśca catuṣkāṇi vinirdiśet||
pṛṣṭharogajvarārśāṃsi raktapittavraṇau tathā|
dvau dvau tisrastu vidradhyastvalajyaḥ śoṇitāni ca||
retasāṃ ślīpadaṃ cārma sapta sapta vinirdiśet|
pramehān kṛmijātīśca yonidoṣāṃśca viṃśatim||
tatrodarāṇyathāṣṭau tu vyākhyātāni cikitsite|
nidānenaiva rogāstu ye noktāstānnibodha me||
aruṇā nīlapītāśca haritaśvetalohitāḥ|
kācaṃ ca liṅganāśaṃ ca timirāṇi ca nirdiśet||
vātaḥ pittaṃ kapho vāpi yasya śīrṣaṃ vidhāvati|
vidahanneṣa mūlāni srotāṃsi samabhidrutaḥ||
adhvanā vātibhārādvā dehināṃ vārdhakena vā|
khālityaṃ palitaṃ vāpi pañcadhaiva bhavatyatha||
vātajaṃ pittajaṃ caiva śleṣmajaṃ māṃsajaṃ tathā|
arbudaṃ medasā caiva pañcamaṃ nirdiśetpṛthak||
vātajaṃ pittajaṃ caiva śleṣmajaṃ sannipātajam|
āgantukaṃ pañcamaṃ ca mukharogaṃ pracakṣate||
uṣṭragrīvaṃ parisrāvi śambūkāvartameva ca|
unmārgaṃ śatayoniṃ ca tadvadvidyādbhagandaram||
chardayatyatha vātena pittena ca kaphena ca|
āhārādamanojñācca sannipātācca pañcamam||
praticchanne tu hṛdaye vātapittakaphairnaraḥ|
arasādamanojñācca bhojyamannaṃ na sevate||
vātajaṃ pittajaṃ caiva śleṣmajaṃ sannipātajam|
sirāgranthiṃ vijānīyātpañcamaṃ snāyuto bhiṣak||
vātajaṃ pittajaṃ caiv kaphajaṃ sannipātajam|
abhiṣyandaṃ vijānīyādadhimanthaṃ ca buddhimān||
śukloparodhāddaurbalyād dhvajabhaṅgāttathaiva ca|
śukrakṣayācca catvāri klaibyasthānāni nirdiśet||
vātajaṃ pittjaṃ caiva śleṣmajaṃ sannipātajam|
akṣirogaṃ vijānīyānmānavānāṃ caturvidham||
pūyānmūtrācca śuklācca sandhānāccāpi dehinām|
upadaṃśaṃ vijānīyādbhiṣagevaṃ caturvidham||
raktāsrāvaṃ kaphasrāvaṃ pūyāsrāvaṃ tathaiva ca|
toyāsrāvaṃ ca jānīyādbhiṣagevaṃ caturvidham||
vātajāṃ pittajāṃ caiva śleṣmajāṃ sannipātajām|
pipāsāmapi jānīyādbhiṣagevaṃ caturvidhām||
vātajāṃ pittajāṃ caiva śleṣmajāṃ sannpātajām|
vidyādrohiṇikāṃ caiva bhiṣagevaṃ caturvidhām||
māṃsārma tvatha medo'rma prastāryarma tathaiva ca|
snāyavarma pravijānīyād bhiṣagevaṃ caturvidham||
dvau jvarau dāhaśītau tu śārīrāgantujavraṇau|
śuṣkārśaḥ śoṇitārśaśca tathā dve cārśasī viduḥ||
pṛṣṭharogāvupakṣārikṣāriṇau ca vinirdiśet|
bāhyāyāmāntarāyāmau vidyādākṣepakau tathā||
medasaḥ snāyuto māṃsādalajyastisra eva ca|
vātātpittātkaphāccaiva tisro vidradhayaḥ smṛtāḥ||
varāhākāraṇaṃ caiva raktaṃ kāṃsyavakaṃ tathā|
vātātpittātkaphānmāṃsānmedasaḥ snāyuto'pi ca||
sirājamarma jānīyātsaptamaṃ dehināṃ bhiṣak|
sapta sātmyāni jānīyāttatra ṣaṭ syuryathā rasaiḥ||
sarvasātmyaṃ yadeva syādvaraṃ sātmyaṃ taducyate|
mṛjjā jarāyujā levā rūpakā bahurūpakāḥ||
parisarpā visarpāśca gātrajā netrajāstathā|
romadā rohitāścaiva kaṣṭā dāruṇakāstathā||
śirojā dantajāścaiva śleṣmajāḥ śakṛdāśrayāḥ|
lohitāḥ kālakāścaiva tathaiva śatamātṛkāḥ||
vidyādviṃśatirityetāḥ kṛmijātīśca dehinām|
yathā patatriḥ śīghro'pi svāṃ chāyāṃ nātivartate|
vātādernātivartante bahavo'pi tathā''āmayāḥ|| ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele ṣaḍviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ||
||saptaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ||
athāto'nnapānavidhīyaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
athodakaṃ kledayati lavaṇaṃ syandayatyapi|
atha pācayati kṣāraḥ kṣīraṃ vai jīvayatyapi||
snehayatyatha sarpiśca piśitaṃ bṛṃhayatyapi|
māṣā bahumalāścāpi ślīpadaṃ janayeddadhi||
tvacaṃ prasādayettailaṃ snehanāya ca kalpate|
piṇyākaḥ kurute glāniṃ takraṃ ca kṣapayatyapi||
madhu cāpyatha sandhatte jarjarīkurute surā|
hareṇubhirmasūraiśca bhṛśaṃ vāyuḥ prakupyati||
kaphakṛnmūlakaṃ vidyātsarṣapaṃ vātakopanam|
laśunaṃ vātaśamanaṃ pittaśleṣmakaraṃ ca tat||
nātiśleṣmakaraṃ hiṅgu ṣaṇḍyaṃ kṣāraḥ karoti ca|
sarvaṃ tiktamavṛṣyaṃ tu vidyādanyatra kūlakāt||
avṛṣyaṃ paiṣṭikaṃ sarvaṃ yavagodhūmavarjitam|
dāḍimāmalakādanyatsarvamamlaṃ tu pittalam||
yavakān dāruṇānvidyātpṛthukānśāliṣaṣṭikān|
nirdiśenmṛgatulyāṃstu tathānūpānmṛgadvijān||
tathaikāntahitāṃścāpi jāṃgalānmṛgapakṣiṇaḥ|
ityetatsaṃgraheṇoktaṃ bhojyaṃ vistarataḥ śṛṇu||
pippalī śṛṅgiberaṃ ca palāṇḍulaśunāni ca|
mūlakaṃ kṛśṇagandhā ca parṇāsaṃ kālamānakam||
surasaṃ sumukhaṃ caiva caṭakaṃ ca phaṇijjakam|
rasena kaṭukaṃ vidyāt vipāke madhuraṃ tathā||
kirātatikto nimbaśca ketukāśakaṭhillakāḥ|
kūlakaṃ ca savetrāgraṃ tiktaṃ madhuvipāki ca||
plakṣakāśmaryamadhukaṃ svādu pāke parūṣakam|
kharjūraṃ nālikeraṃ ca priyālaṃ tadvidhāni ca||
madhukaṃ ca bisaṃ caiva jīvanīyāni yāni ca|
rase pāke ca madhuraṃ tadvidyād vividhaṃ ca yat||
basāni kaṭukā bimbī sarvaṃ caivekṣuvaikṛtam|
rase pāke ca madhuraṃ tadvidyāttrividhaṃ ca yat||
nyaṅkuṃ carasamudrāṃśca sṛmarānkhaḍgasūkarān|
anyāṃścānūpajān vidyānmadhurān rasapākataḥ||
kādambānsaindhavānhaṃsānsārasāñścetavāraṇam|
kācākṣānmallikākṣāṃśca tathā puṣkarasāyakān||
dipavepni śarārīṃśca vikramān sumanāmukhān|
nandimaudgalyakāṃścāpi kālakān kṛṣṇapucchakān||
ājñātān ḍuṇḍubhāṃścaiva maṇḍūkān chuṭumallikān|
harṣyāttu saptavārādi tathā garbhabhatāsakān||
sarvāṃśca vārijānvidyānmadhurān rasapākataḥ|
priyaṅguśca plāṅgaśca balākāśca khagāstathā||
ye cānye'pyaudakā bhaumyāḥ khecarāśca khagāḥ smṛtāḥ|
nakrāṃśca śiśumārāṃśca vallakīmatsyakacchapān||
anyāṃśca vārijānvidyānmadhurānrasapākataḥ|
priyaṅguśca vinaktāśca nīlikāśāliṣaṣṭikāḥ||
parūṣakāśca nīvārāḥ kodravā brahmabhūtikāḥ|
ityetaddhānyajātaṃ vai yaccānyadapi tadvidham||
rasena madhuraṃ tadvai vipāke kaṭukaṃ viduḥ|
mudgān masūrāṃścaṇakān kulutthāṃśca kalāyakān||
rasena madhurāṃstānvai vipāke kaṭukānviduḥ|
rohīṃ śvadaṃṣṭrānuṣṭrāṃśca śarabhānmṛgamātṛkān||
athānyānmadhurāsvādānvipāke kaṭukānviduḥ|
kharāścāścatarāmeṣā ye cānye tādṛśā mṛgāḥ||
rasena madhurāṃstānvai vipāke kaṭukānviduḥ|
harītān stokakāṃścaiva kapotān śukaśārikāḥ||
caṭakān bhṛṅgarājāṃśca tathā pippalakānapi|
godhāputrāṃstailapākānye cānye pratudāḥ khagāḥ||
ye cānye madhurāstānvai vipāke kaṭukānviduḥ|
anupānāni vakṣyāmi yeṣu yāni hitāni vai||
āje kaṣāyaśītastu hāyane codakaṃ tathā|
anupānaṃ vidhātavyaṃ vārāhe khadirodaram||
dadhi cukraṃ ca dhānyānāmanupānaṃ pradāpayet|
śīrodakaṃ bisagranthisevanānāṃ tathāsavān||
tadvārijānāṃ satvānāmanupānaṃ pradāpayet|
triphalāṃ śīdhumārdvīkaṃ kadambapanasodakam||
suradrujambūplakṣāṇāṃ phalaskandhāsavānpṛthak|
mudgādisūpadhānyānāmanupānaṃ pradāpayet||
bilvapiṇḍakakalkānāṃ kapitthasyāthavāsavān|
guhāśayānāṃ satvānāmanupānaṃ pradāpayet||
nyagrodhādigaṇasyātha skandhaśākhāphalāsavān|
viṣkirāṇāṃ vihaṅgānāmanupānaṃ pradāpayet||
kakubhasyānupānaṃ tu snigdhānāṃ cāpi vāruṇīm|
caturvidhasya snehasya toyamuṣṇaṃ pradāpayet||
drākṣākāśmaryakharjūrayavavāṭyāṃstathāsavān|
aśvagandhakaṣāyaṃ tu vāruṇīmaṇḍameva ca||
sukhena pācayatyannaṃ rocayatyapakarṣati|
anupānaṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ sātmyatāṃ ca prayacchati|| ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele saptaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ||
||aṣṭāviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ||
athāto bhojanavidhīyaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
yad bhakṣayati bhuṅkte vā vidhivaccāpi mānavaḥ|
anyacca kiñcitpibati tatsarvaṃ ṣaḍrasānvitam||
pathyāpathyamabodhāryaṃ, tasmādbhojyaṃ śarīriṇām|
rasato vīryataścaiva pravakṣyāmyata uttaram||
śālayo madhurāḥ sarve vipāke kaṭukāḥ smṛtāḥ|
raktaśālirvarasteṣāmaraukṣyāllekhano na ca||
dīrghaśūko mahā śāliḥ sugandhiḥ śālikāmukhaḥ|
kṛṣṇavrīhiḥ kāñcanakaḥ lopārkṣaḥ śītabhīrukaḥ||
hāyano lodhraśāliśca raktaśāliguṇānvitaḥ|
pūrvapūrvo laghutaraḥ pathyaḥ kiṃca bhavatyataḥ||
sugandhakā gandhavanto gandhena madayantyatha|
raukṣyācca kalamaṃ vidyādadhikaṃ vai viśeṣataḥ||
kaṣāyastvadhikaḥ kiṃcit ṣaṣṭikāḥ śālibhiḥ samāḥ|
mahādoṣāḥ suguravaḥ pāṭalāvrīhayastathā||
yavakā nityamahitāḥ kṛṣṇavrīhipramodakāḥ|
vīryoṣṇā madhurāste vai sakaṣāyāśca kīrtitāḥ||
bahūrūkṣāḥ kaṣāyāśca koradūṣāḥ savāruṇāḥ|
prasādhikāḥ kaṃ gavaśca śyāmākāśca samā rasaiḥ||
uddālakāstu saṃkhyā tāḥ kaṣāyā madhurā rase|
grīṣme tu sevitāste vai kopayantyāśu mārutān||
yavāstvatiyavāścaiva godhūmāśca samā rase|
madhurāḥ sakaṣāyāśca śleṣmalā lohitā nṛṇām||
atha veṇuyavā nātikaṣāyā madhurā rase|
kaphaṃ pittaṃ ca medaśca kopayantyatisevitāḥ||
kṛṣṇā mudgā mahāmudgāḥ haritāścirajīvinaḥ|
makuṣṭhakāśca te'pyuktāḥ kaṣāyā madhurā rase||
hareṇavo masūrāśca satīnāśca tathāḍhakī|
kulutthāśca viseṣeṇa kaṣāyamadhurā rase||
sarve tu lavaṇakṣārā dīpanīyāḥ prakīrtitāḥ|
avṛṣyāśca viśeṣeṇa durbalānāṃ hitā na ca||
śvadaṃṣṭrā yāvanālaśca dvau kṣārau śītalau smṛtau|
śuklaṃ vinihatastau tu dṛṣṭiṃ cāpi śarīriṇām||
navanītaṃ tathāmlaṃ tu ghṛtaṃ vai madhuraṃ smṛtam|
sarvaṃ dugdhamabhiṣyandi gavyaṃ tebhyo viśiṣyate||
vājī bhavati dugdhena balaṃ cātyupajāyate|
sañjīvanaṃ sambhavati sarvaṃ kṣīramudāhṛtam||
rase pāke ca madhuraṃ tailaṃ sarvaṃ prakīrtītam|
yasminyasminviśeṣastu yo yastasminnibodha tam||
tailameraṇḍabilvānāmuṣṇaṃ vātaharaṃ smṛtam|
ityuktānyuṣṇavīryāṇi ya danyacchītameva tat||
vibhītakānāṃ tailaṃ tu kaṣāyamadhuraṃ rase|
kaphapittaharaṃ proktaṃ keśyaṃ caiva śarīriṇām||
ataḥ karañjabījānāṃ vijñeyāni tathaiva ca|
tailāni rasato vidyāttiktāni madhurāṇi ca||
pittaśleṣmakaraṃ tiktamiṅgudītailamucyate|
sakaṣāyaṃ madhūkānāṃ kaphapittānilāpaham||
kaulakaṃ madhurasparśaṃ rasato'tha vipākataḥ|
yasminyasminviśeṣastu yo yastaṃ me nibodhata||
palālajātaṃ chvākamatīva guru sammatam|
mahādoṣamabhiṣyandi sukhaṃ bāhyopaghātakam||
chatrākaṃ gomayodbhūtaṃ kaṣāyamadhuraṃ rase|
kaṣāyaṃ veṇucchatrākaṃ mārutaṃ kopayennṛṇām||
sarvaṃ tu śākaṃ madhuraṃ rasato'tha vipākataḥ|
yasminyasminviśeṣastu yo yastasminnibodha tam||
mārutaṃ kopayennṛṛṇāṃ viṣṭambhitvādalāvukam|
śleṣmāṇaṃ vardhayeccāpi vipākena balīyasā||
kūṣmāṇḍaṃ śleṣmalaṃ vidyānnavaṃ śaradi bhakṣitam|
tadalpadoṣaṃ bhavati parisaṃvatsaroṣitam||
kaṣāyamadhuraṃ vidyātkhādiraṃ cātisāri ca|
tiktaṃ dīpanasaṃsṛṣṭaṃ nirdoṣaṃ taccharīriṇām||
kośātakī karīrāṇāṃ śatāvaryāśca tiktakam|
kaṣāyaṃ nālikāyāśca tiktaḥ puṣkaravartakaḥ||
vetrāgraṃ tiktakaṃ nimbaṃ tilaśākaṃ ca śītikā|
kurūṭikā kunaṭikā tathā gandharvahastakaḥ||
tathā nāḍīkalāyaśca pañcāṅgulyastathaiva ca|
etāni vidyācchākāni tiktāni rasapākataḥ||
sa tīnaśākaṃ madhuramīṣatiktopasaṃhitam|
kovidārasya śākaṃ tu pūrveṇaiva samaṃ matam||
vārtākīnāṃ phalaṃ tiktaṃ kaṭukenopasaṃhitam|
grahaṇīṃ dīpayatyetacchaleṣmāṇaṃ vinihanti ca||
kaṭhillabakulau caiva madhurau śukraśo dhanau|
hatastandrāṃ ca vātaṃ ca nityameva viṣūcikām||
alambusaḥ sakaṭukastiktako vātakopanaḥ|
bhinatti kaphasaṅghātaṃ viṣṭabhyātha vipacyate||
lāṅgaśākaṃ tu madhuraṃ lavaṇenopasaṃhitam|
kaṭukaṃ bahudoṣaṃ ca tathā pakṣanikācanam||
kaṭuko dīpanīyaśca viṣahā svastiko rase|
kaṣāyamadhurā cillī cuñcurmadhuraśītalaḥ||
bhārṅgī māṣaśca madhurau rasataḥ parikīrtitau|
mudgaśākaṃ satiktaṃ tu sakṣāraścaiva vāstukaḥ||
niṣpāvaśākaṃ madhuraṃ māṣaśākaṃ tathā smṛtam|
candrākaśākamamlaṃ tu kaṣāyastu gavedhukaḥ||
tunnakaṃ prapunāṭaśca jīvantī suniṣaṇṇakam|
madhurāṇyalpadoṣāṇi tathaiva tu kuṭiñjakaḥ||
piṇḍāluśākaṃ ca navaṃ tuṇḍikeraphalāni ca|
madhurāṇyalpadoṣāṇi saṃsṛṣṭakaṭukāni ca||
upodakā tu madhurā lavaṇenopasaṃhitā|
ityetannirdiśecchākaṃ yadanyadapi tadvidham||
ājaṃ gavyamathaurabhraṃ vārāhaṃ caiva yatsmṛtam|
hastimāṃsaṃ ca madhuramuṣṭraṃ guru ca nirdiśet||
vidyādevaṃguṇaṃ caiva sakaṣāyaṃ ca māhiṣam|
khaḍgamāṃsamabhiṣyandi sasvādulavaṇaṃ rase||
kutamāyurmayūraśca gokarṇo gavayo guruḥ|
sṛmaraścamaro nyaṅkurmadhurā guravaśca te||
ṛśyaśca mṛgamātā ca tuṅgaśca hariṇī mṛgaḥ|
pārāvata...............................||
iti bhele aṣṭāviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ||
iti bhelasaṃhitāyāṃ sūtrasthānaṃ samāptam||
<2>
urovighātāttasyātha jvaraḥ kāsaśca jāyate|
svaraḥ sīdati cāpyasya niṣṭhīvati saśoṇitam||
athavāpyavaśo jantuḥ śaśvatsa parihīyate|
ityetairlakṣaṇairvidyātsāhasaprabhavaṃ kṣayam||
yāvatsa balavāneva bṛṃhayettāvadeva tam|
yasmād balasamāveśaṃ puruṣasyeha jīvitam||
sa yadā gurumadhye vāpyathavā rājasaṃsadi|
goṣṭhe strīdyūtamadhye vā hastipṛṣṭhe'thavā rathe||
bhayātprasaṅgācca hriyā ghṛṇitvādvāpi mānavaḥ|
prāptapātaṃ purīṣaṃ vā mūtraṃ vāpi ruṇaddhi yaḥ||
tasya sandhāraṇādvāyurūrdhvabhāge samīritaḥ|
uraḥśūlaṃ pārśvaśūlaṃ gulmaṃ ca janayatyatha||
gulmajanmanimittaśca jvaraḥ kāsaśca jāyate|
svaraḥ prabhidyate cāsya niṣṭhīvati sapūtikam||
athavāpyavaśo jantuḥ śaśvatsa parihīyate|
ityebhirlakṣaṇairvidyātsandhāraṇakṛtaṃ kṣayam||
ātmārthaṃ lajjate janturgurorvā strījanasya ca|
tasmādātmārthameveha prāptaṃ vegaṃ na dhārayet||
sa yadā durbalo jantuḥ svalpāhāraḥ kṛśo'pi vā|
rūkṣabhojī viśeṣeṇa striyo yaścātisevate||
saraktaṃ kurute mūtraṃ jantuḥ śuklaparikṣayāt|
retaḥsthānaṃ ca suṣiraṃ vāyurasya pradhāvati||
tasya vātābhibhūtasya jvaraḥ kāsaśca jāyate|
svaraḥ sīdati cāpyasya niṣṭhīvati saśoṇitam||
athavāpyavaśo jantuḥ śaśvatsa parihīyate|
ityebhirlakṣaśairvidyādatimaithunajaṃ kṣayam||
ratimūlaṃ śarīraṃ hi śarīrasya ratiḥ phalam|
tasmātphalārthī mūlārthaṃ striyaḥ seveta yuktitaḥ||
sa yadā durbalo jantuḥ sevate viṣamāśanam|
bhuñjānasyāsya viṣamaṃ vaiṣamyaṃ yānti dhātavaḥ||
tataḥ purīṣameveha vardhayatyasya bhojanam|
nāvāpnoti rasaṃ dehe vikṛtasyeva dehinaḥ||
rase nivṛtte tasyātha jvaraḥ kāsaśca jāyate|
svaraḥ sīdati cāpyasya niṣṭhīvati saśoṇitam||
athavāpyavaśo jantuḥ śaśvatsa parihīyate|
ityebhirlakṣaṇairvidyādviṣamāśanajaṃ kṣayam||
yasmādarthī śarīrārthaṃ rasabhojanamicchati|
śarīrāpekṣayā tasmādāhāraṃ susamācaret||
iti catvāri śoṣāṇāmuktānyāyatanāni me|
yāni buddhvā pariharedārogyārthī pumāniha|
buddhau cārogyamāyattamidamuktaṃ maharṣiṇā|| ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele nidāne dvitīyo'dhyāyaḥ||
||tṛtīyo'dhyāyaḥ||
athāto gulmanidānaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
vātātpittātkaphāccaiva nicayādatha lohitāt|
pañca gulmā bhavantīha teṣāṃ vakṣyāmi lakṣaṇam||
sa yadā vātalo janturvātalaṃ bhajate'śanam|
dhāvati plavate vāpi rātrau jāgarti vā punaḥ||
atibrūte'tihasati striyo vātiniṣevate|
udāvartayate vāpi karma cātiniṣevate||
vṛkṣaprapatanādvāpi chardayatyathavā balāt|
tasyaivaṃ kupito vāyurāmāśayamupāgataḥ||
pārśvayorhṛdi kukṣau vā gulmaṃ saṃjanayatyatha|
sa sakṛdbhavati sthūlaḥ punarbhavati vāpyaṇuḥ||
todasphuraṇasaṃyukto vidhyate ca vidhāvati|
vedanāṃ janayatyeṣa jvaraṃ sañjanayatyapi||
vastiśīrṣaṃ ca saṃgṛhya dārayanniva tiṣṭhati|
viṣaṃ bhavati cāhāro mūrdhānaṃ pratipadyate||
punaśca dṛśyate vyaktaṃ punarnaśyati cāpyatha|
karoti gāḍhaṃ duḥkhena purīṣaṃ vā saśoṇitam||
kṛṣṇābhāsaśca puruṣo vātagulmī sa dṛśyate|
yassadā pittalo jantuḥ pittalaṃ bhajate'śanam||
apramāṇena durmedhāḥ sevate pānamīdṛśam|
tasyaivaṃ kupitaṃ pittamāmāśayamupāgatam||
pārśvayorhṛdi kukṣau vā gulmaṃ sañjanayatyatha|
uṣyate dūyate cāpi dahyate dhūpyate tathā||
nityaṃ taptaśca raktaṃ ca svedaṃ muñcatyabhīkṣṇaśaḥ|
ayogolo yathā taptastathā sthānagate dahet||
tṛṣṇāṃ mūrcchāṃ ca janayetsthānādapi visarpati|
vedanā paramā caiva tasmin sthāne prajāyate||
purā jātāni lomāni tasmādgulmaparigrahāt|
cyavante na ca jāyante yāvadvyādhirna śāmyati||
pittagulmena puruṣaḥ pītābhāsaśca lakṣyate|
yassadā śleṣmalo jantuḥ śleṣmalaṃ bhajate'śanam||
apramāṇena durmedhā divāsvapnaratastathā|
tasyaivaṃ kupitaḥ śleṣmā hyāmāśayamupāgataḥ||
pārśvayorhṛdi kukṣau vā gulmaṃ sañjanayatyatha|
na cāsya svadate bhojyaṃ bhuktaṃ na ca vipacyate||
śleṣmaṇā saha bhuktaṃ ca muhurūrdhvaṃ prapadyate|
śuklamūtrapurīṣaśca śuklābhāsastathaiva ca||
śuklanetraśca bhavati gulme śleṣmasamudbhave|
yassadā karśito janturvyādhinā bheṣajena vā||
asañjātabalāgniśca doṣalānyupasevate|
tasya sannicitā doṣā gulmaṃ kurvanti dāruṇam||
tatra sarvāṇi rūpāṇi dṛśyante sānnipātike|
atha lohitagulmastu strīṇāmeva prajāyate||
nāsau bhavati nṛṛṇāṃ tu tasya vakṣyāmi lakṣaṇam|
acirapracyute garbhe sūtikāyāstathā'cirāt||
atiprajananādvāpi tathā'prajananena vā|
sandhāraṇādvā bhārasya raktamādhmāpayettataḥ||
sā śoṇite sthite nārī garbhiṇī strīti manyate|
samayādatha śūlāni tasyāḥ kukṣau bhavatyatha||
koṣṭhe gulmodaraṃ tacca garbho'yamiti manyate|
garbho'yamiti ca vyādhirvarṣamekaṃ bahūnyapi||
dhārayatyatha nirbhedaṃ kathañcitsanniyacchati|
athāsyāḥ kālaparyāyātsakṣīrau bhavataḥ stanau||
kṛśā bhavati sā pāṇḍurdohadaṃ cābhinandati|
chardirniṣṭhīvikā caiva tandrā caivaṃ prabādhate||
pādayoḥ śvayathuśca syādraktagulme praduṣyati|
eṣāṃ tu khalu pañcānāṃ gulmānāṃ samudāhṛtam|
vidyādasādhyaṃ nicayaṃ yatnasādhyāṃstathetarān|| ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele nidāne tṛtīyo'dhyāyaḥ||
||caturtho'dhyāyaḥ||
athātaḥ kāsanidānaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
vātātpittātkaphāccaiva kṣatādvātha kṣayādapi|
pañca kāsā bhavantīha teṣāṃ vakṣyāmi lakṣaṇam||
yassadā vātalo janturasātmyaṃ vai niṣevate|
rūkṣamaśnāti pibati rūkṣaṃ hi bahu khādati||
tasya vātaḥ prakupito gṛhītvā hṛdayaṃ tataḥ|
ūrdhvaṃ samprāpya dhamanīratha kāsāya kalpate||
uraḥśūlaṃ pārśvaśūlaṃ pṛṣṭhastambhaśca jāyate|
āṭopyate'syodaraṃ ca śiraścāsyātimanthyate||
uro'bhighātātpārśve ca gṛhyete iva dehinaḥ|
kaphaḥ prakāsamānasya sasaṃrambhaḥ pravartate|
pratataṃ kāsamānasya mūtraṃ vāto ruṇaddhi hi|
ityebhirlakṣaṇairvidyādvātakāsaṃ śarīriṇām||
yassadā pittalo janturasātmyaṃ vai niṣevate|
amlamaśnāti pibati khādatyāsvādayatyapi||
tasya pittaṃ prakupitaṃ gṛhītvā hṛdayaṃ tataḥ|
ūrdhvaṃ samprāpya dhamanīstataḥ kāsāya kalpate||
uro vidahyate cāsya tathaivāpyabhitapyate|
soṣmanāsaśca bhavati dahyete cākṣiṇī tataḥ||
niṣṭhīvati sapittaṃ ca raktaṃ vā nā kadācana|
mukhaṃ bhavati vā pītaṃ pittakāsena dehinaḥ||
yassadā śleṣmalo jantuḥ śleṣmalaṃ bhajate'śanam|
pibatyaśnāti madhuraṃ madhuraṃ vāpi khādati||
avyāyāma rataścāpi divāsvapnaṃ ca sevate|
tasya śleṣmā prakupito gṛhītvā hṛdayaṃ tataḥ||
ūrdhvaṃ samprāpya dhamanīstataḥ kāsāya kalpate|
grathitaṃ śleṣmaṇā kāsaṃ niṣṭhīvati sapūtikam||
duṣṭaśleṣmapratiśyāyaḥ śleṣmakāsena jāyate|
sāhasaṃ karma yaḥ kṛtvā vikṣataṃ khedayatyuraḥ||
niṣṭhīvati saraktaṃ ca kāse kṣatasamudbhave|
strīṣu saktaśca yo jantustasya śuklaparikṣayāt||
liṅgaṃ vātābhibhūtatvātsūcībhiriva tudyate|
saraktaṃ kurute mūtraṃ tathā śuklaṃ ca lohitam||
sarva ete samuddiṣṭāḥ kāsā lakṣaṇato mayā|
tānavekṣya bhiṣag buddhyā tataḥ kuryāccikitsitam|| ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele nidāne caturtho'dhyāyaḥ||
||pañcamo'dhyāyaḥ||
athātaḥ kuṣṭhanidānaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
pippalīṃ kākamācīṃ ca likucaṃ dadhisarpiṣā|
vātāyuṃ payasā sārdhaṃ guḍena saha mūlakam||
anyadevaṃvidhaṃ yacca viruddhaṃ tatsamaśnataḥ|
chardiṃ pratighnataścāpi mithyāsaṃsargasevanāt||
anullikhya vidagdhaṃ ca vidāhi ca samaśnataḥ|
pathi cārādviriktasya śrāntasyodakasevanāt||
matsyān payaśca nimbūṃśca tathaikadhyaṃ samaśnataḥ|
jantossañjāyate kuṣṭhaṃ tadvidhānāṃ ca sevanāt||
yassadā śleṣmalo jantuḥ śleṣmalaṃ bhajate'śanam|
sevate ca divāsvapnaṃ tasya śleṣmā pravardhate||
sa vṛddho dūṣayatyasya māṃsaṃ tvagrudhiraṃ tathā|
utsādyate tvagdoṣeṇa svidyate tena cāpyatha||
tatra kuṣṭhāni jāyante dadru sidhmāni vai punaḥ|
maṇḍalāni ca citrāṇi teṣāṃ vakṣyāmi lakṣaṇam||
maṇḍalāni ghanānīha piṭakābhāni sarvaśaḥ|
sakaṇḍūni visarpīṇi dadrukuṣṭhāni nirdiśet||
prasravanti yadā tāni doṣotsannāni dehinaḥ|
tadāsya kīlaṃ koṣaṃ ca picchilaṃ saṃsravanti ca||
saṃhatāni vipāṇḍūni paṅkiloṣṭhacitāni ca|
tvagutthāni tu rūksāṇi maṇḍalāni tanūnyapi||
sidhmakuṣṭhāni jānīyāttathā tāni bhavanti vai|
picchilaṃ madhuvarṇaṃ ca yadā doṣāḥ sravanti ca||
maṇḍalāni ca śuklāni ghanotsannāni sarvaśaḥ|
vidyānmaṇḍalakuṣṭhāni cirabhedīni dehinām||
yadā tu tāni bhidyante tadā śvetaṃ sravanti hi|
iti śleṣmasamutthāni trīṇi kuṣṭhāni nirdiśet||
yassadā vātalo janturvātalaṃ bhajate'śanam|
vātātapau sevate ca tasya vātaḥ pravardhate||
sa vṛddho dūṣayatyasya tvaṅmāṃsarudhiraṃ tathā|
utsādyate tvagdoṣeṇa svidyate tena cāpyatha||
kapālakuṣṭhaṃ tenāsya praduṣṭe māṃsaśoṇite|
jantorvivṛddhavātasya tasya vakṣyāmi lakṣaṇam||
paruṣāṇyaruṇābhāni maṇḍalāni samāni ca|
vidyātkapālakuṣṭhāni cirabhedīni dehinām||
yassadā pittalo jantuḥ pittalaṃ bhajate'śanam|
vātātapau sevate ca tasya pittaṃ prakupyati||
tad vṛddhaṃ dūṣayatyasya tvaṅmāṃsarudhiraṃ tadā|
utsādyate tvagdoṣeṇa svidyate tena cāpyatha||
tataḥ kuṣṭhāni jāyante praduṣṭe māṃsaśoṇite|
pittasya parikope vā teṣāṃ vakṣyāmi lakṣaṇam||
pakvodumbaravarṇāni maṇḍalānīha yāni tu|
vidyādaudumbarāṇyatra tānyasādhyāni dehinām||
prāpnuvanti yadā bhedaṃ kuṣṭhānyaudumbarāṇi ca|
maṇḍalīkāni kuṣṭhāni tadā tāni bhavanti vai||
maṇḍalāni ca yānīha puṇḍarīkanibhāni vai|
puṇḍarīkāṇi sādhyāni tāni vidyādvicakṣaṇaḥ||
prāpnuvanti yadā bhedaṃ puṇḍarīkāṇi yāni vai|
ṛśyajihvāni kuṣṭhāni tadā tāni vinirdiśet||
nīlotpalasavarṇāni maṇḍalānīha rūpataḥ|
ṛśyajihvāni kuṣṭhāni tānyasādhyāni nirdiśet||
prāpnuvanti yadā bhedamṛśyajihvānyaśeṣataḥ|
kākaṇānīti matimāṃstadā tāni vinirdiśet||
kākaṇantikavarṇāni maṇḍalānīha yāni tu|
kākaṇānīti tānyāhuḥ pratyākhyeyāni dehinām||
iti kuṣṭhanidānaṃ vai vyākhyātamanupūrvaśaḥ|
tanniśamyeha matimān hitajīrṇāśano bhavet|| ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele nidāne pañcamo'dhyāyaḥ||
||ṣaṣṭho'dhyāyaḥ||
athātaḥ pramehanidānaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
prakṛtiprabhavaścaiva narasya svakṛtastathā|
jñeyaḥ prameho dvividhastasya vakṣyāmi lakṣaṇam||
ślakṣṇāṅgā mṛdavaḥ snigdhā bhṛśaṃ śleṣmalamedurāḥ|
jātapramehā nardanti matsyamāṃsocitā narāḥ||
mātāpitṛbhyāmīdṛgbhyāṃ janito yastu mānavaḥ|
medaḥśithilanāttasya prakṛtyā sa tu mehati||
anūpajānāṃ susnigdhairvividhaiścāpi vārijaiḥ|
gavyājaurabhramāṃsaiśca sadyo dadhnā ghṛtena vā||
guḍaprakāraiḥ payasā palalenaudakaiḥ khagaiḥ|
avyāyāmāddivāsvapnātsukhaśayyāsanāttathā||
ityebhirīdṛśaiścānyairmedaḥ snigdhaiḥ pravardhate|
medaḥ pravṛddhaṃ dehaṃ ca vastiṃ ca kledayatyapi||
yassadā medasā klinnaḥ śleṣmalaṃ bhajate'śanam|
tasya prakupitaḥ śleṣmā pramehān kurute daśa||
tadyathodakamehaṃ ca piṣṭamehaṃ tathaiva ca|
evamādīṃstathānyāṃśca teṣāṃ vakṣyāmi lakṣaṇam||
sphaṭikāmbunibhaṃ mūtramudamehī pramehati|
śuklapiṣṭanibhaṃ cāpi piṣṭamehī pramehati||
kāṇḍekṣurasamehī ca mehatīkṣurasopamam|
mūtraṃ śuklopamaṃ cāpi śuklamehī tu mehati||
ityetāṃścaturo mehāñjānīyātkevalātkaphāt|
śleṣmaṇyanubale pitte ye mehanti tu tāñchṛṇu||
lavaṇāmbunibhaṃ mūtraṃ vidyāllavaṇamehinaḥ|
pramehati tathā jantussurāmehī surākṛti|| mūtraṃ sāndraṃ prasannaṃ tu dṛśyate sāndramehinaḥ|
.................................................
.................................................
iti bhele nidāne ṣaṣṭho'dhyāyaḥ||
||saptamo'dhyāyaḥ||
................................................... ................................................... gītāni bhajate nityaṃ pittonmādanipīḍitaḥ||
yassadā śleṣmalo jantuḥ śleṣmalaṃ bhajate'śanam|
sevate ca divāsvapnaṃ tasya śleṣmā pravardhate||
sa vṛddha ūrdhvaṃ hṛdayād gṛhītvā dhamanīrdaśa|
ruddhvā cetovahaṃ mārgaṃ saṃjñāṃ bhraṃśayate tataḥ||
sa bhraṣṭasaṃjñaḥ puruṣastāni tāni viceṣṭate|
gāyannṛtyati caikatra hasatyatha ca roditi||
ekatrāste vinā lokaṃ śete cāpi jaḍo yathā|
janaṃ bhīṣayate cāpi śleṣmonmādī pumāniha||
yastvetat sarvamaśnāti yathoktaṃ doṣakopanam|
sannipātāttathonmādaṃ sarvaliṅgaṃ sa ṛcchati||
sa yadā dhananāśena maraṇena priyasya vā|
atha cintayate, tasya saṃjñā bhraśyati cintayā||
sa cintayati yān bhāvāṃstāneva pralapatyatha|
āgantuṃ pañcamaṃ vidyādityunmādaṃ śarīriṇām||
sarvānetān vijānīyādunmādānvai cikitsitāt|
duḥkhena saṃjñābhraṣṭo hi prakṛtiṃ punar ṛcchati||
evaṃ śarīrajā doṣāḥ śarīre paryavasthitāḥ|
śarīrameva hiṃsanti pāvakaḥ svamivāśrayam||
na hi satvāni hiṃsanti na piśācā na rākṣasāḥ|
devāstathā dharmaśīlāḥ madhyasthā manujānprati||
vamane recane yukto nirūhe cānuvāsane|
na jātu dāruṇān rogānunmādān prāpnuyānnaraḥ|| ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele nidāne saptamo'dhyāyaḥ||
||aṣṭamo'dhyāyaḥ||
athāto'pasmāranidānaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
vātātpittātkaphāccaiva sannipātāttathaiva ca|
apasmārā bhavantīha teṣāṃ vakṣyāmi lakṣaṇam||
yassadā vātalo janturvātalaṃ bhajate'śanam|
vyāyāmaṃ sevate cāpi tasya vātaḥ prakupyati||
sa vṛddha ūrdhvaṃ hṛdayād gṛhītvā dhamanīrdaśa|
ruddhvā cetovahaṃ mārgaṃ saṃjñāṃ bhraṃśayate tataḥ||
sa bhraṣṭasaṃjñaḥ patati dantānkaṭakaṭāyate|
utphālayati netre ca bhruvau prakṣipate tathā||
sa cetpratyāgato brūyāttamasaḥ parato gataḥ|
pratibhāti ca me kṛṣṇā jagatī khaṇḍaśastathā||
tadordhvamevaṃ hṛdayaṃ vāyurvarcopadhāvati|
ityetairlakṣaṇairvidyādapasmāraṃ tu vātajam||
yassadā pittalo jantuḥ pittalaṃ bhajate'śanam|
agnyātapau sevate ca tasya pittaṃ pravardhate||
tad vṛddhamūrdhvaṃ hṛdayād gṛhītvā dhamanīrdaśa|
ruddhvā cetovahaṃ mārgaṃ saṃjñāṃ bhraṃśayate tataḥ||
sa bhraṣṭasaṃjñaḥ patati dantānkaṭakaṭāyate|
utphālayati netre ca bhruvāvutkṣipate tathā||
sa cetpratyāgato brūyāttamasaḥ parato gataḥ|
pratibhāti ca me śuklā jagatī khaṇḍaśastathā||
tadordhvamevaṃ hṛdayaṃ kapho varcopadhāvati|
ityetairlakṣaṇairvidyādapasmāraṃ kaphātmakam||
yastvetatsarvamaśnāti yathoktaṃ doṣakopanam|
sannipātādapasmāraṃ sarvaliṅgaṃ sa ṛcchati||
evaṃ rasairihāpathyairvivṛddheṣvanilādiṣu|
nā'pasmārayate prāṇī na satvairupahanyate||
yadā yadābhivardhante doṣāḥ parvasvivodadhiḥ|
tadā tadāpasmarati naiṣa kliśyati santatam||
jvarasya śoṣagulmānāṃ kāsināmatha kuṣṭhinām|
pramehonmādināṃ caiva tathāpasmāriṇāmapi||
ityaṣṭau vai pradiṣṭāni nidānāni śarīriṇām|
vimānāni pravakṣyāmi yathāvadanupūrvaśaḥ|| ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele aṣṭamo'dhyāyaḥ||
iti bhelasaṃhitāyāṃ nidānasthānaṃ samāptam||
<3>
||vimānasthānam||
||tṛtīyo'dhyāyaḥ||
... ... yasya bhuktaṃ vipacyate|
bhuñjānasyāpi cānnāni balaṃ varṇaśca hīyate||
alpaṃ laghvapi yaccānnaṃ bhuktaṃ duḥkhena jīryati|
nāpnoti balavarṇau ca durbalāgniḥ sa ucyate||
kadācitpacyate bhuktaṃ kadācinna vipacyate|
guru vā laghu vā yasya viṣamāgniḥ sa ucyate||
mitaṃ pacyeta yasyānnamatibhuktaṃ na pacyate|
samāgniṃ taṃ naraṃ vidyātsamapittakaphānilam||
naro bhavati tīkṣṇāgniḥ prakṛtyā vātapaittikaḥ|
vātiko viṣamāgniśca, mandāgniśca kaphānnaraḥ||
yasya hīnādhikastvagniḥ kṣipraṃ dehaṃ sa muñcati|
samāgniśca samātmā yaḥ sa dīrghāyuṣṭvamṛcchati||
gavyamāhiṣavārāhaiḥ kulīrairmatsyakarkaṭaiḥ|
māṃsaissapalalaiḥ snigdhairatyagniṃ pratibhojayet||
godhāniṣkvathite kṣīre siddhaṃ bhuñjīta pāyasam|
pibeddadhi sasarpiṣkaṃ māṣasūpena miśritam||
tathā palalasammiśraṃ kacchapāṇḍarasaṃ pibet|
guḍaṃ ca tailasammiśraṃ kuryādanyacca taddhitam||
snigdhasya viṣamāgnestu vamanādīni kārayet|
grahaṇīdūṣaṇīyoktaṃ mandāgnestu cikitsitam||
antarauṣadhapānāni bahirlepe ca yā kriyā|
śastrakarmavidhānaṃ ca bheṣajaṃ trividhaṃ smṛtam||
madhuro'mlaḥ kaṭuścaiva paktimārgāstrayaḥ smṛtāḥ|
kaṭurbhavati pakvasya pacyamānasya cetarau||
ihānnapānaṃ bhojyaṃ ca madhurāmlaṃ pracakṣate|
vātajeṣu vikāreṣu yadā vā tairna śāmyati||
dadyādathāsmai snigdhāya bhiṣak snehavirecanam|
vastibhirvā cikitseta hyadhobhāge ca mārutaḥ||
tathā ca tiktamadhuraṃ pānamannaṃ pradāpayet|
paittikeṣu vikāreṣu yadi vātanna śāmyati||
tasya snigdhasya vamanaṃ śirasaśca virecanam|
kuryādyathābalaṃ śleṣmā hyūrdhvabhāgaprakīrtitaḥ||
śakṛnmūtrānilāścaiva raktamāṃsādayaśca ṣaṭ|
vidyānnibandhanānīti tāni deheṣu dehinām||
balaṃ vayaḥ śarīraṃ ca pratyekaṃ trividhaṃ smṛtam|
uttamādhamamadhyaṃ tu bhedenātha niśāmayet||
āhāraśca vihāraśca sevyamānau krameṇa tu|
kālena prakṛtiṃ yātastadāhuḥ sātmyalakṣaṇam|| ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele vimāne tṛtīyo'dhyāyaḥ||
||caturtho'dhyāyaḥ||
athāto rogaprakṛtiviniścayaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
pramṛśya dṛṣṭvā pṛṣṭvā ca parīkṣetāturaṃ bhiṣak|
pūrvaṃ tu rogavijñānaṃ tataḥ paścāccikitsitam||
rūpaṃ chāyāmupacayaṃ prāgvyādhervyādhitasya ca|
dṛṣṭvā bhiṣak parīkṣeta prākṛtaṃ vaikṛtaṃ tathā||
jvarasya sāmyaṃ vaiṣamyaṃ gātraṃ vā ślakṣṇakarkaśam|
dṛṣṭvā spṛṣṭvāvagaccheddhi śaityamauṣṇyaṃ ca pāṇinā||
mātāpitṛsamācāraṃ sātmyaṃ garbheṇa dauhṛdam|
vyādhikālaparīṇāmaṃ śakṛnmūtravivarṇatām||
pūrvarūpasamutthānaṃ śarīrāgnivayobalam|
prakṛtiṃ janmadeśaṃ ca bhojanaṃ ca yathocitam||
vyāyāmaniṣṭhāsātmyaṃ ca mātrāmātre ca bhojane|
praśnoktāni vijānīyādyadanyadapi tadvidham||
kecidicchanti munayo doṣamekamihādhikam|
vikārasyāviśiṣṭatvādaniṣṭaṃ kāraṇaṃ ca tat||
rūkṣāsaho naro yastu sa vātaprakṛtiḥ smṛtaḥ|
pettiko'mbusahaścāpi madhurāmlāsahaḥ kaphāt||
svabhāvaprabhavo hyeṣa sahotpanno guṇāgamaḥ|
prakṛtiṃ vai vijānīyāttasya vakṣyāmi lakṣaṇam||
avyaktaṃ ca mahāṃścaiva mahābhūtāni pañca ca|
parāḥ prakṛtayassapta svabhāvaḥ kāla eva ca||
tebhyo bhavanti bhūtāni tānyeva pratiyānti ca|
indriyāṇīndriyārthāśca buddhayaḥ pañca tāstathā||
ṛtukāle yadā nārī rūkṣāṇyannāni sevate|
udāvarte tathāpīha karma cātiniṣevate||
tadaivaṃ vātasanduṣṭaṃ raktaṃ garbhāśayasthitam|
tadvidhenaiva śuklena yadā yogamupaiti vai||
tadā vātapraduṣṭena śuklena rudhireṇa ca|
vātaprakṛtitāmeti garbhaḥ prāgātmanaḥ sa vai||
evamevartukāle vai bhajantyāḥ kaphapaittikam|
ślaiṣmikī paittikī cāpi prakṛtirjāyate tataḥ||
hrasvaḥ śīghraḥ kṛśaścāṇuḥ pralāpī paruṣapriyaḥ|
stabdhāṅgo viṣamaśliṣṭo gaṇarūpe gaṇe dhṛtiḥ||
sahaḥ kleśasya visrambhī rūkṣatvaganavasthitaḥ|
kharamūrdhajaromāṅgaḥ kṣipragrāhī tathā smṛtaḥ||
svapneṣu coṣṭreṇāyāti viyatyapi tu gacchati|
yasyopaśete susnigdhaṃ sa vātaprakṛtirnaraḥ||
śithilāṅgo'garugandhaścaṇḍaḥ śīghro mahāśanaḥ|
valīpalitakhālityaśīghrapākī tathākṣamaḥ||
vṛttākṣaḥ krodhano yaśca durbalo durbalendriyaḥ|
nāmlāśaḥ śītaśītāśī duṣprajāḥ śītalapriyaḥ||
ativarṇo'timedhāvī svapne pāvakadṛk tathā|
śīghramāvāti yaḥ snātaḥ paittikaprakṛtirnaraḥ||
susnigdhaḥ ślakṣṇabaddhāṅgaḥ subhagaḥ priyadarśanaḥ|
dṛḍhasmṛtiściragrāhī dṛḍhabhaktiparāyaṇaḥ||
priyamāṃsoṣṇakaṭukaḥ priyayoṣidbahupradaḥ|
kṣamāvān balavān dhanyaḥ śītāluraśanapriyaḥ||
cirād dṛḍhavyādhiratho mitavāgalpabhuk smṛtaḥ|
dīrghadarśī mahotsāho dhīraḥ kleśasahastathā||
romadantanakhaiḥ keśairbahulairyassubandhanaiḥ|
cirādāvāti ca snātaḥ svapne paśyati codakam||
yastu rūkṣaṃ tu sahate sa śleṣmaprakṛtirnaraḥ|
saṃsṛṣṭaprakṛtiṃ vidyātsaṃsṛṣṭaiścāpi lakṣaṇaiḥ||
nivṛttā prakṛtirdhanyā dvandvā bhavati madhyamā|
sannipātātmikā yā tu jaghanyā sā prakīrtitā|| ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele caturtho'dhyāyaḥ||
||pañcamo'dhyāyaḥ||
athāto vyādhitarūpīyaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
guruvyādhirnaraḥ kaścinmūrtyā caiva balena ca|
laghuvyādhiḥ narastvanyaḥ sattvādibhirananvitaḥ||
guruvyādhirivābhāti bhiṣak tatra pramuhyati|
tatrālpamātraṃ bhaiṣajyaṃ sevitaṃ gururogiṇām||
na nirdahatyataḥ sarvāndoṣānalpena tejasā|
mūrchā chardistimitatā jṛmbhā ca gurugātratā||
tṛṣṇā santatabhāvaśca bhavantyetāni tasya tu|
mustā kuṣṭhaṃ haridre dve pragrahātiviṣāmayāḥ||
bhallātakaṃ vayasthā ca citrakaḥ suradāru ca|
etairāsthāpanaṃ tacca kuryād gomūtrasaṃyutaiḥ||
pūrṇādiṣṭānadiṣṭāṃśca tathā śāntiṃ niyacchati|
āragvadhaṃ saptaparṇaṃ madanaṃ svādukaṇṭakam||
śārṅgeṣṭāṃ kaṭukāṃ pāṭhāṃ naktamālaṃ savatsakam|
etadāragvadhārthaṃ tu kuryādāsthāpanaṃ bhiṣak|| ..............................................
...............................................
||ṣaṣṭho'dhyāyaḥ||
athāta ṛtuvimānaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
ādityagativaiśeṣyādṛtūnāṃ parivartanāt|
kṣayaṃ vṛddhiṃ ca gacchanti yathāsvaṃ dhātavo nṛṇām||
bhūmibāṣpātpurovātād ghanacchāyāprasaṅgataḥ|
mahīkaluṣasanduṣṭātpīyamānānnavodakāt||
graiṣmikātkṛśabhāvācca prāṇe durbalatāṃ gate|
varṣāsu dehināmagnirmṛdutvamupagacchati||
tato viṣṭambhajo vāyuryasmāddhītaḥ prakupyati|
tanna seveta varṣāsu kaṭvamlalavaṇān rasān||
mudgayūṣeṇa vāśnīyātpurāṇān śāliṣaṣṭikān|
athavā yavagodhūmaṃ sṛṣṭavāte hitaṃ smṛtam||
paṭolāni ca takraṃ ca jāṅgalāṃśca rasān laghūn|
kaupaṃ divyaṃ jalaṃ siddhaṃ bhajedāsthāpanāni ca||
asaṃsthitatvādvarṣāsu jalamamlaṃ vipacyate|
