tasmādvivardhate pittaṃ yathāmlaiśca tathāśanaiḥ||
sūryopatāpātsahasā gatvā caivauṣadhīkṛtam|
varṣāsu nicayaṃ pittaṃ kopaṃ śaradi gacchati||
snānānulepanaṃ tasmācchītaṃ śaradi kārayet|
vījayettālavṛntaiśca vigāheta sarassu ca||
lājasaktuṃ pibeccāpi śarkarāmadhuvāribhiḥ|
mudgayūṣeṇa cāśnīyātpurāṇān śāliṣāṣṭikān||
rasān madhurakāṃścāpi jāṅgalān sarpiṣā kṛtān|
vidārīkṣurasaṃ drākṣāṃ sevetānyacca tadvidham||
gatapittapravegaśca śaratkāle ghṛtaṃ pibet|
tathāsyāpyāyyate dehaḥ śeṣā doṣāśca yāntyadhaḥ||
paścādvātasya rūkṣatvācchītalatvādṛtostathā|
hemantena nigacchanti kharatvaṃ dhātavo nṛṇām||
tasmātsnehaṃ bahuvidhaṃ snānaṃ coṣṇena vāriṇā|
bhajetkharatvapāruṣye tathābhyaṅgātpraśāmyati||
śakunānaudakānmatsyān snehāmlalavaṇānvitān|
ānūpāni ca māṃsāni sevetānyacca tadvidham||
kaṭurhi paścimo vāyuḥ śaityaṃ tīvraṃ ca vāriṇaḥ|
atyarthaṃ rūkṣayedvāyuḥ śarīraṃ rūkṣabhojāt||
guruprāvaraṇaśca syādātapāgnī ca saṃśrayet|
rūkṣe hi tejasā jantoḥ śarīrāddhriyate balam||
āpo hi śītamadhurā hemante tu bhavantyatha|
tasmātkaphastathā tābhiḥ snigdhairannaiśca cīyate||
śītalatvādṛtoścāpi na tāvatparibhidyate|
tasmāttailaguḍopetāṃ vāruṇīṃ śiśire pibet||
vividhāni ca māṃsāni bhakṣayecca prakārataḥ|
evaṃ tu nicitaḥ śleṣmā śītatvādiha dehinām||
dravatāmeti saṃspṛṣṭo vasante sūryatejasā|
ravirhi madhyamāṃ kāṣṭhāṃ vasante pratipadyate||
dahyatāmiva śailānāṃ nṛṇāmaṅge prakupyati|
tataḥ śleṣmā dravībhūto hṛdayaṃ vyapalimpati||
tasmācchardyavipākau ca dṛśyete śiśirātyaye|
tasmādvasante śleṣmaghnaṃ madhumārdvīkamīritam|| .................................................
.................................................
iti bhele ṣaṣṭho'dhyāyaḥ||
iti bhelasaṃhitāyāṃ vimānasthānaṃ samāptam||
<4>
athāto rasavimānaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
śarīraṃ dhārayantīha ṣaḍrasāḥ samamāhṛtāḥ|
ato'nyathā vikārāṃstu janayanti śarīriṇām||
rūkṣo laghuḥ sthiraśśītaḥ kaṣāyastikta eva ca|
tīkṣṇoṣṇāvamlalavaṇau kaṭurvāpi vikāśyatha||
śītaḥ snigdho gururbalyaḥ picchilo madhuro rasaḥ|
kaṣāyatiktakaṭukaḥ śīto rūkṣo'nilaḥ smṛtaḥ||
rūkṣoṣṇamamlaṃ pittantu kaṭukaṃ ca pracakṣate|
śleṣmā tu madhuraḥ snigdhaḥ śīto mandaḥ sthiro guruḥ||
ityetān rasadoṣāṇāṃ sahotpannān guṇānviduḥ|
tatra vāyuguṇaistulyān kaṣāyakaṭutiktakān||
kaṭvamlalavaṇaistulyāṃstathā pittaguṇānviduḥ|
madhuraṃ lavaṇāmlau ca vidyātkaphasamān rasān||
tasmādabhyasyamānaistaiḥ śleṣmā dehe pravardhate|
atraikatra kṛto rāśirdvirmahatvamiharcchati||
guṇasāmyādvivardhante yathāsvaṃ dhātavo nṛṇām|
rasaistadviparītaiśca yāntyete kṣayamāhṛtaiḥ||
yathodakaṃ samāsādya śāntiṃ gacchati pāvakaḥ|
kaṣāyatiktakaṭukai rūkṣo rūkṣairvivardhate||
mārutaḥ snigdhabhāvaiśca tato'nyairupaśāmyati|
kaṭvamlalavaṇaiḥ pittamuṣṇamuṣṇairvivardhate||
śītaiśśāmyati śeṣaistu guṇānāṃ hi viśeṣataḥ|
snigdhaḥ snigdhaiḥ kaphaścāpi vardhate madhurādibhiḥ||
rasaiḥ śāmyati rūkṣaiśca kaṣāyakaṭutiktakaiḥ|
ekaikamekasāmānyādvardhayanti trayastrayaḥ||
ghnanti cānyaguṇatvena rasā doṣaṃ śarīriṇām|
na vāyussaha tailena snehauṣṇyādvyapalīyate||
śītatvānmadhuratvācca na pittaṃ saha sarpiṣā|
raukṣyātkaṣāyabhāvācca snigdho madhura eva ca||
na śleṣmā madhunā sārdhaṃ dehe paryavatiṣṭhate|
ānūpamāṃsajā vāpi vasā majjāna eva ca||
tailavanmārutaṃ ghnanti snehauṣṇyagurubhāvataḥ|
śākādaviṣkirāṇāṃ ca rasā majjāna eva ca||
ghṛtavad ghnanti te pittaṃ śītamādhuryabhāvataḥ|
kaṣāyatiktakaṭukaṃ yacca kiñcidihauṣadham||
madhuraṃ tatkaphaṃ hanti guṇānyatvena dehinām|
atha nātyupayuñjīta pippalīṃ kṣārameva ca||
lavaṇaṃ caiva, bahvetad bhuktaṃ doṣāya kalpate|
śleṣmāṇaṃ cyāvayitvā tu notsahetāpakarṣitum||
pippalī pākamadhurā tasmāttāṃ nātibhakṣayet|
atyāhṛtā paceddehaṃ tīkṣṇoṣṇakaṭubhāvataḥ||
kṣāraṃ ca lavaṇaṃ caiva bhoktuṃ neheta kevalam|
mātrāvaduṣṇaṃ snigdhaṃ ca sātmyaṃ svādu ca bhojanam||
avidāhi ca yat pāke jīrṇe tadupayojayet|
balavarṇakaraṃ snigdhamuṣṇaṃ śleṣmānilāpaham||
vidahyate na mātrā ca sātmyatāṃ ca karotyatha|
svādu puṣṭikaraṃ jantoravidāhi prasādanam||
nṛṇāmāyuṣkaraṃ caiva jīrṇe bhojanamiṣyate|
na jalpanna hasaṃścāpi na drutaṃ na vilambitam||
bhuñjītābhyādṛtaḥ snigdhaṃ dehe nirvartate rasaḥ|
yatheha vodyato vahniḥ pravarteta sudāruṇi||
pacyamāne tathā bhukte rasādirupacīyate|
jīvayanti paraṃ varṣā hemantaḥ pāyayatyapi||
grīṣmaḥ pacati cāpyenaṃ pariṇāma ivāparaḥ|
bhṛśamauṣṇyātkaṭurgrīṣmaḥ sarvadhātuviśoṣaṇaḥ||
dhairyasthairyakaraṃ śītaṃ, varṣāmadyaba....|
........||
ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
||śārīrasthāna||
dvitīyo'dhyāyaḥ||

....................... māno'vatiṣṭhate|
jātasya daśame māse nāmagotre namaskṛte|| taruṇasya kumārasya vardhamāneṣu dhātuṣu|
asthimajjasu pūrṇeṣu śukraṃ na pratipadyate||
aṅgāṅgeṣu pravṛddheṣu pratimūleṣu dhātuṣu|
śuklaṃ ca ṣoḍaśe varṣe suvyaktaṃ pratipadyate||
tathā vṛddhasya jantośca parikṣīṇeṣu dhātuṣu| vivekā[ma] na yathāpūrvaṃ vivicyante parikṣayāt||
tato'lparetā bhavati sujīrṇo durbalo'thavā|
na paśyati naraḥ śuklaṃ sarvadhātuparikṣayāt||
raktaṃ māṃsaṃ vasāsthīni[ca] majjā śuklaṃ tathānalaḥ|
śakṛnmūtre ca tairmandaṃ vidyānnavatitaḥ param||
iha narchati garbhaṃ strī vātenopahatā tathā|
yonidoṣeṇa[yā] cānnena na hi vandhyāsti nā ca na||
vamanaṃ recanaṃ caiva vastimāsthāpanaṃ[vastirā] tathā|
tasmāttatkārayetstrīṇāṃ prasiddhāḥ prasuvanti[prasa] vai||
athātra bhavati praśnaḥ kasmācchabdaṃ[rasā chardiṃ] na nāsayā|
gṛhṇāsyāsyena[gṛhīṣvā] vā gandhaṃ tulyaṃ sarvatra khaṃ yadi||
tvagbhāge[tvadbhā] ca same kasmānna gṛhṇātyanyayā rasam|
iti tadvacanaṃ śrutvā pratyuvāca punarvasuḥ||
ghrāṇaṃ gandhaṃ ca bhaumaṃ hi rūpaṃ[rūkṣaṃ] cakṣuśca taijasam|
saṃsparśaṃ sparśanaṃ vāyoḥ śrotraṃ śabdātmakhaṃ[śabdātmakaṃ] tathā||
rasanaṃ ca raso hy āpyaṃ[vyā-] tasmādetairihendriyaiḥ|
yathāsvaṃ tulyayogitvādviṣayagrahaṇaṃ smṛtam||
svaṃ svaṃ hi viṣayaṃ dhāturvijānātyātmanānvitaḥ|
ātmendriyamanorthānāṃ bandhācceti[bandhiśce] samādiśa||
ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele dvitīyo'dhyāyaḥ||
tṛtīyo'dhyāyaḥ||
athāto'samānagotrīyaṃ śārīraṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
ihāsamānagotrān tu[vai] ṛtusnātāṃ striyaṃ vrajet|
medhā vinamarogaṃ[di-] sā putramevaṃ prasūyate||
bījadoṣādyathā sasyaṃ na samyak viprarohati[-rohiṇī]|
mātāpitrostu doṣeṇa tathā garbhaḥ praṇaśyati[prayaccha]||
tasmātsamyagrasāhārā vṛtukāle[dṛ-]'tha dampatī|
rahassaṃyogameyātāṃ smarantau manasā vibhum||
vikṛtāḥ syuragarbhā vai rasāpathyaniṣevaṇe|
sandhāraṇādvā vegānāṃ yonidoṣeṇa vā punaḥ||
yonau doṣopasṛṣṭāyāṃ[doṣopavṛ-] na garbho hyavatiṣṭhate|
tathaiva bāhyayonau[brāhma] hi nirvāhiṇyāṃ[nirvāhinyāṃ] ca sarvadā||
gṛhṇāti vāyuryasyāṃ ca yonau śukram[ruśraṃ] upāgatam[upāgataḥ]|
bibharti garbhaṇī garbhaṃ śuddhārtavasamanvitā||
cyavate ca yadā cāsau tadā garbhaḥ praṇaśyati|
vātodaraṃ striyāstadvai tasmā dūkṣāṇi[dūpā] varjayet||
bhavatyabhyadhike śukle puruṣaḥ śoṇite'ṅganā|
napuṃsakaṃ tayoḥ sāmye tasmācchuklaṃ vivardhayet||
yadā tu kalalaṃ vāyustad dvidhā kurute balī|
yamau tadā sambhavataḥ kṛṣṇātreyavaco yathā||
tatra śuklottare bhāge pumān raktottare'ṅganā|
anenaiva ca kalpena yamakeṣvapi nirdiśet||
vāyustvaśvavarāhāṇāṃ deheṣu valavān punaḥ|
sa tatra kalalaṃ bhittvā karoti bahuputratām||
nāpnoti ca yadā garbho rasaṃ duṣṭaiḥ sirāmukhaiḥ|
asaṃpūrṇo vasannāgaḥ tathā varṣāṇi tiṣṭhati||
sampūrṇagātro bhavati yadā sa rasabhāvitaḥ|
tadā sūto[prasautyathākāśaṃ] yathākālaṃ garbhaḥ strīkukṣivicyutaḥ||
ye ca te viṃśatiḥ proktā yonidoṣāś cikitsakaiḥ[cikitsite]|etaiścānyaiśca bahubhirgarbho vyāpadyate striyāḥ||
tasmādetāṃścikitsettu doṣān putracikīrṣayā|
ibhāśvagarbhasārūpyaṃ[ibhāśvā] prayogāddhi bhavecchuci||
vāyvākāśartuyukto hi daivateṣvitareṣu tu|
antarjāto jātavedāḥ prabhāvāyopakalpate||
ṛtau yadā strīpuruṣau prasannamanasau rahaḥ|
upeyātāmatha tadā garbho bhavati sāttvikaḥ||
ṛtau yadā strīpuruṣau vyāyastamanasau bhṛśam|
rpeyātāmatha tadā garbho bhavati rājasaḥ||
ṛtau yadā strīpuruṣau pradīnamanasau rahaḥ|
upeyātāmatha tadā garbho bhavati tāmasaḥ||
lakṣaṇaṃ ca samutthānamityetatsamudāhṛtam|
tisṛṇāṃ sattvayonīnāṃ, miśrāṃstenaiva[mitrāntenaira] lakṣayet||
ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele śārīre tṛtīyo'dhyāyaḥ||
caturtho'dhyāyaḥ||
athātaḥ puruṣanicayaṃ śārīraṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iha khalu bho ayam[jaya]-annātpuruṣo bhavati|
rasajanmāno'sya[rajja] vyādhayo bhavanti|
tadyathā khalvayaṃ puruṣo rasajanmā rasajīvī rasajvalano rasasamādhiko rasajīvanaśca bhavati|
rasānāmasamyagupayogānmithyopayogāttadvikārānṛcchati|
na kaścinmithyopayogādajīrṇāpathyabhojanātsvastho bhavati||
athātra praśno bhavati|
ko'tra khalvasyāhāraṃ pacati vātaḥ pittaṃ śleṣmānupānaṃ veti|
netyāha bhagavān punarvasurātreyaḥ|
yadyete pākahetavaḥ syustarhi na kaścidiha durbalāgniḥ syādvātādīnāṃ sannihitatvāt, sānupānatvācca|
athāsyoṣmā tejaśca śarīrasthamāhāraṃ pacataḥ|
te kāyāgnir iti[-agne] vidyāt||
tatra bhela ātreyamidamuvāca---bhagavan|
pañcadhā ye śārīrāḥ[śārīrā] paṭhyante̍ ālocakarājakabhrājakasādhaka-pācakabhedena[vā]̍ teṣāṃ katham idaṃ[idāṃ] pañcābhidhāyināṃ pṛthaktvaṃ bhavatīti||
atrovāca bhagavānātreyaḥ tatrālocako nāma varṣaśītātapapravṛddhaḥ|
sa dvividhaḥ|
cakṣurvaiśeṣiko buddhivaiśeṣikaśceti|
cakṣurvaiśeṣiko nāma ya ātmamanasossannikarṣajñānamudīrayitvā citte cittamapyādhāya saṃsvedajāṇḍajodbhijjajarāyujānāṃ caturṇāṃ bhūtagrāmāṇāṃ lakṣaṇasaṃsthānarūpavarṇasvarairuccāvacānāṃ puṣpaphalapatrāṇāṃ rūpanirvṛty arthamekaikasya[arthamaikekaṃ dvau vā trayo vā] dvayostrayāṇāṃ sarveṣāṃ vā yugapatpraṇipatitānāṃ cakṣuṣā vaiśeṣyamutpādayatīti[vaiṣamya]||
buddhivaiśeṣiko nāma yo bhruvormadhye śṛṅgāṭakasthaḥ susūkṣmānarthānadhyātmakṛtān gṛhṇāti jñānena, jñeyaṃ jñānaṃ kṛtsnajñeyakaivalyārtheṣu darśayati|
tasmād jñānajñeyaṃ jñātvā jñānaṃ gṛhṇāti|
gṛhītaṃ dhārayati|
dhāritaṃ pratyudāharati, atītaṃ smarati, pratyutpannaṃ kṛtvā'nāgataṃ prārthayati, jātamātraśca punaranupadiṣṭasvabhāvaṃ mātṛstanyamabhilaṣati|
dhyāne pratyāhāre yojanācca buddhivaiśeṣyamutpādayatīti||
tatra bhrājako nāma yo hyasya[yasya] śarīraṃ lakṣaṇaṃ copagamayati, prādhānyaṃ pradarśayati, śiraḥpāṇipādapārśvapṛṣṭhodarajaṅghāsyanakhanayanakeśānāṃ ca pratibhāvṛddhiviśeṣānutpādayati, bhrājayatīti bhrājakaḥ||
prabhaviṣṇutvendriyaprābalyād budhyavasthāhaṃkāreṇa vā-abhimatarthamarthebhya[abhimate] ātmakṛtamādhate|
cakṣuḥśrotraghrāṇarasanasparśanavākpāṇipādapāyūpasthebhyaḥ sarveṣāṃ viṣayārthānāṃ svabhāvoparaktānāṃ parasparebhyo rāgamutpādayatīti|
antarmadhye ca pittasthānamantaraṃ praviśya rāgaṃ janayatīti rājakaḥ[rājakam]||
sādhako nāma yaḥ[yā] śabdasparśagandhebhyo'rthakāmebhyaśca devapitṛṛṣibhyaśca iha cāmutrakāṇāṃ ca pādārthānāṃ viśreyasama?dhikṛtya sarvapadārthā nāpnoti[nāṃ nūti] svayuktyā sādhayatīti sādhakaḥ||
pācako nāma aśitapītalīḍhakhāditamāhārajātaṃ jātanīryaṃ pācayatīti[ - -ca] pācakaḥ|
yaḥ svakaṃ kāmamevāgniṃ prapūrayati harṣayati||
bhavanti cātra--- yo'yaṃ nirdahati kṣipramāhāraṃ sarvadehinām|
apānamadyanidanaḥ? kāyāgniḥ paripaṭhyate[pariparyate]||
prabhāvalakṣyassaṃyukto[-lakṣyasati] jīvasyeha sanātanaḥ|
nābhimadhye śarīrasya vijñeyaṃ somamaṇḍalam||
somamaṇḍalamadhyasthaṃ vidyāttat sūryamaṇḍalam|
pradīpavaccāpi nṛṇāṃ tasya madhye hutāśanaḥ||
dehinā bhojanaṃ bhuktaṃ nānāvyañjanasaṃskṛtam|
sūryo divi yathā tiṣṭhan tejoyukto gabhastibhiḥ||
viśoṣayati sarvāṇi palvalāni payāṃsi ca |
tadvaccharīriṇāṃ vyaktaṃ[bhuktaṃ] jāṭharo nābhisaṃsthitaḥ||
mayūkhaiḥ kṣipramādatte sūryakānto maṇiryathā|
kṣipraṃ samyak pradahati gomayaṃ kāṣṭhameva ca||
sthūlakāyeṣu sattveṣu yavamātrapramāṇataḥ|
hrasvakāyeṣu sattveṣu truṭimātrapramāṇataḥ||
krimikīṭapataṅgeṣu vāyumātro'vatiṣṭhati|
yastaṃ cikitsetsīdantaṃ[cikitsiddhantaṃ] vyādhinā cāpi dehinām||
āyurvedābhiyogena sa vai kāyacikitsakaḥ|
rasaṃ ca śoṇitaṃ caiva medo māṃsamathāpi ca||
mahatyanaśane nyaṇāṃ sarvāṇyetāni khādati|
agnīṣomātmakaṃ sarvaṃ jagatsthāvarajaṅgamam||
agnīṣomātmakāḥ sarve dehinastu caturvidhāḥ|
sūryātmakāni sarvāṇi[cāndrāṇi] tathā somātmakāni ca||
mahatyanaśane nyaṇāṃ tenāntrāṇi sa[na] khādati|
jāṭharo jalasambhūtaḥ pāvakaḥ pavanaissaha||
pradīpyate nṛṇāṃ koṣṭhe sati vendhanapūritaḥ|
ikṣvākukośam[śoka] āsthāya yathā dīpaḥ sthire'mbhasi||
tiṣṭhate timire sakto na tathā calite'mbhasi|
evaṃ śarīriṇāṃ koṣṭhe[rūṣṭhe] vaikṛtena punaḥ punaḥ||
agnirvaiṣamyamāpnoti pūryamāṇaḥ punaḥ punaḥ|
sa ca yatnena vai rakṣyo vipanne[vipanno] doṣadarśanāt|
chardyate[dviyarta] cātisāryeta vikāraṃ cāyamṛcchati||
asāvasamprāptakālo[śvāsaṃ] ya evaṃ mriyate, varṣaśataṃ hi puruṣāyustaccānāptuṃ jīrṇalaghupathyabhojanānuvartinā bhavitavyamiti||
tatrāha kasmād alasakaviṣūcikāvān[-viṣūcikāvasādhyaye vāgavi] sadya evāgadī bhavati kaścinmriyata iti? atrāha---rūkṣasyādhyaśanena-utpīḍitā[utpiti] vātapittaśleṣmāṇaḥ[-śleṣmāṇi] ūrdhvamadho vā nānulomā bhavanti, udvṛttaistatra taiḥ sadya eva mriyate|
snigdhasyādhyaśamotpīḍitā vātādayaḥ ūrdhvamadho vā'nulomāḥ[anulomāṃsvāṅgasāṃstu] svāṅgadeśāṃstu pradadyante, tatra taiḥ sadya evāgadī bhavati||
atha daśāntarguhāḥ[-antahānta], daśa bahirguṇāḥ?|
tadyathā---dvicakṣuṣī dvināsike dve śravasī kaṇṭhanāḍīgudameḍhravāyusrotāṃsīti[srotasī kaṇṭhanābhi-] daśa antarguhāḥ|
daśa dhamanyo hṛdaye[hṛdayaṃ] nibaddhā bhavanti|
tāḥ prabhave[prabhavaṃ] caturaṃgulamātraṃ gatvā viṃśatirbhavanti|evametā daśa dhamanyaḥ ṣaṣṭirbhavanti|
tatra tu bhavanti[bhavantutrī] trīṇi śatasahasrāṇi ṣaṣṭiriyaṃ[ṣahtaṃśāli] jālāni sirāṇāṃ|
tadyathā---vṛkṣaḥ[vṛkṣa] śākhāvṛtaḥ[śākhāvṛta] phalapalāśairavatarati sarvatra|
tadyathā vā hāgo[hanyo]'śmabhiratataḥ[aśmabhirataraḥ] tathāyaṃ[tatāyaṃ] sirābhiravatataḥ|
romakūpe romakūpe[rūpakūpe] hyasya sirāmukhaṃ bhavati, yataḥ svedaḥ[svetaḥ] kṣaratīti[kṣiratīti]||
tatrāha kasmādayaṃ puruṣo na santatamapasmaratīti? atrocyate---yathā saritāṃ prādurbhāve vārijāni sattvāni prādurbhavanti hāse vā hasanti|
yadā yadā rasavegaṃ prāpnuvanti tadā tadā apasmārayanti|
tasmād dvyahāttryahāt pakṣānmāsāntarāccāpasmarante||
kecidrakṣopahata ityāhuḥ, taccāyuktam|
yadi hyevaṃ syād dṛśyeran puruṣaśarīre prahārādīni vā|
tataśca[tastaccha] naivam|
vetālābhibhūta[velāvabhūta] iti cettacāpyanupapannam|
puṇyāni hyeṣāṃ manāṃsi[vanāṃsi] dhyāyanti[dhyāyantye]|
yadi caivaṃ kadācidbhavati| tasmāttadeva pāṭhyaṃ rasopahatamiti cedevameva|
rasapravivekakāle tṛtīyakacaturthakāva pi[va] dṛśyete jvarāvapi|neṣatrāṃ hyananārthaḥ?||
atrāha kiṃ khalvasya[jalpa] garbhasya prathamaṃ sambhavati? hastau[hastaṃ] pādāviti baḍiśaḥ tatpratiṣṭhitatvāt[pratiṣṭatāt] śarīrasya[śaīraśva]|
paścādguda iti śaunakaḥ tadāśritatvādvāyoḥ|
nābhiriti khaṇḍakāpyaḥ tatra nāḍīnāṃ pratiṣṭhitatvāt[pratiṣṭa]|
hṛdayamiti parāśaraḥ vijñānamūlakānāṃ tanmūlatvāt|
śira iti bharadvājaḥ śarīrasya tanmūlatvāt|
cakṣuriti kāśyapaḥ|
netyāha bhagavānpunarvasurātreyaḥ|
tasmādarbudamevāsya prathamaṃ sambhavati|
tatra sarve śarīrapradeśāssambhavanti arbudasnehotpannāḥ||
atrāha---kiṃ nu[tu] garbho[garvo] māturudarastho'śnāti[māturastho] na veti|
atrocyate nāśnāti| yadi hyaśnīyāt syād asya[asyā puruṣa] purīṣamatītakālaṃ, na cedamasti[cedasi]|
kathaṃ tarhi? nābhyāṃ nāḍī pratiṣṭhitā tasyāmaparā māturhṛdayamāśritā tayā māturannaraso'bhivahan garbhaṃ prīṇayatyabhivardhayati|
tadyathā kulyāḥ kedāramabhisaṃśrayantyo bhāvayanti tadvat||
tatrāha kathaṃ garbho māturudare tiṣṭhatīti|
ūrdhvamiti śaunakaḥ|
avākchirā iti bharadvājaḥ|
netyāha bhagavānpunarvasurātreyaḥ|
yadyūrdhvaṃ tiṣṭhet tarhi mātṛghātī syāt|
yadyavākchirāḥ svaghātī syāt|
kathaṃ tarhi? tiryak sarvairayamaṅgapratyaṅgaiḥ pratibhugnaḥ śete|| tasya tadananataraṃ tatprathamaṃ pratipadyate [sandarbheṇānena pratipipādayiṣito'rthaḥ na spaṣṭaḥ]|
tasmāttasya śiraḥ prathamaṃ punarvasurātreyaḥ pratipadyate|
tadasya gurutaraṃ bhavatīti|
atha khalu vṛkko medo gururiti saṃpravṛddhau parasparamabhivardhayanti||
tatra ślokaḥ, ūṣmā rasastho dehe'smiñjīvanaṃ gṛhya tiṣṭhati|
rasodbhavaḥ pumāṃstasmādraso jīvanamucyate|| ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele śārīre caturtho'dhyāyaḥ||
||pañcamo'dhyāyaḥ||
athātaḥ śarīranicayaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iha khalvojastejaḥ śarīre nitye ca[va] bhavataḥ |
tayoḥ[teyo] sthānāni dvādaśa bhavanti|
tadyathā---tvakśoṇita[vaśoṇīta]-māṃsamedo'sthimajjāśuklasvedapittaśleṣmamūtrapurīṣāṇīti|
tānyavyāpannāni sukhamityucyate|
vyāpannaistu[vyāpannestu] vātapittaśleṣmāṇaḥ praduṣṭā rasādiṣu vikārānupajanayanti||
atha yonayaścatasro bhavanti|
tadyathā---jarāyujāṇḍajodbhijjasvedajāśceti|
tatra jarāyujā jarāyuyuktāssaṃbhavanti[-yu] paśumṛgamanuṣyādayaḥ|
śakuna-matsya[macha]-kacchapa-sarpa[sarva]-prabhṛtayoṇḍajāḥ|
yūkāmat-kuṇa[kupa]-pataṅgāśiviṣamakṣikādayaḥ svedajāḥ|
udbhijjāstu tṛṇalatāvṛkṣavanaspataya iti||
tatra puṣpaphalavanto vṛkṣāḥ|
apuṣpaphalavanto vanaspatayaḥ|
sapuṣpāḥ saphalāścāpuṣpāścāphalāśca bīrudhaḥ|
phalapākā latāścauṣadhayaḥ|
tatra ye śvetakṣīrāḥ saumyāḥ, raktakṣīrāḥ kādrā vāruṇā vāligrahā? iti||
iha khalu triṣu daśarātreṣu pumān rasena saṃyujyate|
āsāṃ tu khalu catasṛṇāṃ yonīnāṃ ṛtukāle yadā rasāḥ samyak prāptiṃ vā gacchanti tadā garbhaḥ tiṣṭhaty[tiṣṭe] avikṛtaḥ viparyaye viparyayaḥ||
tryahantu[-ha] khalu purāṇarudhiraṃ parivarjayet|
kiñca tatpurāṇam? yadāditastryahaṃ parisravadṛtukāle tatpurāṇam|
tasmin tryahe garbha-upakramaṇe[upakrameṇa] na tiṣṭhati|
avasthito[avasthi vā] vā nāyuṣi samartho bhavati|
nirgate[nirgave] tu tryaheṇa purāṇe rudhire cāvasthite[ṇa] śuddhasnātāyāścaturthaṣaṣṭha-aṣṭama[aṣṭa]-daśama-dvādaśeṣu[dvādaśamu] ahassu garbhovakramamāṇaḥ pumān bhavati|
pañcamasaptama-navamaikādaśeṣu[-navaikādaśu] strītvāyopakalpate|sa eṣa āsaptarātrātsarvasañcāro'bhidadhatyataḥ paramasañcārovakṛtatvādvālpāt?||
strīṇāṃ khalu śoṇitaṃ śarīraṃ śoṣayati tasmānna tāḥ rajaḥ paśyanti|
paripūrṇadhātuśarīrāstu yadā bhavanti tadā vivekajalalohitaṃ māse māse prativedayanti|
pratigatapraveśaṃ ca tatpunarmāsena samāgacchatyārtavam||
yadā tu tacchoṇitaṃ duṣṭaṃ mārgaṃ pratipadyate tadā strīṇāṃ pradaro bhavati|
taṃ śarīraṃ śoṣayantaṃ[śoṣaya] lohitapittabheṣajenopakrameta||
garbhiṇyāstu tridhā kāye raso'bhinirvartate, garbhatvāya[garbhātpā] stanyatvāya rasatvāya ceti||
iha khalu bhoḥ garbhātsthirādantaḥsthādāyatamudaraṃ[-sthitāvasthātamā] bhavati madhye nāryāmantargatāyām|
sthūlamūlasaṃsthitasaṃvṛttamacchidraṃ puruṣo'ntargate|
droṇīvodaraṃ[droṇicodaraṃ] bhavatyubhayorantargatayoḥ|
vāmaṃ pārśvamāyataṃ māturudarasthā strī, dakṣiṇe pumān, madhye napuṃsakam|
vāmamakṣi hrasati nāryāmantargatāyām, puruṣe dakṣiṇam, ubhe napuṃsake|
savyaṃ pādaṃ pūrvaṃ prakrāmati savyena cākṣṇā bhruvā ca pūrvaṃ prati kurute, ceṣṭate ca[na] savyena[sarvena] pārśvena prāyaḥ saṃviśati[śaṃsate] strīsaṃjñāneṣu ca prāyaśo dauhṛdaṃ kurute nāryāmantargatāyām|
viparyaye tadataḥ puruṣaṃ bibhartīti vidyāt||
atha iha[ye] khalu garbhasya ṣaḍbhyaḥ sthānebhyaḥ śarīramabhinirvartate|
tadyathā---jalakāyādvāyukāyāttejaḥkāyāt pṛthivīkāyādākāśakāyādrasakāyācceti||
ṣaḍdhāturevāyaṃ puruṣo bhavati|
dhātavaḥ punaḥ pañca bhūtāni brahma yadavyaktam[padavyaktam]||
tatra yat kharakaṭhinam, tadyathā---dantakeśaroma-nakha[mukha]-purīṣasnāyvasthi, gandhajñānaghrāṇasaṅghātagauravāṇīti|
yad dravaṃ snigdhaṃ mṛdu vā tadaudakam||
tadyadā[tadyamu] sannipatitayoryatra puruṣaḥ pūrvamarthaṃ[sarvamardha] nandayati jaghanyaṃ strī tatra pumānihāṅgapratyaṅgaiḥ[pumānipratyaṅgaiḥ] sadṛśo jāyate|
yatra tu strī prathamamarthaṃ sādhayati jaghanyaṃ puruṣaḥ, tatra strī vāṅgapratyaṅgais sadṛśī[sadṛśo] jāyate||
atha strīpuruṣāvṛtukāle[-nāle] rūkṣāṇi vātalānyannāni[vātaśānyannāni] sevete, vegāṃśca dhārayataḥ, tayorgarbhaḥ śoṇitādiṣu vātasaṃdūṣiteṣu niḥsṛto[viḥsṛto] bhavati, gadgadabādhiryaminmiṇatvamanyeṣāṃ ca vātapittavikārāṇāmanyatamaṃ prāpnoti|
evameva pittaśleṣmalānṛtukāle mātāpitroḥ sevamānayoḥ pittaśleṣmavidūṣito garbhaḥ sambhavati||
atha khalu garbhaśarīraṃ catrudaśendriyakāyāḥ samanupraviśanti|
vidhṛtāścānupalabhyamānāśca saptadivyāḥ saptamānuṣāḥ||
tatra divyāḥ brahmadeva[brahmadevataṃ]-varuṇagandharvapiśācāsuramahārajakāyā bhavanti|
tān vyākhyāsyāmaḥ|
tatra yaḥ satyārjavānṛśaṃsakṣamādam-adhyāna[advāyāna]-sampanno'dhyātmatattvadarśī bhavati taṃ brahmakāyamiti vidyāt||
yastādyaśīlopādānayadyatraiyavān? muditastaṃ deva kāyamiti vidyāt||
yo yajñananditarāgadṛṣṭiḥ salilapriyaścirasnāyī piṅgākṣaḥ kapilakeśaḥ saṃbhavati taṃ varuṇakāyamiti vidyāt||
yastu priyanṛtyagītavāditraḥ strīvihāranityaḥ śucivastragandhamālyānulepanaratirbhavati taṃ gandharvakāyāmiti vidyāt||
yastu priyamadyamāṃsamatsyastathā gomahāśano bībhatso bālānāṃ bhīṣayitā nidrābahulaśca bhavati taṃ piśācakāyamiti vidyāt||
yastvātmagurūṇāṃ[-gu] mānayitā paścād dveṣī caṇḍaḥ krodhano jñātīnāṃ bhedako bhavati tamāsuraṃ kāyamiti vidyāt||
yastu dhīraḥ śūraḥ mahābhogo mahotsāho mahaiśvaryaśca bhavati taṃ mahārājakāyamiti vidyāt||
anurāgeṇa mānuṣāstu pratyātmadarśanaśravaṇasparśanarasanaghrāṇasukhaduḥkhamiti tatpravidyāsahitāḥ kevalāścāvatiṣṭhante kārtsnyena|tairanvito janturliṅgati nimiṣati ākuṃcati prasārayati vedyaṃ vedayate||
sa[na] yadā bhedaṃ gacchati tadāyamantataḥ[tathāyaḥ antaḥ kāyameva yānti] jalaṃ jalakāyameva yāti, vāyurvāyukāyaṃ, tejaḥ tejaḥkāyaṃ, pṛthivī pṛthivīkāyaṃ, ākāśamākāśakāyamiti|
yathākāyam[dāyam]-indriyakāyaṃ[-kālaṃ] bhajate||
bhavati cātra--- vidyamāne śarīre vai dhāturdhātuṃ niyacchati |
mano buddhiśca sarveṣāṃ brahmaṇi pratitiṣṭhati|| ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele śārīre pañcamo'dhyāyaḥ||
||ṣaṣṭho'dhyāyaḥ||
athātaḥ khaḍḍikāṃ garbhāvakrāntiṃ śārīraṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iha khalu bho mātṛjaścāyaṃ garbhaḥ pitṛjaścātmajaśca sātmyajaśca rasajaśca|
asti ca sattvam aupapādakam[aupavādakam] ityātreyavacanam|
neti bharadvājaḥ |
amātṛjaścāyaṃ garbhaḥ apitṛjaścānātmajaśca-asātmyajaś[anātmajaś] cārasajaśca|
nāsti ca sattvam aupapādakam[aupavādhikam] iti|
yadi hi mātā putraṃ janyet bhūyiṣṭhaṃ hi strī putrakāmā maithunavargamabhisandhāya putraṃ janayet strīkāmā ca striyam||
yadā[-vi] jīvena saha sūkṣmāvagnimārutau garbhaṃ viśataḥ tadā[-va] dvāvetāvaṅgapratyaṅgāni vikurutaḥ,tau ceṣṭayataḥ[ceṣṭataḥ], tau vardhayataḥ, tāveva yadā śarīrād vyavakrāmataḥ tadā tadbhavati nirvāto nirūṣmā preto mṛta iti|
netyāha bharadvājaḥ|
mṛto'pi jantuḥ vāyunādhmāṣyate, agninā śoṣyate|
netyāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
saha vā tasyāgnimārutau jīvayataḥ, tayor apakrāntayor[apakrāntayorvātya] bahyāgnimārutāvāviśata iti||
yatpunarāḥ sati ca bhūtanānātve[bhūtarnātve] kathamekaḥ syāditi? atrocyate|
yatraitannānātvamāśritaṃ tadetadavyaktamasti pañcamahābhūtasaṃgraha iti||
yatpunarāha yadyavyaktaṃ sarvaṃ syādavikāraḥ syād iti[ya---sa vivasyādadhi], atrocyate|
vātapittaśleṣmakṛtā rasasamutthā asya vyaktāḥ procyante vikārāḥ, avyakte hyukte te[rute] vyaktāḥ kathamavyaktaṃ srakṣyanti, kathaṃ tarhi śarīre tadvikriyate, unmādaḥ kathamavyaktaṃ manaḥ spṛśatīty[spṛśatithena] etaccānupapannam|
kiṃ tarhi yathādityassambhūto'pi meghāntarito[memāmtarito] na prakāśamupajanayati tathā[---] mano'ntarhiteṣu vijñānasrotassu tamasā smṛtiṃ nopajanayatīti||
yatpunarāḥ yadyayaṃ paralokād garbho'vakrāmennāsya[garma ukta] kiñcittatrādṛṣṭaṃ syāditi, atrocyate|
iha tāvadayaṃ cirotsṛṣṭāni vijñānāni vividhāni cāścaryabhūtāni na smarati kiṃ punardehāntarāṇi bhūtāni bhāvaviśeṣā-antarāṇi[---]||
tatra ślokaḥ--- tamasā bhāvito yo vai saṃsmarenna sa mānavaḥ|
saṃsmaretpūrvacaritaṃ sukṛte vedavad[vedavidṛja] dvijaḥ||
ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele śārīre ṣaṣṭho'dhyāyaḥ||
||saptamo'dhyāyaḥ||
athātaḥ śarīrasaṃkhyāśārīraṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iha khalu śarīre ṣaṭ tvaco bhavanti|
udakadharā prathamā'sṛgdharā dvitīyā, sidhmakilāsasaṃbhava[maṃ] adhiṣṭhānā tṛtīyā, dadrukuṣṭhasambhavādhiṣṭhānā caturthī, alajīvidridhisambhavādhiṣṭhānā pañcamī, ṣaṣṭhī tu yasyāṃ chinnāyāmuttāmyati timiramivānupraviśati duṣṭāruṣkāṇi cāsya yāmāśritya jāyante iti||
asthigaṇanā---trīṇi saṣaṣṭīni[saṣaṣṭhiṇi śavā] śatānyasthnāṃ tadyathā---dvātriṃśaddantāḥ, dvātriṃśaddantolūkhalāni, viṃśatirnakhāḥ[paṇipādaśalānānyaṅgulyasthīni], ṣaṣṭiḥ pāṇipādāṅgulyasthīni, viṃśatiḥ pāṇipādaśalākāḥ, catvāri pāṇīpādaśalākādhiṣṭhānāni, dve pārṣṇyorasthinī[pārṣorasthīni], catvāraḥ pādayoḥ gulphāḥ, dvau maṇikau, māṇike dve hastayoḥ, catvāryaratnyorasthīni[catvāryaśayo], dve jaṅghayoḥ, dve jānunī, dve jānukapālike, dvāvūrunalakau[dāvūrudvāvūrunaśaktau], dvāvaṃsau, dve aṃsaphalake, dvāvakṣakau, ekaṃ jatru, dve tālunī, dve cibuke, dve śroṇiphalake, ekaṃ bhagāsthi, pañcacatvāriṃśat pṛṣṭhagatānyasthīni[pṛṣṭhagatau dhṛsthini], pañcadaśa grīvāyām, caturdaśorasi, caturviṃśatiḥ parśukāḥ pārśvayoḥ, tāvanti[yāvanti] caiva sthālakāni, tāvanti caiva sthālakārbudakāni, ekaṃ hanvasthi, dve hanubandhane, ekaṃ nāsāsthi-hanu[tathā hanu]-kūṭalalāṭam, catvāri śīrṣakapālāni||
hṛdayamekaṃ cetanāyatanam|
daśa prāṇāyatanāni tadyathā---mūrdhā kaṇṭho hṛdayaṃ gudo nābhirvastirojaḥ śauklaṃ śoṇitaṃ māṃsamiti||
pañcadaśa koṣṭhāṅgāni[koṣṭhāni]|
tadyathā---nābhiśca hṛdayaṃ ca kloma ca yakṛcca plīhā ca vṛkkau ca vastiśca purīṣādhānaṃ cāmāśayaścottaragudaścādharagudaśca kṣudrāntraṃ ca sthūlāntraṃ ca vapāvahanaṃ ceti||
ṣaṭpañcāśatpratyaṅgāni tadyathā---dvau gulphau, dve nitambe, dve jaṅghe, dve piṇḍike, dve ūrupiṇḍike, dvau sphicau, dvau vṛṣaṇau, ekaṃ śephaḥ, dvau śaṅkhau, dvau baṅkṣṇau, dvau kukundarau, eko vastiḥ, śīrṣamekamudaramekam, dvau stanau, dvau bāhū, dvāṃvasakau, ekaṃ cubukaṃ, dvāvoṣṭhau, dve dantaveṣṭe, dve sṛkvaṇī[sṛkvaṇīti], ekaṃ tālu, galaśuṇḍikā ekā, dvau karṇau, dve karṇaśaṣkulike, dvau gaṇḍau, dve akṣikūṭe, catvāryakṣivartmāni, dve akṣiṇī[akṣiṇīti] iti||
śarīradravyāṇī---daśodakasyāñjalayaḥ śarīre pracyavamānaṃ purīṣamanubadhvātyatiyoge, navāñjalayaḥ pūrvasyāhārapariṇāmadhātoryantaṃ rasa ity ācakṣate[ācakṣati] kuśalāḥ, aṣṭau śoṇitasya, sapta purīṣasya, catvāro mūtrasya, dvau medasaḥ, eko majjñaḥ, mastiṣkasyāñjaliḥ śuklasya ceti||
athātra praśno bhavati--- katamayaṃ jīvo[devo] dehāddehāntaramupakramata iti? atrovāca bhagavānātreyaḥ|
jalūkāyā ivāsya kecidgatiṃ bruvate|
tanna yuktam|
iha vyaktyantāmūrtaṃ yugapatsyādevāpare'pyevamicchanti|
sarvathāpi mumukṣorasyāyamantarātmā paramupakramata iti sarvathāpyasmin parityakte paricaye tāvadasaṃprāptaṃ tarā syāt|
avasthānatvāccettadiṣṭaṃ karmaṇopyevaṃ bhavati vaiyyarthyamapi tu khalu pratiśrutyāpahitaḥ paratra gamanaṃ tasya vidyāt|
athavā yathādityasya hṛdaye bhūmau raśmayaḥ pratitiṣṭhante|
vilambitāvevamatasya tatra gamanamanupaścediti||
atha khalu puruṣe[puruṣā] ṣoḍaśādhyātmadevatā bhavanti|
tadyathā---agniśca pṛthivī cāpaścākāśaśca vāyuśca vidyucca parjanyaśca indraśca[-ga] gandharvaśca mṛtyuścādityaśca candramāśca tvaṣṭā[tṛṣṇā] ca viṣṇuśca prajāpatiśca brahmā ceti|
tāḥ karmabhirvidyādutpattitaśca parimāṇataśca vidyāt|
ūṣmā hy atrāagneś[-gniś] ca karma dehe|
ghrāṇaṃ ca pṛthivyāḥ, sneho raso jñānaṃ codakasya, sparśanaṃ vāyoḥ, śrotraṃ cākāśasya, rūpādānaṃ parjanyasya saṃvegādānāni vidyutaḥ, balamindrasya, kāmo gandharvāṇāṃ, kopo mṛtyoḥ, cakṣurādityasya, prasādaścandramasaḥ, rūpaṃ tvaṣṭuḥ, ceṣṭā viṣṇoḥ, vyavāyaḥ prajāpateḥ, buddhirbrahmaṇa iti||
tatra ślokaḥ--- viśuddhanetrāstapaso munayaḥ śāntakalmaṣāḥ|
jagataścopapannāṃśca sarvān[svarbhā] paśyanti mānavān|| ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele śārīre saptamo'dhyāyaḥ||
||aṣṭamo'dhyāyaḥ||
athāto jātisūtrīyaṃ śārīraṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iha khalu bhoḥ yāḥ striyaḥ pathyalaghubhojinyo'nudāvartanaśīlā apraduṣṭā yathāgarbhāśayāḥ suviśuddhasrotaso bhavanti, tā ācakṣate avandhyāḥ iti kuśalāḥ|
tā iṣṭarūpaṃ medhāvi cāpatyaṃ janayanti, viparyaye viparyayaḥ||
ṛtau ca garbho'vatiṣṭhate|
tadyathā---sukṛṣṭakṣetre bījaṃ prakṣiptaṃ tatra vrīhiḥ vrīhitvāya kalpate, yavo yavatvāya evameva[priya] indriyamṛtukāle visṛṣṭamaduṣṭāyāṃ[-māmaṭṭa] yonau garbhāśayamupagacchati|
tadyathā[tadyathāya]---āvartagṛhītāḥ pratīpaṃ pratidhāvanti tadvat śuklaśoṇitaṃ garbhāśaye[garbhāśaya astikaṃ] āsiktaṃ kṣīramiva|
taccānekakanakamiva[taṃ cānekanakamivo]-udumbareṇa niṣiktamekatvamāpannamṛddhiṃ labhate||
sā ced evamākāṅkṣetaudvāhike[evayā kāṃkṣā-] śivāvadātaputraṃ janayeyam[janayanti] iti, yavānāṃ manthaṃ[mandaṃ] sarpissamyuktaṃ saptarātramanupahataṃ bhuñjīta|
tato dauhṛdinī[dauhṛtdvi] śvetāyā goḥ sarūpavatsāyāḥ payasi pāyasaṃ pācayitvā tadevāśnīyāt[-atmīyāt]|
śukle ca vāsasī paridadhyāt[parirabhyāt]|
sarvaśvetaṃ cāsyāḥ saṃskṛtaṃ vimānaṃ kārayet|
vṛṣabhamaśvaṃ[vṛṣabhagaga] vāsyā darśayet|
evamavadātaṃ putraṃ janayet|
netyāha śaunakaḥ|
paiṅgalyaṃ vā tato'pyatreti|
ātreya uvāca kiṃ syādāvādhakam, piṅgalāvayavayā āyuṣmanto nīrogāśca bhavantīti||
sā cedevam[iti hā] āśāsīta[āsita] śyāmaṃ lohitākṣaṃ putraṃ janayeyam[jana yetre yāvat dvitīye māsapuṣpaṃ paśyet yamiti] iti eṣāmeva[eṣāma] yavānāṃ manthaṃ[mandaṃ] kārayet lohitakukkuṭaraktena saptarātraṃ raktaśālīnāmodanamanupahataṃ bhuñjīta|
dauhṛde sā tāmreṇa ca vāsasā paridadhyāt|
tāmre cāsyāḥ śayanāsane dadyād raktavṛṣamaśvaṃ[-vṛṣamadaṃ] vāsyā darśayet|
evaṃ śyāmaṃ lohitākṣaṃ putraṃ janayati||
yā[yāvad] vi dvitīyamāsi puṣpaṃ paśyenna vāsyā[va] garbhastiṣṭhatīti vidyādajāta sārā[nā] hi tadā garbhavatyo bhavanti|
yasyāstu[tasyāstu] khalu yoniḥ[yoni] jātasāre puṣpam āsravati[āśraya] tasyāḥ patati vāpyatikālaṃ[vāpitya-] vā'vatiṣṭhate gabhaḥ pariśuṣkagātra iti||
caturthe khalu māse prativihite kṣīre navanītaṃ prāśnīyāt|
pañcame kṣīrayavāgūḥ, ṣaṣṭhe kṣīrasarpiḥ, saptame dadhyudaśvit, aṣṭame kṣīrasarpiḥ, navame tu khalu māse prativihite madhurauṣadhasiddhena[kadambamāṣṭerāghataitānuvāsa] tailenānuvāsaḥ||
evaṃ hyasyāḥ pratimāsaṃ[pratisaṃvartamānāyā yacchatanmataṃ] vartamānāyāḥ kukṣīkaṭīpārśvapṛṣṭhaṃ mṛdūbhavati jīrṇapurīṣaṃ cādhaḥ travetsukhaṃ ca prajāyate||
saptame tu khalu māse garbhaprapīḍitā vātapittaśleṣmāṇa[-śleṣmāṇo] uraḥ prāpya savidāhaṃ[vidahyamānāḥ] kaṇḍūṃ janayanti|tena kikkisāni jāyante strīṇām|
triphalācūrṇaṃ śaśarudhireṇa piṣṭvā tenāsyāḥ tānyālepayet|| aṣṭame tu khalu māse prativihite sati sūkikāgāraṃ samyak prāgdvāramudakdvāraṃ vā kārayet tinduka-palāśa[phalāśa]-aśvatthaiḥ|| athātra pūrvasaṃkalpitāḥ syuḥ dvau khalu bilvamayau paryaṅkau śūrpau ca dvau taṇḍulamusalau gaṇḍopadhānaṃ yavāgūḥ[mayau] sarpiśca tailaṃ ca sarṣapāśceti||
athaināṃ prajanayiṣyatīti yavāgūṃ pāyayet|
anāgatagarbhavedanāṃ caināmavahananaṃ ca kārayet|
sā yadi jānīyādavabhraṣṭo me kukṣiḥ pravimukto me hṛdgarbhaḥ prasrutā me yoniriti, athaināmupakārikalpitanakhāścatasra upajīvikāḥ[upajīvā] pratidinamupatiṣṭheyuḥ|
śanaiḥ pūrvaṃ pravāhetapaścād[pravāhetatpaścād] balavattaramiti|na cāpyanāgatavegā vā bāliśatayā[bābāhūhlitatayādyā], anāgatavegā hi duḥkhāyātmānaṃ[tanā duḥkhā] prayacchati saha putreṇa||
tasyāścet prajātāyā[prajātāyāparānapra] aparā na prapadyate, tadaināṃ raktaśālīnāmakṣamātraṃ kalkamamlena[kalpya] mūtreṇa[mūtreṇa] vā pāyayet|
etenaiva kalpena dantīdravantīvṛścikālīvunarnavāvanaśīrṣākaṃ kārayet|
tathā[talau]-umākāla-sarpa[sarva]-purāṇamālānāmanyatamena dhūpayet|
sā cedanena vidhinā na pravartate athaināṃ tīkṣṇaphala-tailena[taire]-anuvāsayet||[ayamadhyāyaḥ mātukāyāmardhe vicchinno dṛśyate|]
... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ...
... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ...
iti bhele śārīre'ṣṭame'dhyāyaḥ||
iti bhelasaṃhitāyāṃ, śārīrasthānaṃ samāptam||

................dve vā jāta eva vinaśyati|
hrasvaikapakṣo vitato yastu garbhastu susvaraḥ|
māsamekaṃ tu jīvitvā vināśāyopapadyate||
piṇḍīśirā dīrghahanuranalpe saṃhate bhruvau|
dvitīye māsi saṃprāpte mriyate garbha īdṛśaḥ||
jātamātrasya garbhasya vyañjanaṃ yatra dṛśyate|
tṛtīye māsi saṃprāpte mriyate garbha īdṛśaḥ||
trikekaro ghaṭaśirā yastu garbhastu susvaraḥ|
caturthe māsi saṃprāpte vināśamupagacchati||
yasya ṣoḍaśavarśasya vyañjanaṃ tūpajāyate|
śīghraṃ ca pañcadhātutvādalpāyuriha dṛśyate||
yasya viṃśativarṣasya vyañjanaṃ tūpajāyate|
sa dīrghamāyurāpnoti puruṣo nātra saṃśayaḥ||
śarīrāpacayo'buddhirapathyāni balaṃ tathā|
bālasya yasya dṛśyante taṃ gatāyuṣamādiśet||
yasya jātasya jāyete gulmakāstunakau tathā|
aṣṭāviṃśe gate varṣe sarvaṃ taṃ hanti mānavam||
pṛṣṭhe corasi cāvartassavyatho yastu dṛśyate|
catvāriṃśe gate varṣe maraṇāyopakalpate||
āvarto yaśca pṛṣṭhe tu sannatastriṣu dṛśyate|
phalamasya tu vijñeyaṃ sthāpanaṃ na prasūyate||
sthūlāsthūlāṅgulī pāṇī nakhā yasyāpi lohitāḥ|
kalyāṇadeśapratyaṅgaḥ pañcāśadvarṣa eva ca||
vyūṭhorasko dīrghabhujaḥ sthūlajānuśca yo bhavet|
dīrghāṅgulirdīrghanakhaḥ ṣaṣṭi varṣāṇi jīvati||
utpīḍitasvaraṃ vāpi tuṅganāsakaconmukham|
ūrukaṃ bhadrasampannaṃ vidyātsaptatikaṃ naraḥ||
bhadraṃ puraśca paścācca ṛjukaṃ priyavādinam|
aśītikaṃ naraṃ vidyātkṛṣṇātreyavaco yathā||
lalāṭaṃ nāsikā karṇau yasyaitāni pṛthak pṛthak|
ṣaḍaṅgulapramāṇāni sa jīvati śataṃ samāḥ||
yasyākuñcitameva syājjānubhyāṃ samitaṃ śiraḥ|
ūrdhvajānugatau karṇau pautraṃ paśyatyayaṃ naraḥ||
mahāntau vipulau karṇau bhavetāṃ romaśau tathā|
snigdhau bahulakeśaśca sa ceha śatamṛcchati||
dharmeṇa satyavākyena guruśuśrūṣaṇena ca|
rasāyanopayogācca sa dīrghamanujīvati||
ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele indriye prathamo'dhyāyaḥ||
||dvitīyo'dhyāyaḥ||
mukhaṃ netre śarīraṃ ca pāṇipādaṃ tathaiva ca|
saraktaṃ dṛśyate yasya sa vai svastho bhaviṣyati|| yasya gomayacūrṇābhaṃ narāṇāṃ mūrdhni dṛśyate|
śirasi snihyamāne ca kaṇḍūssaṃjāyate dṛḍham||
yasya keśāḥ pramucyante śīrṇamūlāḥ śarīriṇaḥ|
yasyaitāni tu rūpāṇi sa vai svastho bhaviṣyati||
nāsya dantāḥ prahṛṣyanti mukhaṃ ca na vilupyati|
nābaddhaṃ bhāṣate cāpi sa vai svastho bhaviṣyati||
na vikṣipati gātrāṇi svaro'sya na vivartate|
vastreṇa gūhate guhyaṃ sa vai svastho bhaviṣyati||
na bhavatyunnato nābhiryathāvastho'bhitiṣṭhati|
sukhaṃ śvasiti rātrau ca sa vai svastho bhaviṣyati||
na nakhāḥ karkaśābhāsā na śyāvā na ca niṣprabhāḥ|
prasannāssuprabhāścaiva sa vai svastho bhaviṣyati|| ātapādhiṣṭhito yastu na ... prakāśace|
na ca lohitasaṃyu.......................... ..........................................
||tṛtīyo'dhyāyaḥ||
........................................................... ...........................................................
....dhitasvapne tāṃ rātriṃ nātivartate||
iyaṃ me śiṣṭaśibikā vaiḍūryamaṇiyantritā|
evaṃ pralāpayeccārto gatāyuriti taṃ viduḥ||
prajvalatyapi yo dīpe tama evābhipaśyati|
(śabdānvipratibudhyeta cāpūrvāniva nāsti saḥ)||
āvākśirāḥ pralambāmi nāma mā parivartaya|
manujaḥ pralapannevaṃ saptāhaṃ nātivartate||
marmāṇi dalitānīva yo'bhīkṣṇaṃ cātisāryate|
pravāhamāṇo durgandhi kuṇapaṃ pūtikaṃ tathā||
tacca lohitagandhaṃ vāpyathavā matsyagandhikam|
kṛṣṇaṃ nīlaṃ vivarṇaṃ vā mumūrṣuśca sa hocyate||
madhumehī vasāmehī sarpirmehī ca yo naraḥ|
bahumehī ca yo jantuḥ sa vai proktaḥ parāsukaḥ||
prameheta yadā janturbinduṃ binduṃ savedanam|
vāyunā bhinnavastiḥ syād durlabhaṃ tasya jīvitam||
krimayo bahavo yasya nissaranti śarīriṇaḥ|
āturasya śayānasya nauṣadhaṃ tasya sidhyati||
ityetāni bhiṣak dṛṣṭvā lakṣaṇāni mumūrṣatām|
na cikitsāṃ prayuñjīta yathāmārgaṃ cikīrṣakaḥ||
ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele indriye tṛtīyo'dhyāyaḥ||
||caturtho'dhyāyaḥ||
yadāturasya hṛdayaṃ vāyuḥ saṃgṛhya tiṣṭhati|
dhamanīssaṃparīpīḍya sadyo jahyātsa jīvitam|
āturasya yadā vāyuḥ śarīramanupadyate|
uttāne netraniṣyandaḥ sadyo jahyātsa jīvitam||
yasya koṣṭhagato vāyurupāvṛttaśśarīriṇaḥ|
kṣīṇalohitamāṃsasya sadyo jahyātsa jīvitam||
yasyāturasyeha vātādvātāṣṭhīlā vivardhate|
na saṃsarati cānyatra sadyaḥ prāṇān jahāti saḥ||
yasyobhe piṇḍike stabdhe nāsā jihmā ca lakṣyate|
vyāvṛtte cākṣiṇī yasya sadyaḥ prāṇān jahāti saḥ||
āmāśayasamutthānā yasyaiva parikartikā|
tṛṣṇā ca tīvrarāgaśca sadyaḥ prāṇān jahāti saḥ||
pakvāśayasamutthānā yasya syātparikartikā|
tṛṣṇā galagrahaścograḥ sadyo jahyātsa jīvitam||
śoṇitaṃ romakūpebhyo yasya kṣarati dehinaḥ|
atīva mukhato bhedi sadyo jahyātsa jīvitam||
hṛdayasya tu saṃghātaṃ parikṣīṇasya dehinaḥ|
atyarthaṃ pīḍayecchūlaṃ sadyaḥ prāṇān jahāti saḥ||
yasya kṣīṇaśarīrasya saṃjñāṃ harati mārutaḥ|
vyāhanti ca mahāsrotaḥ sadyaḥ prāṇān jahāti saḥ||
hṛdayasya tu saṃghātaṃ parikṣīṇasya dehinaḥ|
etairevaṃvidhairliṅgairanyaiścāpi tathāvidhaiḥ|| prakṛtervikṛtiṃ prāptaṃ parīkṣetāturaṃ bhiṣak|
ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele indriye caturtho'dhyāyaḥ||
||pañcamo'dhyāyaḥ||
athāto yasyaśyāvīyaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
yasya śyāve ume netre dṛśyete harite tathā|
utpannaśca śirorogaḥ phalitaṃ tasya jīvitam||
haritāśca sirā yasya romakūpāśca lohitāḥ|
bhuṅkte vānnāni sāmlāni tatraiva sa vinaśyati||
yasyordhvavātaḥ kopena jantorāmāśayaṃ gataḥ|
hṛdayaṃ parigṛhṇāti paretaṃ tasya jīvitam||
gātre ca pāṇipāde ca yasya śuṣyati śoṇitam|
muhurmuhurnṛtyati ca paretaṃ tasya jīvitam||
vṛṣaṇau pāṇipādau ca yasya śuṣkaṃ mukhaṃ tathā|
chaviśca śoṣamāyāti paretaṃ tasya jīvitam||
hanū hastau ca pādau ca vṛṣaṇau liṅgameva ca|
dṛśyate dehino yasya paretaṃ tasya jīvitam||
hṛdayaṃ dahyate yasya mumūrṣuṃ taṃ samādiśet|
āturastu sa yāto vai paretaṃ tasya jīvitam||
apasmāraḥ kṣayaḥ kuṣṭhaṃ raktapittamathodaram|
gulmaśca madhumehaśca dīrgharogā bhavanti te||
balamāṃsakṣayo yasya dehino dīrgharogiṇaḥ|
dṛśyeta svarahāniśca paretaṃ tasya jīvitam||
hṛdayaṃ dahyate yasya koṣṭhe śūlaṃ svarakṣayaḥ|
abhīkṣṇaṃ dahyate cāpi paretaṃ tasya jīvitam||
trāsābhitāpā jantūnāṃ koṣṭhe śūlāścaranti ca|
hikkācchardiparītaśca paretaṃ tasya jīvitam||
vāpitaṃ jvalitaṃ martyaḥ, salilaṃ hi hutāśanam|
bhāskaraṃ manyate somaṃ durlabhaṃ tasya jīvitam||
yaścāpi vimale sūrye meghān paśyati sarvaśaḥ|
durdinaṃ sudinaṃ cāpi paretaṃ tasya jīvitam||
yaṃ rasā nāvatiṣṭhante bheṣajaṃ cendriyāṇi tu|
yasya vā viparītāni na ca jīvati tādṛśaḥ||
vānaspatyaphalaṃ mūlaṃ rogaspṛṣṭasya yasya vai|
bhaiṣajyārthaṃ na dṛśyeta na ca jīvati tādṛśaḥ||
ityetāni bhiṣag dṛṣṭvā lakṣaṇāni mumūrṣatām|
na cikitsāṃ prayuñjīta yaśomārgapratīkṣayā|| ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele indriye pañcamo'dhyāyaḥ||
||ṣaṣṭho'dhyāyaḥ||
athātaḥ pūrvarūpīyaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
antarlohitakāyastu bahiḥ pāṇḍuḥ prakāśate|
pūrvarūpaṃ tadācaṣṭe mānavasya mumūrṣataḥ||
bahirlohitakāyastu pāṇḍurantaḥ prakāśate|
pūrvarūpamanuprāptaḥ sa mṛtyorucyate naraḥ||
antarglāno bahiḥ pīno bahirādhmāta eva ca|
yaścātyantasamādhmātaḥ sarve'pyete parāsukāḥ||
abhīkṣṇaṃ jvaryate yastu nivātamabhinandati|
anuṣaktapratiśyāyaḥ kṣipraṃ śvāsena hanyate||
ninādī vā pralāpī vā hasatyatyarthameva ca|
unmādena kṛśo jantuḥ pañcatvamupagacchati||
ghanaṃ saśūlaṃ yo veda sadāhaṃ hṛdayaṃ naraḥ|
hṛdrogeṇa kṛśo janturvināśamupagacchati||
prasvidyate ca kaṇḍūmān yo viruddhaṃ ca sevate|
avirecanaśīlaśca kuṣṭhena sa vinaśyati||
sukumāraśca yo jantuḥ snehaṃ māṃsaṃ ca sevate|
divā svapiti cābhīkṣṇaṃ sa pramehī vinaśyati||
pariśūnaśca yo jantuḥ svede ca paruṣacchaviḥ|
bhinnaṃ yaścopaviśati so'tisāreṇa hanyate||
yasyāgniśca balaṃ caiva nālpaṃ bhavati dehinaḥ|
kṣīṇalohitamāṃsasya yathā pretastathaiva saḥ||
sadyo raktaṃ śiro yasya pītakaṃ vā pradṛśyate|
kapilaṃ pluṣṭakeśaṃ vā yathā pretastathaiva saḥ||
yasya netre lalāṭaṃ ca mukhaṃ nāsāṃ bhruvau tathā|
jihmāni kurute vāyuryathā pretastathaiva saḥ||
śyāvā kaṇṭakinī jihvā yasya śuṣkā pradṛśyate|
śyāve netre nakhāścāpi yathā pretastathaiva saḥ||
yasya nirbhidyate kaṇṭhaḥ tāmyatyuccaiśśarīriṇaḥ|
bahirāyāmabhājastaṃ pratyācakṣīta paṇḍitaḥ||
yasyordhvakāye balavān navo rogastu duṣkriyaḥ|
pūrvarūpaṃ tathā vācyaṃ mānavasya mariṣyataḥ||
yasya cucchundarīgandhaḥ puruṣasya bhavatyatha|
sauvarṇānapi vṛkṣāṃśca yo veda sa vinaśyati||
ityebhirīdṛśaiścānyairvikārairvarṇitaṃ naraḥ|
nopakrameta medhāvī ya icchedātmanaḥ sukham|| ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele indriye ṣaṣṭho'dhyāyaḥ||
||saptamo'dhyāyaḥ||
athāta indriyāṇīkamindriyaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
indriyāṇi yathā jantoḥ parīkṣeta viśeṣataḥ|
āyuḥpramāṇaṃ jijñāsurbhiṣak tanme nibodhata||
annapānātparīkṣeta darśanādyaiśca tattvataḥ|
arthādivihitaṃ jñānamindriyāṇāmatīndriyam||
svasthebhyo vikṛtaṃ yasya jñānamindriyasaṃśrayam|
alakṣitaṃ nimittena lakṣaṇaṃ maraṇe hi tat||
ityuktaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ samyagindriyeṣvaśubhodayam|
tadeva tu punarbhūyo vistareṇa nibodha me||
ghanībhūtamivākāśaṃ paśyaṃstamiva medinīm|
vigītaṃ hyubhayaṃ hyetatpaśyanmaraṇamṛcchati||
yasya darśanamāyāti māruto'mbaragocaraḥ|
agnirnāyāti vā dīptaḥ tasyāpi kṣayamādiśet||
jale'pi nirmale jālamajālaṃ manute naraḥ|
sthire gacchati vā dṛṣṭvā jīvitātparihīyate||
jāgratpaśyati yaḥ pretān rakṣāṃsi vividhāni ca|
anyadvāpyadbhutaṃ kiṃcinna sa jīvati tādṛśaḥ||
yo'gniṃ prakṛtivarṇasthaṃ nīlaṃ paśyati niṣprabham|
kṛṣṇaṃ vā yadi vā śuklaṃ na sa jīvati mānavaḥ||
marīcīnasato meghe meghānvāpyamalāmbare|
vidyuto vā vinā meghānna sa jīvati mānavaḥ||
mṛṇmayīmiva vā pātrīṃ kṛṣṇāṃ vāpi purīṣitām|
ādityamardhacandraṃ vā kṣipraṃ dṛṣṭvā vinaśyati||
naktaṃ sūryamahaścandramavahnau dhūmamutthitam|
agniṃ vā niṣprabhaṃ dṛṣṭvā rātrau, maraṇamādiśet||
prabhāvataḥ prabhāṃ hīnāṃ niṣprabhānvā prabhāvataḥ|
narān viliṅgān paśyanti bhāvān bhāvajighāṃsavaḥ||
vyākṛtīni ca varṇāni visaṃkhyopacitāni ca|
nimittāni ca paśyanti rūpāṇyāyuḥparikṣayāt||
yastu paśyatyadṛśyaṃ vā dṛśyaṃ yastu na paśyati|
tāvubhau gacchataḥ kṣipraṃ yamakṣayamasaṃśayam||
aśabdasya ca yaḥ śrotā śabdaṃ yaśca na vindati|
dvāvapyetau yathā pretau tathā jñeyau vijānatā||
viparyayeṇa yo vidyād gandhānāṃ caiva nāma tam|
na vā tatsarvato vidyādvidyāttaṃ vai gatāyuṣam||
yo rasaṃ na vijānāti vipakvaṃ vā ca tattvataḥ|
apakvaṃ dṛśyate, pakvaṃ tamāhuḥ kuśalā narāḥ||
uṣṇān śītān kharān ślakṣṇān mṛdūnapi ca dāruṇān|
spṛśyān spṛṣṭvā tato'nyacca mumūrṣusteṣu manyate||
antareṇa tapastīvraṃ yogaṃ vā vidhipūrvakam|
indriyairadhikaṃ paśyan pañcatvamupapadyate||
indriyāṇāmṛte dṛṣṭerindriyārthānnadoṣajān|
naraḥ paśyati yaḥ kaścidindriyairna sa jīvati||
svasthāḥ prajñāviparyāsairindriyārtheṣu vaikṛtam|
paśyanti ye tu bahuśaḥ teṣāṃ maraṇamādiśet||
etadeva ca vijñānaṃ yassamyaganupaśyati|
maraṇaṃ jīvitaṃ caiva sa bhiṣag jñātumarhati||
ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele indriye saptamo'dhyāyaḥ||
||aṣṭamo'dhyāyaḥ||
athāto dūtādhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
tṛṇānnakhānvā chindan vai bhiṣajaṃ paripṛcchati|
āturasya yadā dūtaḥ pratyākhyeyastathāvidhaḥ||
viplutaṃ bhāṣamāṇaśca bhiṣajaṃ paripṛcchati|
āturasya yadā dūtaḥ pratyākhyeyastathāvidhaḥ||
bhinatti kāṣṭhaṃ kāṣṭhena loṣṭaṃ loṣṭhena vāpyadhaḥ|
āturasya yadā dūtaḥ pratyākhyeyastathāvidhaḥ||
spṛśannaṅgāni bālāṃśca bhiṣajaṃ paripṛcchati|
āturasya yadā dūtaḥ pratyākhyeyastathāvidhaḥ||
pidhāya pāṇinā nābhiṃ bhiṣajaṃ paripṛcchati|
āturasya yadā dūtaḥ pratyākhyeyastathāvidhaḥ||
kapālikāṃ śarkarāṃ vā bhinattyaṅgārikāmapi|
āturasya yadā dūtaḥ pratyākhyeyastathāvidhaḥ||
āste bhūmau pariśrānto gṛhṇātyaṅgamatha bhraman|
āturasya yadā dūtaḥ pratyākhyeyastathāvidhaḥ||
naṣṭaṃ mṛtamatikrāntaṃ nānuśocanti paṇḍitāḥ|
ityāturasya hi yadā vadeddūto na so'sti vai||
karaṃ kareṇa gṛhṇāti pāṇinā tāḍayetkaram|
āturasya yadā dūtaḥ pratyākhyeyastathāvidhaḥ||
khādedoṣṭhau ca jihvāṃ ca nakhān dantaiśca kalpayet|
āturasya yadā dūtaḥ pratyākhyeyastathāvidhaḥ||
āturasya yadā gehe vaidye vai paryupasthite|
chidyate bhidyate caiva pratyākhyeyastathāvidhaḥ||
parāvartya ghaṭaṃ pūrṇaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ paripṛcchati|
āturasya yadā dūtaḥ pratyākhyeyastathāvidhaḥ||
gṛdhraḥ sṛgālaḥ kākaścāpyulūko vāyasastathā|
nadeyurdakṣiṇe pārśve rogiṇo yasya nāsti saḥ||
kaṣāyavastro muṇḍo vā jaṭilo vātha nagnakaḥ|
carmabhirvā parivṛto, mahānasyeva śāṭikaḥ||
tailābhyaktaśchinnanāso vāgmī conmatta eva vā|
bhagnauṣṭhaḥ kharavāṭo vā na dūtaḥ sampraśasyate||
iti dūtasamācāro vyādhitānāṃ prakīrtitaḥ|
ya evaṃ veda nipuṇaṃ siddhikāmaḥ sa vai bhiṣak||
ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele indriye aṣṭamo'dhyāyaḥ||
||navamo'dhyāyaḥ||
athāto gomayacūrṇaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
cūrṇaṃ śirasi yasyaiva śuṣkagomayasannibham|
snehino dṛśyate jantormāsāddehaṃ jahāti saḥ||
kṛśasya kapharogeṇa yasya śleṣmaparikṣayaḥ|
karṇau raktau mukhaṃ caiva dvau māsau nātivartate||
yasya lohitakābhāsamambu tāluni dṛśyate|
kṣīṇalohitamāṃsasya sa māsaṃ nātivartate||
arundhatīṃ na paśyettu sthitāṃ saptarṣisaṃsadi|
sa māsādaṣṭamānmartyaḥ kṣipraṃ prāṇairvimucyate||
aṇukābhiśca kṛṣṇābhirāsyaṃ jihvā ca tāluke|
sarvatassamanucchinnaṃ na sa jīvati tādṛśaḥ||
śīrṣābhitāpino yasya śleṣmarogavatastathā|
hikkā viniṣyandate vai nāyamastīti nirdiśet||
yasya lomāni keśāśca pluṣyantīva śarīriṇaḥ|
saṃhṛṣṭānīva vā dehī na sa jīvati tādṛśaḥ||
yasya kālāntare dantā dṛśyante raktasannibhāḥ|
niṣprabhāścānuliptā vā na sa jīvati tādṛśaḥ||
kṣāreṇa vidhṛtaṃ gātraṃ dṛśyate yasya dehinaḥ|
samamuṣṇe ca śīte ca na sa jīvati tādṛśaḥ||
gātreṣu kharavarṇeṣu yasya vārilavaplavaḥ|
anabhyakteṣu gātreṣu na sa jīvati tādṛśaḥ||
śūlamaṅge bhavedyasya sphuṭitaṃ raktamāsravet|
adho golakasaṃkāśaṃ paretaṃ tasya jīvitam||
āpāṇḍu madhumehe tu yaśca mehati mānavaḥ|
abhyantareṇa pañcāhātsa pañcatvaṃ hi gacchati||
aratiścāvipākaśca kārśyadaurbalyameva ca|
yasya saṃdṛśyate jantorna sa jīvati tādṛśaḥ||
yastu dīnamanātho vā balena parihīyate|
bhidyāmārogamāpnoti yathā pretastathaiva saḥ||
anuvṛttau yathā jantuḥ pittena parimūrcchitaḥ|
saṃmūḍhavākyo bhavati yathā pretastathaiva saḥ||
yastvāsane'tha śayane ratiṃ na labhate naraḥ|
sa śīghraṃ kurute kālaṃ yaśca sāśrūṇi khādati||
āruhya vānaraṃ yastu saṃkalpaṃ nāvabudhyate|
tamāhuḥ paralokāya svapne tu kuśalā narāḥ||
parisaṃvatsarādyasya jvaro nāpaiti dehinaḥ|
uṣṇo vā yadi vā śīto yathā pretastathaiva saḥ||
yasya jātapramehasya piṭakā pāṇḍurā bhavet|
sopadravā śatapadā yathā pretastathaiva saḥ||
yasyordhvaṃ kramate vāyuḥ śrotraṃ vādhaḥ pravartate|
sarvāṇi ca prabhidyante yathā pretastathaiva saḥ||
ityetairlakṣaṇairyuktaṃ bhiṣak dṛṣṭvaiva mānavam|
nopakramettathā vīra rakṣannātmayaśaḥ sphuṭam||
ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele indriye navamo'dhyāyaḥ||
||daśamo'dhyāyaḥ||
athātaśchāyādhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
anucchāyāthavā jantordṛḍhacchāyāthavā punaḥ|
vicchinnā yasya vā chāyā na sa jīvati tādṛśaḥ||
yo vidyutamivākāśe vyabhre paśyati mānavaḥ|
dhūmāyate śiraśchāyā yasya nāstīti taṃ viduḥ||
lākṣāraktaṃ yathā vastramevaṃ paśyati yo mahīm|
athavā raktamākāśaṃ raktapittena hanyate||
yo hṛṣṭaromā puruṣaḥ kāsena śleṣmaṇācitaḥ|
kaṇṭhaśca śūkānugato yasya nāstīti taṃ viduḥ||
yasya śaṅkhāccyutaṃ māṃsaṃ śyāve netre tathaiva ca|
cūrṇakaśca mukhe jātaḥ paretaṃ tasya jīvitam||
yasya hastāccyutaṃ māṃsaṃ jantordṛśyeta kuṣṭhinaḥ|
athāviprayuktasya na sa jīvati mānavaḥ||
avipakvaṃ vipakvaṃ vā bhuktaṃ bhuktaṃ yathā bhavet|
kāśaśvāsajvaraiḥ spṛṣṭo nāsti tasya cikitsitam||
hṛdayaṃ pūrvamāvāti yasya snātasya dehinaḥ|
ardhamāsātparaṃ tasya jīvitaṃ nātivartate||
ūrdhvaśvāsahato yastu raktaṃ pracchardayennaraḥ|
śūlaṃ vā bhinnakoṣṭhasya na sa jīvati mānavaḥ||
antardāho'dhiko yasya śītārtiścāpi bāhyataḥ|
ākāśaṃ paripūrṇaṃ vā vetti yo na sa jīvati||
yasya pakvāvubhāvoṣṭhau nīlau jambūphalopamau|
ucchūnaṃ yasya ca śiraḥ paretaṃ tasya jīvitam||
yasyocchūnaṃ bhavenmadhyamubhāvaṃsau kṛśau tathā|
viriktaḥ punarādhmāti yathā pretastathaiva saḥ||
etadindriyavijñānaṃ yassamyaganupaśyati|
sa jīvitaṃ ca mṛtyuṃ ca nṛṇāṃ vidyādbhiṣagvaraḥ|| ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele indriye daśamo'dhyāyaḥ||
||ekadaśo'dhyāyaḥ||
athātaḥ puṣpīyaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
śirasyaṅge raktavarṇaṃ yo'nilaṃ vāpi paśyati|
ghaṭikāmekavarṇaṃ vā sa puṣpita ihocyate||
aṣṭāpadaṃ vā sukṛtaṃ jagatīṃ yaḥ prapaśyati|
sa dṛṣṭiparihīnatvātpuṣpitaḥ procyate naraḥ||
suptaśca saṃvṛte gehe paśyatyākāśameva yaḥ|
romanthayati dantaiśca sa puṣpita ihocyate||
dīpyamānamivākāśaṃ pṛthivīṃ ca vanāni ca|
yo vetti romasaṃhṛṣṭaḥ puṣpitaḥ sa ihocyate||
anullipto yathā dehī vāti cetkuṇapaṃ yathā|
sevante makṣikāścaiva puṣpitaḥ sa ihocyate||
avijñātā naraṃ nārī svapna eva nivāsinī|
dakṣiṇāṃ diśamehīti yaṃ brūyānna sa jīvati||
prakīrṇakeśo vikaraḥ svapne yo dakṣiṇāṃ diśam|
pratipadyeta tatraiva na sa jīvati tādṛśaḥ||
kuśairiva nirūḍhāṅgamātmānaṃ svapna īkṣate|
khaṃ vā sadhūmaṃ yo vetti na sa jīvati tādṛśaḥ||
yaḥ svapne vṛkṣamāruhya na rogī trāyate gṛhe|
prakīrṇakeśo vikacaḥ yo'gnimāruhya roditi||
prāsādamekasthūṇaṃ tu svapne yaścādhirohati|
naro nṛtyati paṅke vā sa pumānna ca jīvati||
svapne prāsādamāruhya mahāntaṃ kāñcanaṃ tathā|
yo nṛttagītapānānnī na sa jīvati mānavaḥ||
gajenoṣṭreṇa vā gacchan yaḥ paśyeddakṣiṇāṃ diśam|
jīvettu rogī saptāhaṃ, nīrogaḥ śaradāṃ śatam||
yāti yo durdine vāpi svapne vā dakṣiṇāṃ diśam|
pratibudhyeta tatraiva na sa jīvati tādṛśaḥ||
bhāsairbarhivarāhaiśca śvabhirmahiṣavājibhiḥ|
samaṃ yo taddivā yone svapne yogaphalaṃ bhavet||
yadā gaṇḍaṃ ca khaḍgaṃ ca svapne cāśnaṃśca paśyati|
prakīrṇakeśamapi ca na sa jīvati tādṛśaḥ||
yaḥ svapne puruṣaḥ kālaṃ pītaṃ vā vastramṛcchati|
gāyannaceṣṭayanvāpi na sa jīvati tādṛśaḥ||
yaḥ svapne vītakāmo'pi pītāṃ pibati vāruṇīm|
citrakaṇṭhaguṇo vāpi na sa jīvati tādṛśaḥ||
ākulāpāṃ nadīṃ svapne yovagāheta mānavaḥ|
jīvetsarogassaptāhaṃ, nīrogasśaradāṃ śatam||
svapne snātānuliptasya bhraṃśedyasya tu varṇakaḥ|
gātrasya puruṣasyeha sa śastreṇa vinaśyati||
etadindriyavijñānaṃ yassamyaganupaśyati|
jīvitaṃ caiva mṛtyuṃ ca nṛṇāṃ vidyādvicakṣaṇaḥ|| ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele indriye ekādaśo'dhyāyaḥ||
||dvādaśo'dhyāyaḥ||
athāto'vākcitīyaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
avākcitā vā jihmā vā yasya chāyālpikā bhāvet|
netre ca viṣame syātāṃ paretaṃ tasya jīvitam||
yadi dīnāni pakṣmāṇi na nimīlanti dehinaḥ|
dahyete nayane vāpi paretaṃ tasya jīvitam||
namantyau yasya dṛśyete bhruvau mūrdhani bā sthite|
jīvettu sa tryahaṃ rogī ṣaṇmāsān vyādhivarjitaḥ||
luñcyamāneṣu keśeṣu vedayannāti vedanām|
nivṛttasukhaduḥkhaḥ syātparetaṃ tasya jīvitam||
yasyāturasya piṭakā vyaṅgo vā dṛśyate mukhe|
adṛṣṭapūrvaṃ prathamaṃ paretaṃ tasya jīvitam||
śuṣyate nāsikāvaṃśaḥ pṛthutvaṃ yasya gacchati|
aṃso'nilāt kāsavataḥ paretaṃ tasya jīvitam||
atyuṣṇaṃ vātiśītaṃ vā stabdhaṃ vā mṛdu vāpyatha|
manyate pāṇipādaṃ ca paretaṃ tasya jībitam||
yo'vatīrya nadīṃ pūrṇāṃ toye paśyati jālakam|
gātraṃ liptamathādbhiśca yasya nāstīti taṃ viduḥ||
vivartayati yaḥ śīrṣamatyarthaṃ ca nasā naraḥ|
na svidyate lalāṭaṃ ca nāsti tasya cikitsitam||
jvaryate kāsate vāpi tathocchavasiti vai dṛḍham|
ākramyate tāmyate ca yathā pretastathaiva saḥ||
yasyodaraṃ samādhmātaṃ tadvartma ca vibhāvyate|
bhinnaṃ purīṣaṃ tṛṣṇā ca yathā pretastathaiva saḥ||
anārataṃ gṛhe yasya kāṃsyaṃ bhidyeta dehinaḥ|
candrastīkṣṇo mṛduścārko yasya syāttaṃ vivarjayet||
apradhātuḥ pradhāturvā svastho vā yadi vā''āturaḥ|
yaścandanamivāvāti na sa jīvati tādṛśaḥ||
yūthikotpannagaṇḍaśca, vāti yaścāpi vartmavat|
abhaktaḥ tasimaṃ bālye yo vāti na sa jīvati||
dviṣadbhiḥ kurute sakhyaṃ yaḥ priyairyāti vipriyam|
akasmātkupyate yastu paretaṃ tasya jīvitam||
ebhirevaṃvidhairliṅgairanyaiścāpi yathāyatham|
nopakrameta medhāvī bhiṣaganvitamāturam||
etadbhiṣagariṣṭānāṃ yo jñānamanubudhyate|
yathoktaṃ veda vedāyuḥ sa bhiṣak śāstrakovidaḥ||
ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele indriye dvādaśo'dhyāyaḥ||
iti bhelasaṃhitāyām indriyasthānaṃ samāptam||
<6>
athāta ekādaśasarpiṣkaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
akalpayanna yajñāṅgaṃ purā dakṣastriśūline|
tasmātpraṇāśayāmāsa yajñaṃ dakṣasya śaṅkaraḥ||
taṃ ca nāśayatā yajñaṃ devyāḥ priyacikīrṣayā|
krodhenoṣṇaṃ viniśvasya tān lokān vyāpito jvaraḥ||
sa eṣa santāpayati jagatsthāvarajaṅgamam|
nidhane cāpi bhūtānāṃ nānyo heturvinā jvarāt||
yāvanto'nye samākhyātā vyādhayastu śarīriṇām|
saṃvatsareṇa te ghnanti sadyaḥ prāṇaharo jvaraḥ||
rogānīkasya sarvasya jvaro rājā prakīrtitaḥ|
jvarayatyeṣa bhūtāni tasmājjvara iti smṛtiḥ||
śārīramānasānāṃ ca rogāṇāṃ pravaro jvaraḥ|
tasmātprathamatastasya pravakṣyāmi cikitsitam||
uktarūpasamutthānā jvarāścāṣṭau prakīrtitāḥ|
śītaścoṣṇaśca niyataḥ samāsāt dvividho jvaraḥ||
śītasyoṣṇāṃ kriyāṃ kuryādvātaśleṣmātmakasya tu|
uṣṇasyaikāntapittasya śītāṃ kuryātkriyāṃ bhiṣak||
vāyurāmāśayātpūrvamūṣmāṇaṃ samudīrayet|
tvaggato roma cāpannaḥ saṃharṣayati mānavam||
sa tu pittānubandhena tejasā paripācitaḥ|
prāpnoti vilayaṃ śleṣmā sambandheneva toyadaḥ||
śānte śīte punardāhaṃ pittātprāpnoti mānavaḥ|
utpittamindhanībhūtaṃ vapurdahati pāvakaḥ||
gatavegenilaḥ paścādūṣmaṇā paripācitaḥ|
svameva bhajate sthānamānulomyaṃ ca gacchati||
evaṃ jvaramavāpnoti hyevameva ca mucyate|
etad buddhimatāṃ proktaṃ samāsena cikitsitam||
durlabhā buddhimantastu tasmādvakṣyāmi vistaram|
ekādaśaprakāraṃ hi jvaritānāṃ cikitsitam||
sarpiścaikādaśaṃ vidyāt tanme vistarataḥ śṛṇu|
siddhāssvedāḥ kaṣāyāśca lehaḥ pācanakāni ca||
cūrṇaḥ pradehāssekāśca vaṭikā modakāḥ payaḥ|
sarpiścaikādaśaṃ proktaṃ siddhamāmamathāpi ca||
eṣāṃ prayogaṃ vakṣyāmi vīryāṇi ca pṛthak pṛthak|
śleṣmāṇamanilaṃ caiva jitvā pittantu dehinām||
karoti yasmādūṣmāṇaṃ tasmājjvarita ucyate|
vāyuḥ pittena saṃsṛṣṭaḥ śleṣmā vā pittamūrcchitaḥ||
ūṣmāṇaṃ kurute yasmāttataḥ pittamupācaret|
tṛṣyate coṣṇasalilaṃ deyaṃ doṣavipācanam||
jvaraghnaṃ dīpanīyaṃ ca kaphapittānilāpaham|
srotasāṃ śodhanaṃ caiva rucisvedakaraṃ nṛṇām||
dīpyamāne hi kāyāgnau śleṣmā vāyuśca śāmyataḥ|
tayoścāpyanulomatvātpittam apyupaśāmyati||
tasmāduṣṇodakaṃ peyaṃ śreṣṭhamāhurnavajvare||
śītaṃ hi salilaṃ pītaṃ kopayetkaphamārutau|
kuryād bhūyo'gnimṛdutāṃ staimityamaruciṃ tathā||
kāmaṃ tu tiktakaissiddhaṃ hitaṃ kvathitaśītalam|
teṣāṃ sāmiśṛtaṃ pānaṃ ye cāpyekāntaśītalāḥ||
bhojanārthaṃ hi ṣaḍrātraṃ peyā deyā bubhukṣite|
kṣutpipāsāpahā pathyā śakṛnmūtrānilāpahā||
auṣṇyādvātakaphau hanyātllaghutvātparameva ca|
hṛdvyathāṃ vidhiyuktā tu sā hanyātsvedamāvahet||
tasmājjvareṣu sarveṣu jvaramukte ca mānave|
pāyayedvidhivatpeyāḥ yathāsvauṣadhasādhitāḥ||
śītajjvaraharaṃ kṛtsnamuktamevaṃ cikitsitam|
ata ūrdhvaṃ pravakṣyāmi bhojyābhojyaṃ savistaram||
śālayaḥ ṣaṣṭikāścaiva nīvārāssapramodakāḥ|
yavānnavikṛtiścāpi bhojaneṣu hitāssadā||
kapiñjalāssahariṇā vartakāḥ kālapucchakāḥ|
eṇā vartīrakāścaiva jāṅgalā jvarite hitāḥ||
mudgāḍhakīmasūrāṇāṃ satīnānāṃ tathaiva ca|
siddhāssūpāḥ praśasyante yūṣāśca jvaranāśanāḥ||
uṣṇān vrīhīn sayavakān saktukāṃścitrakānapi|
māṣāṃstilāṃścoṣṇavīryān jvaritaḥ parivarjayet||
grāmyānūpaudakaṃ māṃsaṃ tathobhe gavyamāhiṣe|
chāgaso raṇavārāhaṃ dadhi śuktaṃ ca varjayet||
piṣṭānnāni ca sarvāṇi śākāni vividhāni ca|
vidāhīnyuṣṇavīryāṇi jvaritaḥ parivarjayet||
barhiṇastittirikrauñcān kapotānatha kukkuṭān|
pañcaitānviṣkirānuṣṇān jvaritaḥ parivarjayet||
vamane ca vireke ca snehane laṅghane tathā|
proktā me jvariṇaḥ kālāḥ sūtrasthāne savistarāḥ||
amātyāścālayaścāpi suhṛdaścānuśāsinaḥ|
chandajñā matisampannāḥ śāstrajñāneṣu kovidāḥ||
te vadeyuḥ kathāścitrā dharmakāmārthasaṃhitāḥ|
āturasya vinodārthaṃ tandrāśokavināśanāḥ||
dāruṇāścāmanojñāśca na ca prītivivardhanāḥ|
pittavyādhiparītānāṃ kathāśca parivarjayet||
utsāhameva janayedāturasya cikitsakaḥ|
udagrasya hi bhaiṣajyamamṛtatvāya kalpate||
candrānanāḥ pīnakucāḥ sugandhāḥ śuklavāsasaḥ|
rujāklamavināśārthamupāsīta ca yoṣitaḥ||
atyāśanamatisthānamaticaṅkramaṇāni ca|
jvarito varjayennityaṃ prāṇān rakṣecca sarvaśaḥ||
krodhaṃ striyaṃ caṅkramaṇaṃ divāsvapnātibhāṣaṇam|
gurūṇyadhvā viruddhāni vyāyāmo vegadhāraṇam||
praduṣṭo mārutaḥ pānaṃ niśi jāgaraṇaṃ ca yat|
kāraṇānyupaśāntasya jvarasya prabhave punaḥ||
na cainaṃ snāpayejjātu sahasā jvarakarśitam|
saṃdūṣitāṃ hyasya tanuṃ punarāvartate jvaraḥ||
maheśvarakrodhabhavo jvaraḥ prokto maharṣibhiḥ|
tasmājjvaravimokṣārthaṃ pūjayet ṛṣabhadhvajam||
snānāni śāntayo homavratāni niyamo yamaḥ|
śasyante ceṣṭayaḥ kāmyā vedoktā jvaranāśanāḥ||
rogādhipatirugraujāḥ vyādhīnāṃ prasavo jvaraḥ|
sarvabhūtāntako ghoro hutāśātmā jvaraḥ smṛtaḥ||
prasaṅgī duścikitsyaḥ syādbhiṣagbhiḥ pāpasambhavaḥ|
tasmādvedoditairmantrairhomaiśca vinivartayet||
bhūtavidyāsamutpannaṃ caṇḍakarma jvarāpaham|
tatkāryaṃ bhūtavaidyena tathā nāviśati jvaraḥ||
rudrabhaktena śucinā vaidyenātha tapasvinā|
prayatena prayoktavyaṃ cirajvaracikitsitam|| ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele cikitsite prathamo'dhyāyaḥ||
||dvitīyo'dhyāyaḥ||
athāto viṣamajvaracikitsitaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
kecidvātātmakaṃ prāhurbhiṣajo viṣamajvaram|
sannipātodbhavaṃ kecidapare pittasambhavam||
śleṣmajaṃ tvapare prāhurapare bhūtasambhavam|
janmanakṣatrapīḍābhirapare devacintakāḥ||
īdṛśaḥ śāstrakārāṇāṃ matayo'tha pṛthagvidhāḥ|
sannipātodbhavaṃ hyetadahaṃ vakṣyāmi hetubhiḥ||
pakvāśayasthapavano hyasthimajjagatastathā|
kupitaḥ kopayatyāśu śleṣmāṇaṃ pittameva ca||
sa gambhīrasamutpattisthānatantratrayo mahān|
anyedyuṣkaikāntaritau kuryādvāpi caturthakam||
majjānamasthi medaśca dūṣayetprathame'hani|
dvitīye'hani samprāpte dūṣayenmāṃsaśoṇitam||
tatastṛtīye divase dūṣayetkaphamārutau|
abhigamya tataḥ pittaṃ dūṣayettu caturthake||
jvaro'yaṃ prāṇināṃ dehe raudro māheśvarastathā|
prayateta praśāntau tu tasmādasya vicakṣaṇaḥ||
gambhīrasthānasambhūto dhātusaṃkaradūṣitaḥ|
tasmāccaturthako nāma duścikitsyatamo mataḥ||
śoṣayatyeṣa bhūtāni dāruṇo viṣamajvaraḥ|
tasmācchoṣopacāreṇa kuryāttasya cikitsitam||
triphalākvāthasiddhena ghṛtena matimān bhiṣak|
snehayettaṃ yathānyāyaṃ yuktyā vṛṣaghṛtena vā||
sraṃsayettamadhobhāgaṃ yathāvyādhibalābalam|
doṣeṣvāmāśayastheṣu kārayedvamanaṃ bhiṣak||
āsthāpanaṃ ca kurvīta tathā cāpyanuvāsanam|
etanmahāpañcagavyaṃ vikhyātaṃ sarpiruttamam||
caturthakaṃ mokṣayati mantrasiddhiyuto yathā|
śvayathuṃ pāṇḍurogaṃ ca plīhānaṃ sabhagandaram||
udarāṇi tathā gulmān kāmilāṃ cāpakarṣati||
śakṛdrasaṃ payo mūtraṃ dadhi sarpiśca pāyayet|
tatpañcagavyaṃ śamayecchvayathuṃ pāṇḍutāṃ jvaram||
samūlapatraśākhasya śataṃ kṛtvā vṛṣasya tu|
jaladroṇe vipaktavyamaṣṭabhāgāvaśeṣitam||
garbheṇa vṛṣapuṣpāṇāmāḍhakaṃ sarpiṣaḥ pacet|| tatsiddhaṃ pāyayedyuktyā madhupādasamanvitam||
kāsaṃ śvāsaṃ pāṇḍurogaṃ tṛtīyakacaturthakau|
raktapittaṃ kṣayaṃ caiva vṛṣasarpirniyacchati||
nāgaraṃ saindhavaṃ cavyaṃ pippalīkṣāracitrakau|
eteṣāṃ palikairbhāgairghṛtaprasthaṃ vipācayet||
kṣīraprasthena saṃyojya śanairmṛdvagninā pacet|
etena gulmāḥ śāmyanti kuṣṭhāni jaṭharāṇi ca||
jvare ca viṣame peyaṃ plīhaśvayathumehinām|
śūle pāṇḍvāmaye caiva grahaṇīdīpaneṣu ca||
ūrdhvavātāśca ye kecitsarveṣāmauṣadhaṃ param|
tryūṣaṇaṃ citrakaṃ hiṃsrāṃ vilaṅgāni harītakīm||
vibhītakānyāmalakaṃ cirabilvatvacaṃ tathā|
daśaitāni samāṃśāni ślakṣṇānyakṣasamāni ca||
taiḥ pācayeddhṛtaprasthaṃ samyak payasi ṣaḍguṇe|
grahaṇī dīpyate tena vātagulmaśca śāmyati||
arśasāṃ śamanaṃ caiva gudaśophe ca pūjitam|
etadeva hi vai śreṣṭhamudāvarte ghṛtaṃ bhavet||
darbhavetasamūlāni candanaṃ madhukaṃ balā|
phenilā padmakośīramubhe ca kamalotpale||
kiṃśukaśceti bhāgāḥ syuḥ pṛthak pañcapalonmitāḥ|
jaladroṇe vipaktavyaṃ caturbhāgāvaśeṣitam||
jīvakarṣabhakau medāṃ lodhraṃ lāmajjakaṃ tathā|
kāleyakaṃ sapriyakaṃ dadyātkesarameva ca||
tripuṇḍarīkaṃ lodhraṃ ca padmakaṃ padmakesaram|
surabhiṃ kuṅkumaṃ caiva mañjiṣṭhāṃ madayantikām||
mācipatraṃ ca tulyāni dviguṇaṃ kuṅkumaṃ bhavet|
caturguṇāṃ ca mañjiṣṭhāṃ sauvīraṃ snehasammitam||
tailaprasthaṃ pacettena kaṣāyeṇārdhapeṣitam|
etadabhyañjanaṃ tailaṃ viṣamajvaranāśanam||
mahāpadmakamākhyātametatsarvajvarāpaham|
vātapittodbhavaṃ kṣipraṃ jvarametanniyacchati||
trāyamāṇāṃ jayāṃ vīrāṃ nākulīṃ gandhanākulīm|
kāyasthāṃ ca vayaḥsthāṃ ca jīrakaṃ sapalaṅkaṣam||
chatrāticchatrajaṭilāṃ sūkarīṃ karkaṭīṃ tathā|
cāraṭīṃ pūtanāṃ keśīṃ vacāṃ kaṭukarohiṇīm||
mahāpuruṣadantāṃ ca vṛścikālīṃ kaṭambharām|
sthirāṃ ca piṣṭvā taissiddhaṃ caturthakavināśanam||
mahāpaiśācikaṃ nāma sarpiretajjvarāpaham|
bhūtagrahānapasmārānunmādāṃścāpakarṣati||
etairevauṣadhagaṇaiḥ pradehaṃ kārayedbhiṣak|
dhūpayejjvaritaṃ caiva tathā sampadyate sukhī||
balayaḥ śāntikarmāṇi homasvastyayanāni ca|
snānāni copavāsaśca śamayanti caturthakam||
sraṣṭāraṃ cāsya rogasya bhūtādhipatimacyutam|
pūjayaṃścābhigacchecca śmaśāne vṛṣabhadhvajam||
bhūtavidyāsamutpannairbandhanaissādhanairapi|
homairbalividhānaiśca nāśayedviṣamajvaram|| ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele cikitsite dvitīyo'dhyāyaḥ||
||tṛtīyo'dhyāyaḥ||
athāto raktapittacikitsitaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
ūrdhvaṃ cādhaśca martyānāṃ raktapittaṃ pravartate|
atastu dvividhā proktā cikitsā raktapittinaḥ||
atyamlalavaṇāhārāt satatātapasevanāt|
upavāsād bhramāt trāsādviruddhādhyaśanādibhiḥ||
tilapiṇyākāśākānāṃ tathā piṣṭakṛtasya ca|
pittalānāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ matsyādīnāṃ ca sevanāt||
ūrdhvaṃ cādhaśca kupitaṃ raktapittaṃ pravartate|
ghrāṇakarṇākṣimukhataḥ pāyuto meḍhratastathā||
niṣṭhīvati saraktaṃ ca tiktamamlamathāpi vā|
na ca tatpatitaṃ bhūmau bhakṣayanti pipīlikāḥ||
makṣikā vāpi tiktatvācchuṣkaṃ pittaṃ ca lakṣyate|
sandahyate dūyate ca kaṇṭhastālu ca śuṣyati||
lohagandhi mukhaṃ cāsya kaṇṭho dhūmāyate tathā|
etānyevaṃ tvadhobhāge lakṣaṇānyupalakṣayet||
ūrdhvabhāgaṃ tu sādhyaṃ syādadho vai yāpyamucyate|
sarvasrotaḥpravṛttaṃ tu raktapittaṃ na sidhyati|| raktapittamadhobhāgaṃ chardinaissamupācaret|| ................................................... ...................................................
||caturtho'dhyāyaḥ||
.................................................. ...................................................
svapneṣu nityaṃ paśyanti śuṣkāndagdhāṃśca pādapān|
makṣikātṛṇakeśāśca sampatantyasya bhojane||
romāṇi mūrdhajāścāsya vivardhante viśeṣataḥ|
avaguṇṭhanaśīlaśca rahaḥkāmotthakopanaḥ||
jugupsāluramarṣī ca strīkāmo durbalendriyaḥ|
ucitā hīyate chāyā tāluśoṣśca jāyate||
nāsāsrāvaḥ pratiśyāyaḥ svarabhedaśśirorujā|
ūrū ca svidyato'tyarthaṃ hāniśca balavarṇayoḥ||
śirasaḥ paripūrṇatvaṃ hastapādaṃ ca dahyate|
aruciścāvipākaśca mūrchā śvāso mṛdurjvaraḥ||
kṣatoraskaśca kāsena niṣṭhīvati saśoṇitam|
etāni pūrvarūpāṇi yakṣmiṇāṃ kīrtitāni ca||
ṣaṭ caikādaśarūpāṇi śuṣyatastāni lakṣayet|
ucchritopadravaṃ cainaṃ pūrvarūpairabhidrutam||
prakṣīṇabalamāṃsaṃ ca na cikitsedviśoṣiṇam|
alpopadravasaṃyuktamaciropadravotthitam||
dravyavantaṃ vayaḥsthaṃ ca pratyākhyāyārabheta vai|
athāsyānumataṃ pūrvaṃ pravakṣyāmi cikitsitam||
yadadhītya bhiṣagyuktyā yaśaḥ svargamavāpnuyāt|
śoṣī nityajugupsāluḥ sukhaśīlaśca mānavaḥ||
lolo dveṣṭā nikṛṣṭānāṃ vaidyabhaiṣajyakutsakaḥ|
tasmāttasyānukūlena bhiṣajā''āśvasanena ca||
asaṃbhrāntena yuktena prayoktavyaṃ cikitsitam|
śoṣiṇo'tha balasthasya pañcakarma vidhīyate||
tadeva kṣīṇamāṃsasya prayuktaṃ viṣamaṃ bhavet|
tasya saṃśuddhākāyasya bhojanānyupakalpayet||
kalamān dīrghaśūkāṃśca raktaśāliṃ saṣaṣṭikān|
yavānnavikṛtīścāpi jāṅgalāṃśca mṛgadvijān||
bhojaneṣu praśaṃsanti mudgān sūpārthameva ca|
saṃvatsarasthitaṃ dhānyaṃ bhojane śoṣiṇāṃ hitam||
taddhi vīryādahīnaṃ syānmāṃsaṃ sadyohataṃ ca yat|
viśeṣataśca māṃsāni kravyādabilavāsinām||
śoṣī nityaṃ niṣeveta madyāni vividhāni ca|
śleṣmā dehe vivṛddho'pi vāyunā susamīritaḥ||
pittaṃ raktaṃ ca sandūṣya srotāṃsyūrdhvaṃ ruṇaddhi hi|
te parasparasaṃruddhā dhātavaḥ pavaneritāḥ||
kaphapradhānā rundhanti sarvā rasavahāḥ sirāḥ|
tadā tā rasavāhinyaḥ sirā hṛdayavāhikāḥ||
rasamāhārajātānāṃ na vahanti yathā purā|
tasya bhuktaṃ na raktāya na māṃsāya ca kalpate||
na ca medo'sthimajjabhyo na śuklatvāy dehinām|
teṣāmavṛddhādhātūnāṃ baddhānāmayaneṣvatha||
bhuñjānānāmapi sarogāṇi gātrāṇi dehinām|
hetunā tena śuṣyanti śuṣyatāṃ śoṣavanti ca||
etacchoṣasya śoṣatvaṃ mukhyamāhurmanīṣiṇaḥ|
sattveṣvatipramāthitvaṃ vyālakarma yathā''āpadi||
māṃsānyapi tathā teṣāṃ srotāṃsi vikasantyapi|
śodhayanti pramāthitvātsrotāṃsi grathitānyapi||
tasmātkravyādamāṃsāni śoṣī śaktyāvaśīlayet|
rahasyametadvaidyānāmupadiṣṭaṃ cikitsakaiḥ||
madyaṃ kravyādamāṃsaṃ ca śoṣiṇāmamṛtopamam|
vṛkāḥ śṛgālā ṛkṣāḥ śvasiṃhavyāghrāssasūkarāḥ||
eṇaśabdena dātavyā nānādravyābhisaṃskṛtāḥ|
gṛdhrān sahaṃsānulūkānmaṇḍūkāñśyenavāyasān||
dadyādbarhiṇaśabdena madyaṃ ca niyataṃ pibet|
ākhumārjāralopākān dvijihvāñśalyakānapi||
nakulāṃstāmracūḍāṃśca bhakṣayecca susaṃskṛtān|
anyamāṃsopadeśena māṃsānyetāni dāpayet||
jugupsayā vamati vā pāde'tyavahito bhavet|
tasmādrahasyasiddhāni māṃsānyetāni bhakṣayet||
bileśayakravyabhujāṃ bhiṣaṅ māṃsāni lābhataḥ|
toyadroṇeṣu daśasu tulāmatha vipācayet||
aṣṭabhāgāvaśiṣṭantu rasaṃ visrāvayedbhiṣak|
garbheṇa jīvanīyānāṃ sarpirdroṇaṃ vipācayet||
tatpibenmātrataḥ sarpirmāṃsaṃ tenaiva sādhayet|
kāsaṃ śvāsaṃ sahṛdrogaṃ jvaraṃ pārśvarujāmapi||
śvayathuṃ svarabhedaṃ ca śoṣiṇāmapakarṣati|
pañcamūlaṃ mahaddhrasvaṃ golomīṃ madhukaṃ balām||
vipācayejjaladroṇe caturbhāgāvaśeṣitam|
tatkaṣāyaṃ jalaṃ pūtaṃ ghṛtaprasthaṃ vipācayet||
caturguṇena payasā jīvanīyaiśca peṣitaiḥ|
vidāryāmalakānāṃ tu tathaivekṣo rasasya ca||
dadyād ghṛtasamān bhāgān tatpacenmṛdunā'gninā|| rasāyanīyamiti tat khyātaṃ sarpirbalāvaham||
hṛdrogakṣatakāsānāṃ kṣayāṇāṃ ca nivāraṇam|
ataḥ sarpirguḍaṃ kuryāccharkarāmadhusaṃyutam||
godhūmacūrṇena saha modakānvāpi kārayet|
pūrvokteṣu vikāreṣu śoṣiṇāmamṛtopamam||
kṣīrānupānaṃ bhakṣyāste modakā balavardhanāḥ|
madyānupānāssevyāste śoṣaḥ śleṣmādhiko yadi||
prasthārdhaṃ dhautaluñcitatilānāṃ kalkapeṣitam|
toyaprasthena saṃyojya ghṛtaprasthaṃ vipācayet||
atha siddhaṃ ca pūtaṃ ca punastadvipaced ghṛtam|
saha kravyādamāṃsena kṣīreṇeha ca sarvaśaḥ||
bilvamātreṇa piṇḍena bhāvitena sadāmbunā|
jāṅgalena ca māṃsena sukṛtena vidhānavit||
atha siddhaṃ ca pūtaṃ ca punaragnāvadhiśrayet|
prasthena payasā sārdhaṃ samyagikṣurasāḍhake||
vidārīṃ mudgaparṇīṃ ca māṣaparṇīṃ kaserukam|
śṛṅgāṭakamṛṇālāni padmabījaṃ sajīvakam||
kṣīraśuklāṃ saṛṣabhāṃ medāṃ samadhukāṃ tathā|
payasyāṃ kṣīrakākolīṃ kandaṃ nīlotpalasya ca||
saha tālakadambena kalkāṃstānakṣasammitān|
tasminnāloḍya tatsarvaṃ vipacenmṛdunāgninā||
tatsiddhaṃ srāvayitvā tu nirvātaṃ syādyathāmṛtam|
tataḥ prātarghanībhūtaṃ khajenābhipramanthayet||
athātra śarkarācūrṇaṃ pañcāśatpalasammitam|
mathyamānaṃ tato dadyāddattvā dattvā ca manthayet||
gandhārthaṃ cātra deyaṃ syāccūrṇaṃ tvaṅnāgapuṣpayoḥ|
ekībhūtaṃ tathā rātrimekāṃ parivasedatha||
akṣapramāṇasaṃsthānāḥ kāryāstu modakāstathā|
yavagodhūmacūrṇābhyāṃ tugākṣīryā ca cūrṇitān||
bhakṣayenmodakān kāle kṣīrapānaṃ pibetpunaḥ|
yakṣmāṇaṃ śamayantyete vṛkṣamindrāśaniryathā||
asṛgdaraṃ kṣataṃ kṣīṇaṃ hṛdrogaṃ viṣamajvaram|
unmāda raktapittaṃ ca kāsaṃ caitena sādhayet||
aśvatthasyātha mūlāni śuṅgāni saphalāni ca|
śatāvarīṃ pṛśniparṇīṃ bṛhatīṃ kaṇṭakārikām||
golomīṃ śreyasīṃ kālāṃ śāribāṃ sapunarnavām|
kṣīradroṇeṣu daśasu kuṭṭitāni vipācayet||
tacchṛtaṃ śītalaṃ pūtaṃ khajenābhipramanthayet|
yattatra sarpirjāyeta taduddhṛtya vipācayet||
bhaiṣajyāni ca piṣṭāni tatremāni samācaret|
madhukaṃ madhuliṃ drākṣāṃ medāṃ vṛṣabhajīrakau||
tālamajjāṃ salāmajjaṃ tathā puṣkarabījakam|
jīvanīṃ trāyamāṇāṃ ca madhukāni kaśerukam||
nīlotpalaṃ puṇḍarīkaṃ vārtākānapi coccaṭām|
kāśmaryāmalakekṣūṇāṃ vidāryāḥ svarasaṃ ca vā||
tatsiddhaṃ dāpayet pūtaṃ kalaśe rājate dṛḍhe|
tulārdhaṃ śarkarāyāśca dattvā caivābhimanthayet||
prakṣipecca tugākṣīryāḥ prasthaṃ prasthaṃ ca mākṣikāt|
ātmaguptāphalasya syād guḍasya maricasya ca||
uccaṭīkṣurasābhyāṃ ca dvau prasthau tatra dāpayet|
tvagelānāgapuṣpāṇāṃ cūrṇaṃ tatra pradāpayet||
palikān modakān kṛtvā sthāpayenmṛṇmaye nave|
aśnīyāttatpalaṃ kalyaṃ sāyaṃ bhūyaḥ palaṃ naraḥ||
śayanaṃ mṛdu seveta brahmacārī samāhitaḥ|
karmaṇānena niyataṃ yakṣmāṇamapakarṣati||
saṃbṛhayati śuṣkaṃ ca puruṣaṃ durbalendriyam|
vājīkaraṇamapyetannarāṇāṃ kṣīṇaretasām||
strībhirhatā bhārahatāḥ jīrṇāścātītayauvanāḥ|
kṣīṇamāṃsāḥ kṣīṇabalāḥ ye cātha kṣīṇaśoṇitāḥ||
pittaroge raktapitte śoṣe doṣajvare tathā|
hatendriyā naṣṭaśuklāḥ saktakaṇṭhāśca ye smṛtāḥ||
aśaktamandakā ye ca ratinaṣṭendriyā narāḥ|
punarnavāṃstānkurute yogo'yamamṛtopamaḥ||
apasmārānathonmādān hṛdrogānapatantrakam|
tejo vāpahṛtaṃ yeṣāṃ bhūtadharmeṇa kenacit||
kṣīrānupānāttanvanti sadyaḥ poṣaṃ hi modakāḥ|
vyākulātyalpādhātūnāṃ śoṣiṇāṃ mandadehinām||
api yogaśatenāpi duḥkhameva cikitsitam|
tasmādrasāyanavidhiṃ vardhamāne kṣaye bhiṣak||
saṃvatsaraṃ paramṛtumayanaṃ vā samācaret|
pippalīvardhamānaṃ tu pañcapañcavivardhitam||
nasye cābhyañjane pāne praśastaṃ bastikarmaṇi|
vātavyādhiṣu sarveṣu kṣatakṣīṇe śirograhe||
pārśvaśūle pramehe ca gulme sārśobhagandare|
cyutabhagnāṅgahīnānāṃ kāse śvāse ca hṛdgrahe||
jvarātisāreṣvarucau varṇabhede svarakṣaye|
sukumāramidaṃ tailaṃ bālavṛddhasukhāvaham||
etaddhi vṛṣyaṃ balyaṃ ca māsānmāṃsavivardhanam|
svaravarṇakaraṃ caiva śoṣiṇāmamṛtopamam||
niṣpākaścāsya tailasya samyak siddhasya yo bhavet|
udaśvidiva dadhyarthaṃ so'pi kṛtyakaro bhavet||
ekādaśa ca ṣaṭ caiva śoṣajā ya upadravāḥ|
sukumāraṃ praśamayenmedho'gnīniva vṛṣṭimān||
luñcitānāṃ tilānāṃ tu tailaprasthaṃ vipācayet|
palaṃ tu madhukasyātra garbhaṃ kṣīraṃ caturguṇam||
mṛdupākaṃ ca tatsiddhaṃ bhūya eva vipācayet|
caturguṇena payasā madhukasya palena ca||
etena vidhinā caiva madhukasya śataṃ pacet|
śatakṛtvo vipakvantu śatapākamiti smṛtam||
pānābhyañjananasyeṣu bastau vyañjanasādhane|
bhojane cāmṛtaprakhyaṃ narāṇāṃ rājayakṣmiṇām||
hṛdrogaṃ tāluśoṣaṃ ca pārśvaśūlaṃ pratānakam|
tṛṣṇāmunmādavīsarpaṃ kāsaṃ śvāsamasṛgdaram||
raktapittaṃ pravṛddhaṃ ca sarvato bhāgamūrcchitam|
kāmalāṃ pāṇḍurogaṃ ca pānādetanniyacchati||
mukhapākākṣipākau ca bādhiryaṃ karṇavedanām|
naktāndhyaṃ timiraṃ kācaṃ liṅganāśaṃ sirācayam||
biḍālikāṃ pūtinasyaṃ śirorogaṃ śirograham|
nastaḥ kṛtaṃ praṇudati khālityaṃ palitāni ca||
adhobhāge raktapitte raktārśassu bhagandare|
sarvagātragate vāte raktapittasamudbhave||
aprajassu ca nārīṣu puṃsāṃ naṣṭe ca retasi|
jānutrikādikubjeṣu praśastaṃ bastikarmaṇi||
bahūparodhāḥ strīnityāḥ teṣāṃ vṛṣyatamaṃ matam|
prayogeṇa prayuktaṃ ca rasāyanamanuttamam||
sahasrapākamityetatkalpenaitena sādhayet|
rasāyanamidaṃ prāhurnṛṇāṃ varṣasahasrakṛt||
aśvagandhā hyapāmārgo nākulī gaurasarṣapāḥ|
tilā bilvaṃ ca kalkaṃ syāttatkṣayonmardanaṃ param||
maṅgalācārasaṃyukto bhavetsvastyayano naraḥ|
snānāni śāntihomāṃśca śoṣī nityaṃ samācarete||
iṣṭayo vedavihitā nāśanā rājayakṣmaṇaḥ|
tāśca nityaṃ niṣeveta pūjayed vṛṣabhadhvajam||
upadravāśca ye śeṣe ṣaṭ caikādaśa coditāḥ|
teṣāṃ cikitsitaṃ kuryādyathā sve sve cikitsite||
ityevaṃ prayatenoktaṃ rājyakṣacikitsitam|
yaśaḥsvargakaraṃ puṇyaṃ śiṣyāṇāmarthasiddhaye|| ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele cikitsite caturtho'dhyāyaḥ||
||pañcamo'dhyāyaḥ||
athāto gulmacikitsitaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
pañca gulmāḥ samuddiṣṭā nidāneṣu savistaram|
gulmatvaṃ teṣu vakṣyāmi cikitsāṃ tu pṛthak pṛthak||
mārutaḥ kupito dehe srotāṃsyunnahya sarvaśaḥ|
abhyāgatya dūṣayati pūrvaṃ pittaṃ kaphaṃ tataḥ||
dūṣayatyatha raktaṃ ca medo māṃsaṃ vidhāvati|
sa saṅghātasthirībhūto gulmatvamupapadyate||
duṣṭānāṃ hantukāmānāṃ paraṃ prāṇabhṛtāṃ yathā|
hastyaśvarathayānānāṃ saṃghāto gulma iṣyate||
evaṃ dehe rasādīnāṃ dhātūnāṃ viprakarṣaṇam|
saṃsargo gulma ityuktaḥ saṃghāto gulma ucyate||
stambinistambinīnāṃ tu vallīnāṃ vīrudhāmapi|
saṃghāto gahanaṃ gulmaḥ tadvad gulmastu dehinām||
amūrtatvāddhi vātasya saṃvittirnopajāyate|
sandhāya pittaśleṣmāṇau māruto gulmatāṃ vrajet||
madhūcchiṣṭamayaṃ piṇḍaṃ cinvanti bhramarā yathā|
tathā koṣṭheṣu pavano dhātūṃstānvicinotyapi||
sthānāni tasya gulmasya śaṃmanti gurulāghavam|
kukṣikastigato gulmo vātabhūyiṣṭha ucyate||
sa vātagulmarūpāṇi kuryātsthānavaśena tu|
tathaiva pittasthānasthaḥ pittarūpāṇi darśayet||
evaṃ śleṣmaṇi rakte ca sannipāte ca sarvaśaḥ|
sarveṣāṃ sannipāte tu svaṃ svaṃ rūpaṃ nidarśanam||
āganturdūṣayatyatra dhātuṃ sthānastha ulbaṇaḥ|
cikitsitaṃ tu sthānasya sa hi tatra balādhikaḥ||
yadi vastigataḥ pākaṃ vātagulmastu gacchati|
hṛnnābhyorantaraṃ gulmaḥ pākaṃ gacchati paittikaḥ||
hṛdi ca śleṣmagulmastu pākaṃ yātyananuṣṭhitaḥ|
pacyate sannipātāttu gulmaśśoṇitajastathā||
doṣadvayena saṃsṛṣṭāḥ sarve gulmāḥ pramāthinaḥ|
tasmātteṣāṃ bhedanīyāḥ kriyāssarvāḥ prayojayet|
atyarthaṃ snihyamāno'pi gulmo vṛddhimavāpnuyāt||
atastu vātagulmasya pravakṣyāmi cikitsitam|
prabhurhi sarvabhūtānāṃ vāyuḥ prāṇeśvaro balī||
harītakī trikaṭukaṃ vacā kaṭukarohiṇī|
sauvarcalaṃ yavakṣāro viḍaṅgaṃ citrakastathā||
akṣapramāṇairetaistu ghṛtaprasthaṃ vipācayet|
tasmiddhaṃ srāvayitvā tu pāyayettu yathābalam||
vātagulmaṃ krimiṃ kāsaṃ śvāsaṃ plīhānameva ca|
daśāṅgaṃ nāśayatyetadrogān vajramivāsurān||
dvepañcamūlyau suṣavīmaśvagandhāṃ punarnavām|
kālāṃ chinnaruhāṃ rāsnāṃ bhārṅgīṃ gokṣurakaṃ balām||
śaṭiṃ puṣkaramūlaṃ ca palāśaṃ gandhasaṃjñakam|
eteṣāṃ dvipalān bhagān jaladroṇe vipācayet||
kolānāṃ sakulutthānāṃ māṣāṇāṃ ca yavaissaha|
prasthaṃ prasthaṃ pṛthak kṛtvā tasminneva samāvapet||
tena pādāvaśiṣṭena ghṛtaprasthaṃ vipācayet|
dadhyādibhissamaṃ śuktamāranālaṃ tuṣodakam||
dāḍimāmrātakarasaṃ mātuluṅgarasaṃ tathā|
caturguṇaṃ cātra dadhi garbhe caiṣāṃ samāvapet||
kāravīṃ tryūṣaṇaṃ dantīṃ trīṇyeva lavaṇāni ca|
hiṃsrāṃ rāsnāṃ vacāṃ caiva yavānīmamlavetasam||
viḍaṅgaṃ dāḍimaṃ hiṅgu nīlikāṃ trivṛtāmapi|
dvau kṣārau cājamodāṃ ca pāṭhāṃ pāṣāṇabhedakam||
ūṣakaṃ vṛṣakaṃ bhārṅgīṃ śvadaṃṣṭrāṃ hapuṣāmapi|
trapusorvārubījāni śatavīryopakuñcike||
ajājīṃ citrakaṃ mūrvāṃ tumburuṃ gajapippalīm|
dhānyakaṃ surasaṃ caiva dadyādakṣeṇa sammitam||
garbheṇānena tatsiddhaṃ pāyayetsarpiruttamam|
raktagulmādṛte sarvān gulmānetatpraṇāśayet||
ekāṅgage pakṣagate gṛdhrasyāṃ plīhni caiva hi|
hṛdroge grahaṇīdoṣe vātagulme ca dāruṇe||
dādhikaṃ nāma vikhyātaṃ sarpiretanmahāguṇam|
unmādaṃ koṣṭhaśūlāni cāpasmāraṃ ca nāśayet||
yāvaśūkaṃ tathā kṣāraṃ saindhavaṃ hastipippalīm|
pippalīṃ śṛṅgaberaṃ ca maricaṃ ca samāvapet||
eṣāṃ ṣaṇṇāṃ ṣaḍeva syuḥ pṛthagbhāgāḥ palaṃ palam|
eṣāmardhapalān bhāgān kaṣāyamupasādhayet||
peṣyairardhapalīnaistu ghṛtaprasthaṃ vipācayet|
snehatulyaṃ kaṣāyaṃ tu kṣīraṃ taddviguṇaṃ bhavet||
ityetat ṣaṭpalaṃ nāma sarpirgulmavināśanam|
plīhānamarśāṃsi tathā grahaṇīdoṣameva ca||
vastikuṇḍalavardhmāni pānādevāpakarṣati|
eṣāmanyatamaiḥ svehaiḥ snehitaṃ vātagulminam|
virecanena snigdhena yuktyā saṃśodhayed bhiṣak||
ghṛtaṃ ca saktuṃ ca tathā citrakaṃ saindhavaṃ vacām|
pippalīṃ ca pacetkṣīraṃ praśastaṃ cāvatārayet||
tato biḍālapadakaṃ pibeduṣṇena vāriṇā|
ghṛtena payasā vāpi madyenoṣṇena vāriṇā||
vātagulmaṃ nudatyeṣa gulmaśūlāni yāni ca|
saplīhānamudāvartaṃ śleṣmagulmaṃ ca nāśayet||
mahauṣadhaṃ devadāru vāsā kaṭukarohiṇī|
citrakaḥ pippalīmūlaṃ pippalyo hastipippalī||
kuṣṭhaṃ ca sarpagandhā ca pañcaiva lavaṇāni ca|
dvau kṣārau trivṛtā dantī dravantī copakuñcikā||
eṣāmardhapalān bhāgān lavaṇānāṃ palaṃ palam|
cūrṇāni dadhnaḥ prasthārdhe samāloḍya vipācayet||
vasātailaghṛtānāṃ ca prasthaṃ prasthaṃ pradāpayet|
pradīptaṃ ca yathāśāntamathainamavatārayet||
tato biḍālapadakaṃ pibeduṣṇena vāriṇā|
ghṛtena payasā vāpi madyenāmlena vā punaḥ||
eṣa kṣārāgado nāma vātagulmavināśanaḥ|
plīhānamarśaḥśūlāni vardhmodāvartakuṇḍalam||
krimīn sagrahaṇīdoṣān viṣān vātāpatānakau|
sarpamūṣakadaṃśāṃśca granthitān gadayojitān||
vātaśleṣmasamutthānaṃ sannipātātmakaṃ tathā|
prasahya nāśayed gulmaṃ chinnābhrāṇīva mārutaḥ|| vandhyā ca labhate garbhaṃ garānapi ca naśayet|
apasmārānathonmādān......................... ................................................ ...............................................
||ṣaṣṭho'dhyāyaḥ||
.................................................... ....................................................
.................pittaṃ bhṛśaṃ dehe prakupyati|
tatpraduṣṭaṃ dūṣayati raktamāṃsamatholbaṇam||
tatra kuṣṭhāni jāyante dehe bahuvidhāni tu|
teṣāṃ rūpāṇi vakṣyāmi cikitsāṃ ca yathākramam||
doṣāṇāṃ sañcitānāṃ tu tvaṅmāṃsācchāsracāriṇām|
pradūṣaṇaṃ hi sarveṣāṃ kuṣṭhamityabhidhīyate||
viruddhamāhārayato'pyajīrṇādhyaśanena ca|
chardimūtrapurīṣāṇāṃ vegānāṃ ca vidhāraṇāt||
grāmyānūpaudakaṃ māṃsaṃ śākaṃ haritakāni ca|
madyamamlamathātyarthaṃ sevitvā yaḥ pibetpayaḥ||
bhuktvā vāpyuṣṇamāhāraṃ madhu māṃsaṃ ca sevate|
madyaṃ madhu ca yaḥ pītvātyuṣṇamannaṃ ca sevate||
vidagdhabhukto yaścāpi grāmyadharma niṣevate|
uṣṇātapāmyāṃ sahasā yaścāpyapsu nimajjati||
tasyoṣmā sanniruddhastu prakopayati mārutam|
udīrayati vāyustu dūṣito doṣasaṃcayān||
doṣāśśirāḥ prapannāste rudhiraṃ dūṣayantyati|
raktamāṃsaniruddhāstu vātapittakaphāstrayaḥ||
janayantyāśu kuṣṭhāni nṛṇāmaṣṭādaśaiva tu|
nidāneṣvapi nirdiṣṭaṃ nidānaṃ sāptakuṣṭhikam||
aṣṭādaśa tu kuṣṭhāni teṣāṃ vakṣyāmi lakṣaṇam|
yatkuṣṭhamaruṇābhāsaṃ śyāmaṃ rūkṣaṃ savedanam||
pipīlikākīrṇamiva karkaśaṃ cāpi vātikam|
kṣipramuttiṣṭhate yastu dūṣyate paridahyate||
tāmraṃ śyāvaprakupitaṃ sajvaraṃ cāpi paittikam|
samutpanneṣu vā teṣu stimitaṃ bahulaṃ guru||
pāṇḍu snigdhaṃ ca guru ca yatkuṣṭhaṃ śleṣmasambhavam|
sphuṭitaṃ srāvabahulaṃ dāharāgarujānvitam||
tvagromanakhamāṃsaiśca dīryadbhissānnipātikam|
prādurbhaviṣyatāṃ caiva pūrvarūpāṇi me śṛṇu||
tāni saṃlakṣya medhāvī cikitsitamupācaret|
ūṣmāyaṇaṃ parītāpaḥ svedo raukṣyaṃ vivarṇatā||
suptatvaṃ romaharṣaśca gātrāṇāṃ gauravaṃ klamaḥ|
rāgaḥ pipāsā daurbalyaṃ davathuḥ piṭakādayaḥ||
tataḥ kuṣṭhāni jāyante teṣāṃ vakṣyāmi lakṣaṇam|
svedanaṃ kākaṇābhāsaṃ samuptannaṃ savedanam||
sphuṭitaṃ nīlaparyantaṃ kākaṇaṃ kuṣṭhamucyate|
udumbaranibhairyattu maṇḍalairbahubhiścitam||
nirāsrāvaiḥ sravadbhirvā vidyādaudumbaraṃ tu tat|
maṇḍalairbandhujīvābhairutsannaiśca vidāhibhiḥ||
vidyānmaṇḍalakuṣṭhaṃ tadvedanācoṣaṇānvitam|
rśyajihvopamaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ ṛśyajihvaṃ vibhāvayet||
puṇḍarīkadalābhāsaṃ puṇḍarīkaṃ tu tadviduḥ|
alābupuṣpasadṛśaṃ sidhmaṃ kuṣṭhamudāhṛtam||
kapālakuṣṭhaṃ kṛṣṇantu maṇḍalaiḥ paruṣaṃ tathā|
carmakuṣṭhaṃ tu bahulaṃ hasticarmanibhaṃ kharam||
sasphoṭakā sasantāpā pāmā kaṇḍūrujānvitā|
dṛḍhaṃ punaḥ prasravati kaṇḍūrodhānvitaṃ ca yat||
vartate ca samutpannaṃ kiṭibaṃ tatprakīrtitam|
śyāvā raktā samutpannā praklinnā srāviṇī tathā||
māṃsenopacitā yuktā vijñeyā sā vicarcikā|
pariśuṣkāṇi rūkṣāṇi kaṇḍūrāṇi ghanāni ca||
maṇḍalānyunnatāgrāṇi dudrukuṣṭhaṃ hi tatsmṛtam|
aruḍbhiścīyamānaṃ tu nīlalohitakaiḥ kharaiḥ||
bahubhiśca sravadbhiśca śatāruṣkantu tadviduḥ|
śāliśūkapratīkāśairlomabhiḥ śuklalohitam||
anyonyairmaṇḍalairviddhaṃ śvitraṃ tadupapādayet|
mīnamūṣikakīṭānāṃ viṣavegena dūṣitam||
sakaṇḍūrāgapiṭakaṃ viṣajaṃ śyāmameva vā|
pāṇipādatalāṅguṣṭhapārṣṇideśeṣu jāyate||
sphuṭitaṃ vedanādāhayuktaṃ vaipādikaṃ smṛtam|
udumbarasavarṇaṃ tu samutpannaṃ savedanam||
picchārāgavivarṇaṃ ca kuṣṭhaṃ sthūlāruruvyate|
visarpaissaṃparikrāntaṃ vikūṇaṃ saparisravam||
tadekakuṣṭhamityuktamekaṃ vīsarpasambhavam|
etānyaṣṭādaśoktāni kuṣṭhānīha svalakṣaṇaiḥ||
nava teṣāmasādhyāni cikitsyāni tathaiva ca|
puṇḍarīkamatha śvitraṃ ṛṣyajihvaṃ sakākaṇam||
udumbaraśatāruṣkaṃ carmakuṣṭhaṃ ca yatsmṛtam|
ekakuṣṭhaṃ tu yatproktaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ vaipādikaṃ ca yat||
etāni nava kuṣṭhāni na sidhyanti kadācana|
sidhmaṃ vicarcikā pāmā dadruśca kiṭikāni ca||
kapālakuṣṭhaṃ sthūlārurmaṇḍalaṃ viṣajaṃ ca yat|
etāni nava sādhyāni kuṣṭhānyāhurmanīṣiṇaḥ||
karmajāni nava hyeṣāṃ doṣajāni navaiva tu|
karmajāni na sidhyanti sidhyanti hītarāṇi tu||
ātmavānmucyate tebhyaḥ kuśalena svanuṣṭhitaḥ|
raktodbhavāni kuṣṭhāni sannipātodbhavāni ca||
tasmātteṣāṃ prathamataḥ sirākarma vidhīyate|
kuṣṭhasyātyalpato nyāyyaṃ bahuśaḥ pracchanaṃ smṛtam||
jalūkālābuśṛṅgairvā śoṇitaṃ tasya smṛtam|
śṛṇu raktavikārāṃstu pṛthagdhātusamāśritān||
saphenamaruṇaṃ rūkṣaṃ vātikaṃ śoṇitaṃ tanu|
nīlapītāsitaṃ taptaṃ raktaṃ pittānvitaṃ smṛtam||
vijjalaṃ pāṇḍuraṃ snigdhaṃ tantumacca kaphātmakam|
sarveṣāṃ darśane vidyācchoṇitaṃ sānnipātikam||
indragopakasaṅkāśamaduṣṭaṃ raktamucyate|
narasya muktaraktasya pratibhuktavatastathā||
siddhairghṛtaiḥ snehitasya kuryātsaṃśodhanaṃ tataḥ|
vamanaṃ recanaṃ caiva tathā śīrṣavirecanam|| āsthāpanaṃ ca kurvīta na cainamanuvāsayet||
śālīna saṣṭaṣṭikāṃścaiva jāṅgalāṃśca mṛgadvijān|
yavānnavikṛtīścaiva kuṣṭhī nityaṃ samācaret||
grāmyānūpaudakaṃ māṃsamikṣustilaghṛtaṃ surā|
dadhi dugdhaṃ divāsvapnaḥ phalānyamlāni miathunam||
mūlakaṃ piṣṭavikṛtirvasā haritakāni ca|
kaphaṃ pittaṃ ca raktaṃ ca sarvametatprakopayet||
evaṃvidhāni cānnāni hyabhiṣyandakarāṇi ca|
ajīrṇādhyaśanaṃ caiva kuṣṭhī nityaṃ vivarjayet||
dhānvantaraṃ pibetsarpiḥ svehanārhteṣu kuṣṭhitaḥ|
mahadvai pañcagavyaṃ vā tailaṃ śairīṣameva vā||
pippalīvardhamānaṃ vā mākṣikeṇa samācaret|
ariṣṭamabhayāriṣṭaṃ gaṇḍīrāriṣṭameva vā||
muṣṭiṃ khadirasārasya kuṭṭitaṃ pratibhojayet|
tryahaṃ śṛtaṃ pibettacca snāyādudvartayettataḥ||
tena siddhaṃ ca bhuñjīta pānīyārthaṃ ca kārayet|
pakṣaṃ māsamṛtuṃ vāpi ṣaṇmāsānevamācaret||
prasadya khadiro hanti kuṣṭhāni sugurūṇyapi|
saṃvṛddho lokaparyāye yugāntāgnirnagāniva||
pañcābhayāstu savyoṣāḥ saguḍā vāpi cūrṇitāḥ|
lihyātpathyāśanaḥ kuṣṭhaśāntirvā nacirādbhavet||
gomūtreṇa haridrāṃ tu rasāñjanamathāpi vā|
prayogeṇa pibetkuṣṭhī tathā rogātpramucyate||
drākṣā haridrā mañjiṣṭhā triphalā devadāru ca|
nāgaraṃ pañcamūlyau dve mustā madhurasā tathā||
saptaparṇo hyapāmārgaḥ picumandāṭarūṣakau|
viḍaṅgaṃ citrakaṃ dantī pippalyo maricāni ca||
teṣāṃ tu samabhāgānāṃ kuṣṭhī cūrṇapalaṃ pibet|
māsaṃ gomūtrasaṃyuktaṃ tathā kuṣṭhātpramucyate||
uṣṭrīkṣīraṃ pibejjīrṇe kṣīravṛttirbhavennaraḥ|
jātakrimīṇi kuṣṭhāni cyutaromanakhānyapi||
api vā śīrṇamāṃsāni kṣīramauṣṭraṃ vināśayet|
etāstvābhyantarāḥ proktāḥ kriyāḥ kuṣṭhanibarhaṇāḥ||
bāhyāṃ kriyāṃ pravakṣyāmi vistareṇa nibodha me|
ālepanāni kurvīta muktaraktasya dehinaḥ||
vighṛṣya śastraiḥ patrairvā gomayairapi vā punaḥ|
