vamanīyaṃ yadā vidyāttadainaṃ vāmayedbhiṣak|
vamanairmadyasaṃyuktaiścūrṇaiḥ pācanakairapi||
yavakṣāraiśca saṃsargīṃ tasya kuryāccikitsakaḥ|
na hi peyāḥ praśasyante manuṣyāṇāṃ madātyaye||
madātyaye na śasyante yavāgvo doṣanāśanāḥ|
jāṣyāt pānasya pītasya vegaṃ janayate punaḥ||
tanmadyaśeṣaṃ saṃvṛddhaṃ kurvādghorānupadravān|
tasmātpānātyaye'pathyāḥ snehāḥ peyā yavāgvapi||
sūkṣmāṇi dehasrotāṃsi madyaṃ samanudhāvati|
tiṣṭhatyavayavaiścānyasrotassu viṣameṣvapi||
viṣvagdhṛtāṃstu koṣṭhasthān kopayatyānilādikān|
dhātavaste kharībhūtā vivṛddhāḥ pānadūṣitāḥ||
pānena saha saṃsṛṣṭā janayantyāmayān bahūn|
vyavāyi madyamuddiṣṭaṃ viśeṣeṇa manīṣibhiḥ||
ye tu sūkṣmādiviṣayā madyādīnanuyānti vai|
na hi tānviṣayānanyo rasaśśakto vidhāritum||
tasmātpānātyayārtānāṃ madyamevauṣadhaṃ param|
svā hi yoniśca tatpānaṃ pralīnaṃ koṣṭhamāśritam||
vaśībhūtaṃ prayatnena mahāsrotaḥ prapadyate|
ārtasya madyasaṃyuktānyauṣadhānyapi sarvaśaḥ||
madyātyaye prakurvīta kriyāḥ śītāśca sarvaśaḥ|
ataśca madyasaṃyuktāstarpaṇāḥ khalu dehinaḥ||
deyāścātiviriktāya srotasāṃ śodhanāya ca|
auṣṇyāttaikṣṇyādvikāsitvād vyavāyitvācca raukṣyataḥ||
tulyaṃ hi rasavīryābhyāṃ viṣaṃ madyaṃ ca kīrtitam|
tasmāttattatkriyāssarvā viṣavatsamupācaret||
kledanaṃ tīkṣṇamuṣṇaṃ ca vidāhyañjananāvanam|
tasmāduṣṇodakaṃ svedaṃ dhūmaṃ pānaṃ ca sarpiṣaḥ||
jvālānalaṃ tu sarveṣāṃ yavāgūśca vivarjayet|
dadhi dugdhaṃ ca śākaṃ ca grāmyānūpaudakāni ca||
tilapiṣṭakṛtaṃ caiva pānarogī vivarjayet|
śālīn saṣaṣṭikān mudgān jāṅgalānmṛgapakṣiṇaḥ||
pānātyaye praśaṃsanti bhojaneṣu cikitsakāḥ|
sameṣu dhātukopeṣu vivikteṣu ca me śṛṇu||
atimātraṃ yadā pānaṃ pītaṃ kopayate'nilam|
saṃdṛśya vātikaṃ rūpaṃ tasya kuryāccikitsitam||
sauvarcalavyoṣayutaṃ madyaṃ mākṣikasaṃyutam|
cukraṃ dāḍimasaṃyuktaṃ vātapānātyaye hitam||
toyaṃ madyaṃ ca śuktaṃ ca dadhi tvamlaṃ ca kāñjikam|
samākṣikastarpaṇo'yaṃ vātapānātyaye hitaḥ||
abhyaṅgo mardanaṃ snānaṃ pānānyardhodakāni ca|
vyaktāmlavyoṣasaṃyuktān rasān vrīhīṃśca śīlayet||
chāgalaṃ taittiraṃ krauñcaṃ kaukkuṭaṃ bārhiṇaṃ tathā|
vātapānātyayārtānāṃ bhojanārthaṃ hitaṃ nṛṇām||
āmramāmrātakaṃ bhavyaṃ kapitthaṃ karamardakam|
pūrvakalpena pātavyaṃ pānakaṃ vātarogiṇām||
cavyaṃ sauvarcalaṃ hiṅgu mātuluṅgamahauṣadham|
tathājamodacūrṇaṃ ca madyena saha pāyayet||
pānātyaye pittakṛte pānakaṃ śarkarāyutam|
pibenmākṣikasaṃyuktamardhatoyapariplutam||
kecitpānaṃ tu garhanti pittapānātyaye nṛṇām|
tanmadyānna hi cecchakyaṃ vinā cānyaiścikitsitum||
saṃyuktaṃ taddhi pittaghnairmadyamevāpi sevyate|
vidāryāmalakānāṃ ca rasairikṣurasena vā||
madhūkamadhukāśmaryakharjūraṃ nīlamutpalam|
pānakaṃ madyasaṃyuktaṃ pūrvakalpena sādhayet||
ārdrakṣaumaparicchannā muktāmaṇivibhūṣitāḥ|
śyāmāścandanadigdhāṅgāḥ svapyādāśliṣya yoṣitaḥ||
madyaṃ kharjūrakalkena pibedikṣurasena vā|
pakvaṃ pīlurasaṃ vāpi vārtākīrasameva vā||
raso drākṣāvidārībhyāṃ madyamikṣuraso madhu|
pittapānātyayārtānāmetatpānaṃ hitaṃ nṛṇām||
dṛtiṃ himāmbusampūrṇāṃ gandhamālyasamucchritām|
svapyātpariṣvajya gṛhe pittapānātyayārditaḥ||
sarāṃsi vanarājiśca nalinī phullapaṅkajā|
bhavetpaṅkajapatraiśca śayanaṃ candanokṣitam||
upagūhya mṛṇālāni kalhārakusumāni ca|
śayīta jalasaṃsṛṣṭavyajanairupavījitaḥ||
pānātyaye pittakṛte śastrāśaniviṣopame|
jāṅgalairghṛtasaṃyuktairbhojayedraktaśālikān||
mudgayūṣeṇa vā śītaṃ bhojanmṛdulaudanam|
triphalāyāḥ kaṣāyaṃ vā pibenmadyena saṃyutam||
kṣaudreṇa rasamikṣorvā trivṛtkalkena vā punaḥ|
triphalātvaṅmadhūkāni padmakaṃ padmakesaram||
nīlotpalaṃ mṛṇālāni naladaṃ candanaṃ balām|
siddhaṃ kaṣāyaṃ śītaṃ taiḥ śarkarāmadhusaṃyutam||
kiṃcinmadyena saṃyuktaṃ pittapānātyaye hitam|
drākṣā pīlūni pakvāni kharjūrāṇi parūṣakam||
bhavyaṃ panasamajjā ca majjā tālaphalasya ca|
āpothya tāni sarvāṇi palāṃśāni jalāḍhake||
caturbhāgāvaśeṣaṃ tu nave kumbhe samāvapet|
datvā sitāpalānyaṣṭau palaṃ nīlotpalasya tu||
tatpibetpānakaṃ yuktyā pittapānātyayārditaḥ|
ślaiṣmike pānaroge ca madyena vamanaṃ hitam||
paṭolaṃ picumandaṃ ca madanasya ca pallavam|
guḍūcī cāṭarūṣaśca triphalā pāribhadrakaḥ||
kaṣāyaṃ pāyayedetaṃ śītaṃ mākṣikasaṃyutam|
nityaṃ seveta tiktāni kaṣāyakaṭukāni ca||
jāṅgalāni ca māṃāni seveta mṛgapakṣiṇām|
pibetkuṭannaṭācūrṇaṃ triphalārasasaṃyutam||
dīpanīyāṃśca seveta yogān pānena nityaśaḥ|
vikāre śleṣmaṇi hitaṃ madyaṃ bhūyiṣṭhameva hi||
tasmātpānātyaye madyaṃ ślaiṣmike paricakṣmahe|
eṣā vimānataḥ proktā śleṣmapānātyaye kriyā||
sarvataḥ sannipāte tu sadā kuryāccikitsitam|
ahāni sapta cāṣṭau vā nṛṇāṃ pānātyaye smṛtam||
pānaṃ hi bhajate jīrṇamata ūrdhvaṃ vimārgataḥ|
anena kālayogena yo rogo na nivartate||
kṛtvānupūrvyā pānasya kuryāttasya cikitsitam|
hikkāśvāsādayo rogā ye ca sarve samutthitāḥ||
teṣāṃ cikitsitaṃ kuryādyathā sve sve cikitsite|
iti pānātyayārtānāṃ vistareṇa cikitsitam||
prajāhitamidaṃ proktaṃ śiṣyāṇāmarthasiddhaye|| ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele aṣṭāviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ||
iti bhelasaṃhitāyāṃ cikitsāsthānaṃ samāptam||
<7>
athāto madanakalpaṃ vyākhyāsyāma it ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
piṇḍītakāni tu trīṇi saṃgrahoktāni me śṛṇu|
kṛṣṇaṃ śvetamubhe tacca tritayaṃ madanaṃ smṛtam||
phalānāntveva sarveṣāṃ madanaṃ sarvaśodhanam|
vamanāsthāpane yojyaṃ tathaivāpyanuvāsane||
yadā na paripūrṇān paripakvāni tāni ca|
bhavanti rasapūrṇāni tatastānyuddhared bhiṣak||
yavapalle tathaitāni tuṣapalle tathā punaḥ|
saptarātraṃ vāsayitvā saṃkuñcitaphalatvacā||
subhāvitāni vijñāya mṛdubhūtāni śāstravit|
dvidhā tridhā śodhayitvā samyak saṃśodhya cāvapet||
dhānyeṣu māsaṃ saṃsthāpya mṛdā caivāvalepayet|
nidhūme ca nivāte ca kapāṭapihite gṛhe||
vaihāyase sthāpayecca yathā svedo na saṃbhavet|
eteṣāṃ phalamajjānāṃ sanakhaṃ muṣṭimāharet||
jarjarīkṛtya dṛṣadi pātre cāpyadhivāsayet|
madhukasya kaṣāyeṇa rātrimekāṃ nidhāpayet||
atha cotthāya pūrvāhṇe hastau prakṣālya mardayet|
parisrutaṃ salavaṇaṃ madhuyuktaṃ pratāpya ca||
pāyayedāturaṃ snigdhaṃ chardayettena sādhu nā|
etenaiva ca kalpena jīmūtakaphalairapi||
ikṣvākukuṭajābhyāṃ ca lavaṇaiścāpi kārayet|
kṛtavedhairhastiparṇaiḥ dhāmārgavaphalairapi||
kaṣāyaṃ kārayedebhiḥ phalabījāni bhāvayet|
anena kalpena bhiṣak pāyayettu yathābalam||
madanasya phalānyeva punaruddhārayed bhiṣak|
yathā syurnātipāṇḍūni tathaiva haritānyapi||
paripūrṇāni pakvāni tathā jātarasāni ca|
sūcyā vā kaṇṭakairvātha vyadhayitvā samantataḥ||
madanānāṃ kaṣāyeṇa trirātraṃ bhāvayed bhiṣak|
etenaiva ca kalpena jīmūtakaphalairapi||
ikṣvākukuṭajānāṃ tu dhāmārgavaphalairapi|
kṛtavedhairhāstiparṇaiśchardayettena sādhu nā||
sa khalvetena kalpena kuśānāṃ varuṇasya ca|
kākodumbarikāyāśca kuṣṭhākhyāyāstathaiva ca|| ekaikasya kaṣāyeṇa susnigdhaṃ pāyayed bhiṣak|
balābalamavekṣya ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ||
||tṛtīyo'dhyāyaḥ||
... ... ... ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
ikṣvākūṇyuddhareddhīmān vasante'tha śaradyapi|
yadā prayuṣpitāni syurmadhugandhīni vānti ca||
teṣāṃ kriyāṃ pravakṣyāma ikṣvākūṇāṃ yathā vidhiḥ|
ikṣvākubhiḥ payassiddhaṃ tenaivaṃ vāmayed bhiṣak||
payasā dadhi kṛtvā vā pāyayettu yathābalam|
nirmathya vāmayeccainaṃ navanītena vā naram||
vihitā cekṣvākuṣveṣā samākhyātā kriyā mayā|
vṛntānyārdrāṇi cādāya sthāpayetphāṇitodare||
ekarātrasthitānyatra hastau prakṣālya mardayet|
parisrutaṃ salavaṇaṃ madhuyuktaṃ pratāpya ca||
pāyayedāturaṃ snigdhaṃ charayettena sādhu saḥ|
etenaiva ca kalpena ikṣudarbhekṣuvārikaiḥ||
tathā poṭagalekṣośca kārayettu rasāplutam|
athavā kovidārasya patraissammiśritāni tu||
ikṣvākūṇāṃ pallavāni svedayedgomayāgninā|
tāni svinnāni coddhṛtya kṣodayitvā hyulūkhale||
tataḥ phalakaṣāyeṇa svabhyāsicya samasya ca|
ekarātraṃ kaṣāye'smin svanuguptaṃ nidhāpayet||
pūrvakalpavidhānena vāmayettaṃ yathābalam|
ikṣvākūṇāṃ prasṛtaṃ ca sūkṣmaṃ cūrṇāni kārayet||
kovidārakaṣāyeṇa gulikāḥ kārayedbhiṣak|
etenaiva kaṣāyeṇa vinīya gulikāntu tām||
yathābalaṃ pāyayettu chardayettena sādhu saḥ|
etena khalu kalpena kuśānāṃ tagarasya ca||
etaikasya kaṣāyeṇa susnigdhaṃ vāmayedbhiṣak|
yathābalaṃ yathoddiṣṭaṃ chardayettena sādhu saḥ||
ikṣvākubhiḥ payassiddhaṃ pāyayettu yathābalam|
ikṣvākūṇāmayaṃ kalpo vamanārthāya kīrtitaḥ||
mṛdurabhyāsatassiddhaḥ prayojyo deśakālataḥ|| ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele kalpasthāne tṛtīyo'dhyāyaḥ||
||caturtho'dhyāyaḥ||
athāto dhāmārgavakalpaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
dhāmārgavānyuddhareta vasante vā śaradyapi|
yadā prapuṣpitāni syuḥ madhugandhīni bhānti ca||
same bhūmipradeśe'smin kuśarohiṣasaṃskṛte|
kṛṣṇamṛtsnāprabhe vāpi hyathavā svarṇamṛttike||
vyātape na nadītīre śvabhravalmīkayostathā|
paripūrṇāni pakvāni tathā jātarasāni ca||
bhiṣaguddhṛtya matimān yavapalle nidhāpayet|
vusapalle'tha vā dhīraḥ saptarātraṃ nidhāpayet||
subhāvitāni vijñāya mṛdubhūtāni śāstravit|
jarjarīkṛtya dṛṣadi pātreṣvāsicya nirharet||
kovidārakaṣāyeṇa rātrimekāṃ nidhāpayet|
atha cotthāya pūrvāhṇe hastau prakṣālya mardayet||
parisrutaṃ salavaṇaṃ madhuyuktaṃ pratāpya ca|
pāyayedāturaṃ snigdhaṃ chardayettena sādhu saḥ||
dhāmārgavāṇāmeteṣāṃ muṣṭiṃ saṃgṛhya sannakham|
jarjarīkṛtya dṛṣadi pātreṣvabhyavahārayet||
kovidārakaṣāyeṇa rātrimekāṃ nidhāpayet|
pūrvoktenaiva kalpena vāmayettu yathābalam||
naladasya kaṣāyeṇa tālīsaśatapuṣpayoḥ|
kuṣṭhasya mūrvāpāṭhāyāḥ pūrvakalpena chardayet||
dhāmārgavāñjaliṃ pūrṇaṃ sūkṣmacūrṇāni kārayet|
śyāmākaṣāye prakṣipya caikarātraṃ yathāvidhi||
tathā phalakaṣāye'sminnikṣvākukṛtavedhane|
jīmūtake hastiparṇe kaṣāye paribhāvayet||
tata uddhṛtya saṃśoṣya sūkṣmacūrṇāni kārayet|
kṛsarāṃ tena saṃyuktāṃ nātyuṣṇāṃ pāyayennaram||
utpalaṃ puṇḍarīkaṃ ca naladaṃ kumudaṃ tathā|
tena cūrṇena saṃspṛśya ghrātumasmai pradāpayet||
sirāhṛdayamāpnoti yāvattatkila miśrakam|
tvaritaṃ tāni puṣpāṇi ghrātumasmai pradāpayet||
ghrātuṃ punaḥ punarmadhyaṃ cūrṇayitvā muhurmuhuḥ|
anenaiva ca kalpena sukhaṃ vamati mānavaḥ||
dhāmārgavāñjaliṃ pūrṇaṃ sūkṣmacūrṇāni kārayet|
tridhā vibhajya matimān svanuguptaṃ nidhāpayet||
atha bilvakaṣāyeṇa dvau bhāgau ścyotayedbhiṣak|
cyute kaṣāye vimale svanuguptaṃ nidhāpayet||
bhāgaṃ tu gulikāḥ kāryāḥ kolakarkandhusammitāḥ|
kovidārakaṣāyeṇa pūrvakalpena chardayet||
karkandhūbadarāṇāṃ ca kolānāṃ vāpi sīdhunā|
gulikāṃ vinīya vidhivacchardayettena sādhu saḥ||
rohitasya ca matsyasya tathā karkaṭakasya ca|
pakṣiṇāmaudakānāṃ ca rasena tu vametsukham||
etena khalu kalpena cāmlaiśca madhusīdhubhiḥ|
gulikāṃ vinīya vidhivat pāyayettāṃ yathābalam|| ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele caturtho'dhyāyaḥ||
||pañcamo'dhyāyaḥ||
athātaḥ kuṭajakalpaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
vasante vā śaradi vā kuṭajānyuddhared bhiṣak|
śuciśśuddhaśśiraḥsnātaḥ puṣyeṇāśvayujena vā||
pratūṭhāni śucau deśe kuśarohiṣasaṃskṛte|
kṛṣṇamṛtsnāprabhe vāpi tathā vā svarṇamṛttike||
vyātape na nadītīre śvabhravalmīkayostathā|
paripūrṇāni pakvāni tathā jātarasāni ca||
kuṭajānāmathaiteṣāṃ muṣṭiṃ saṅgṛhya sannakham|
kovidārakaṣāyeṇa rātrimekāṃ nidhāpayet||
atha cotthāya pūrvāhṇe hastau prakṣālya mardayet|
parisrutaṃ salavaṇaṃ madhuyuktaṃ pratāpya ca||
pāyayedāturaṃ snigdhaṃ chardayettena sādhu saḥ|
anenaiva ca kalpena kaṣāyaṃ kārayed bhiṣak||
kovidārasya bījaiśca nīpasya vidulasya ca|
gulūcyāśca suṣvyāśca paṭolapicumandayoḥ||
kṛtvā madhūlikāyāśca kaṣāyaṃ tena chardayet|
eṣāmeva phalānāntu muṣṭiṃ saṃgṛhya sannakham||
madhukasya kaṣāyeṇa taccūrṇaṃ parimardayet||
gulikāḥ kārayettena karkandhubadaropamāḥ|
kovidārakaṣāyeṇa pūrvakaloena chardayet||
yathābalaṃ yathoddiṣṭaṃ chardayettena sādhu saḥ|
kṛtavedhanakalpo'yamukto vamanavistare||
ya evaṃ samprajānāti sa rājñāṃ kartumarhati|| ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele kalpasthāne pañcamo'dhyāyaḥ||
||ṣaṣṭho'dhyāyaḥ||
athātaścaturaṅgulīyaṃ kalpaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
manojñadeśajātastu yo bhaveccaturaṅgulaḥ|
phalamuṣṭiṃ samāhṛtya tataścūrṇāni kārayet||
taccūrṇitaṃ tridhākṛtya bhāgamekaṃ nidhāpayet|
dvau bhāgau ca śucissamyak kṛtvā manthanameva tu||
ścyotayitvā parisrutya suprasannamanāvilam|
paribhāgaṃ samāvāpya sādhayedgomayāgninā||
tasmiṃstathā sicyamāne sūkṣmāṇīmāni dāpayet|
harītakīmāmalakaṃ vibhītakaphalāni ca||
madhūkabhāgamāvāpya tadvilepanamāharet|
tataḥ pāṇitalaṃ cūrṇaṃ lehyaṃ tena viricyate||
etenaiva ca kalpena kaṇṭakāryāḥ phalairapi|
ścyotayitvā lehajātaṃ paritaḥ sāsavaṃ pibet||
caturaṅgulasiddhā vā peyā yojyā susaṃskṛtā|
viricyate tena sādhu nāyogo nātiyogitā||
dhātrīphalarasadroṇe śarkarārdhatulāṃ pacet|
ghṛtaṃ catuṣpalaṃ pakvaṃ pṛthagardhapalaṃ kṣipet||
nataṃ vellaṃ caturjātaṃ yaṣṭisaindhavajīrakam|
palāśaṃ viśvamaricaṃ mṛdvīkāyāścatuṣpalam||
prasthaṃ kaṇātrivṛtayormadhunaśca nihanti tat|
tatkhaṇḍāmalakaṃ nāma pāṇḍuśvayathukāmilām||
śirobhramaṇamunmādamamlapittavikārajit||
bhallātakānāṃ pavanāhatānāṃ vṛntacyutānāmiha cāṭhakaṃ syāt|
tadiṣṭakācūrṇakaṇairvighṛṣya prakṣālayitvā visṛjetpravāte||
śuṣkaṃ punastadvidalīkṛtaṃ ca vinikṣipedapsu caturguṇāsu|
pādāvaśiṣṭaṃ paripūtaśītaṃ kṣīreṇa tulyena punaḥ paceta||
tatpādaśiṣṭaṃ punareva śītaṃ ghṛtena tulyena punaḥ paceta|
tadardhayā śarkarayāvagāṭhaṃ lohābhayāvyoṣakacūrṇayuktam||
etatsamaṃ śārkarapādayuktaṃ tataḥ khajenonmathitaṃ nidhāya|
prasthadvayenāmalakīmadhūnāṃ śīte'tha dhānyena punaḥ paceta||
tatsaptarātrādupajātavīryaṃ sudhārasādapyadhikatvameti|
prātarviśuddhīkṛtadehabhājāṃ mātrāṃ dadītātmaśarīrayogyām||
na cānnapāne parihāryamāste na coṣṇavātādhvani maithune ca|
janturnitāntaṃ narasihmavatsyādbhavennaraḥ kāñcanarāśigauraḥ||
dantāśca śīrṇāḥ punarudbhavanti keśāśca śuklāḥ punareva kṛṣṇāḥ|
viśīrṇakarṇāṅgulināsiko'pi kṛmyardito bhinnagalo'pi kuṣṭhī||
so'pi krameṇāṅguligātraśākhastaruryathā rohati vārisiktaḥ|
mahāmayūrāñjayati svareṇa balena nāgāṃsturagān javena||
... ... ... ... ... sudhākṣīreṇa bhāvayet|
tatpibettu yathotsāhaṃ tena sādhu viricyate||
dantyakṣatā tu tejohvā tarkārī sudurālabhā|
kaṣāyakvātha ityetatsudhākṣīreṇa miśrayet||
tatpibettu yathotsāhaṃ tena sādhu viricyate|
sudhākṣīreṇa gokṣīramekasthamabhisaṃsṛjet||
tatpibettu yathotsāhaṃ tena sādhu viricyate|
kṛtamūtrapurīṣasya vāte cāpyanulomite||
prathamaṃ snigdhakoṣṭhasya svinnasya ca yathāvidhi|
sandhyāvelāmatikramya vimale ca divākare|
maitre muhūrte vyakte tu yogānetān prayojayet|| ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|| iti bhele kalpasthāne ṣaṣṭho'dhyāyaḥ||
||saptamo'dhyāyaḥ||
athāto dantīphalakalpaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
... ... ... ... ... ... ... ...
tridhā vibhajya matimān svanuguptaṃ nidhāpayet|
rasenāmalakasyāpi dvau bhāgau ścyotayedbhiṣak||
cyutaṃ kaṣāyaṃ vimalaṃ pariyogamadhiśrayet|
tasmiṃstathā pacyamāne taccūrṇamabhisaṃsṛjet||
tanmātraṃ triphalācūrṇamāvapenmadhusaṃyutam|
lehabhūtaṃ viditvainamathainamavatārayet||
tasya pāṇitalaṃ cūrṇaṃ lehyaṃ tena viricyate|
eteṣāṃ caiva mūlānāṃ kṛtvā cūrṇāni sūkṣmaśaḥ||
śyāmākaṣāyeṇāloḍya ghṛtaṃ siddhaṃ vipācayet|
jānīyāttadyadā siddhamathainamavatārayet||
tad dṛḍhe madhulipte tu nave kumbhe samāvapet|
mṛtsnābhiḥ svanulipte tu svanuguptaṃ nidhāpayet||
prasṛtaṃ pāyayettatra tena sādhu viricyate|
eteṣāṃ caiva mūlānāṃ kṛtvā cūrṇāni sarvaśaḥ||
bṛhatīrasasammiśraiḥ gulikāṃ kārayettu taiḥ|
gulikāṃ kārayitvā tu karkandhubadaropamām||
śyāmākaṣāyeṇāloḍya dve vā tisro'pi vā tataḥ|
pāyayettu yathotsāhaṃ taṃ snigdhasveditaṃ naram||
viricyate tena sādhu nāyogo nātiyogitā|
snehādi snehamūlaṃ tu pracchardanavirecanam|
nāparisnigdhakoṣṭhasya prasidhyati viśodhanam|| ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele kalpasthāne saptamo'dhyāyaḥ||
||aṣṭhamo'dhyāyaḥ||
athātaḥ śaṅkhinīkalpaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
phalāni paripakvāni śaṅkhinyāstūddharedbhiṣak|
antarā caitravaiśākhamavyagro yavasaṃgrahe||
anūṣare niruddhāre kṣetre sūryapratāpite|
kṛṣṇamṛtsnāprabhe vāpi tathā ca svarṇamṛttike||
na vyagre na ca valmīke na cāpi tṛṇachādite|
susame bhūpradeśe tu yā jātā śaṅkhinī bhavet||
tasyā droṇaṃ samāvāpya tadbhāṇḍaṃ siddhabandhanam|
yavaparṇoṣitaṃ cāpi samuddhṛtya nidhāpayet||
saptarātraṃ vārdhamāsaṃ samācchādya samantataḥ|
ātape pariśuṣkāṇi sūkṣmacūrṇāni kārayet||
phalānāmatha caiteṣāṃ dvau bhāgau kārayedbhiṣak|
tilaṃ tṛtīyakaṃ kṛtvā kṣodayitvā hyulūkhale||
samyag dṛṣadi niṣpiṣya tailamuddhārayettataḥ|
śaṅkhinyā hyakṣamātraṃ tu trivṛtkalkamathāpi vā||
tasmiṃstaile payaścaiva bhiṣagdadyāccaturguṇam|
etatsarvaṃ samāloḍya pācayenmṛdunāgninā||
pakvamāhṛtya visrāvya svanuguptaṃ nidhāpayet|
tasyāñjaliṃ prakuñcaṃ vā prasṛtaṃ vāpi pāyayet||
viricyate tena sādhu nāyogo nātiyogitā|
susūkṣmasyātha śaṅkhinyāścūrṇasya prasṛtaṃ śṛtam||
kṣīrapātreṇa saṃyojya pariyogamadhiśrayet|
tathā vipācya visrāvya śītīkṛtyābhimanthayet||
tatra yannavanītaṃ syāttaduddhṛtya vipācayet|
śaṅkhinīphalakalkena kṣīreṇa salilena ca||
sa pibettadyathotsāhaṃ tena sādhu viricyate|
śaṅkhinīphalakalkantu samyak dṛṣadi peṣayet||
gokṣīreṇa samāloḍya pibettena viricyate|
etenaiva ca kalpena hyuṣṭrīkṣīreṇa pāyayet||
ajākṣīre'vikākṣīre māhiṣe vātha vā punaḥ|
gomūtreṇoṣṭramūtreṇa cāvimūtreṇa vā punaḥ||
athavā hastimūtreṇa pibettena viricyate|
āmṛdgannatha mṛdvīkāṃ dāpayetphāṇitodake||
bhṛṣṭvā tu śaṅkhinītaile pariyogamadhiśrayet|
ardhaṃ cūrṇāni pippalyā harītakyāśca dāpayet||
ataḥ pāṇitalaṃ prāśya tena sādhu viricyate|
śaṅkhinīphalamadhyāni dātavyaṃ lavaṇaṃ tathā||
śaṅkhinītailabhṛṣṭāni phāṇitenātha saṃsṛjet|
godhūmājyena saṃsṛjya modakān kārayedbhiṣak||
tāṃstrīn dvau vātha saṃbhakṣya tatassādhu viricyate|
surāṃ sauvīrakaṃ takraṃ dadhimastu tuṣodakam||
kaṭvaraṃ kolasīdhuṃ vā dadhi pakvarasaṃ tathā|
karkandhubadarāṇāṃ vā sīdhuṃ pīlupriyālayoḥ||
bhavyapārāvatānāṃ ca mṛdvīkāmadyameva vā|
bilvaśigrukapitthānāṃ rasānvā saha pāyayet||
ayaṃ hi śaṅkhinīkalpo mayā tu samudāhṛtaḥ|
ya evaṃ samprajānāti sa rājñāṃ kartumarhati|| ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele kalpasthāne'ṣṭamo'dhyāyaḥ||
||navamo'dhyāyaḥ||
athātaḥ śyāmātrivṛtkalpaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
śyāmāyāstrivṛtayāśca mūlānyuddhārayedbhiṣak|
kalyāṇadeśajātāyāḥ kuśarohiṣasaṃskṛte||
kṛṣṇamṛtsnāprame vāpi hyathavā svarṇamṛttike|
mūlānyetāni saṃgṛhya sūkṣmacūrṇāni kārayet||
sthirākaṣāye saṃsṛjya pāyayettu yathābalam|
viricyate tena sādhu nāyogo nātiyogitā||
triphalārasamātreṇa drākṣāpīlurasena vā|
āloḍya vāpi payasā sarpiṣā vāpi pāyayet||
hariṇasaya rasaṃ dadyānnyaṅkośca mahiṣasya ca|
khaḍgasya kalaviṅkasya tittirerlāvukasya ca||
rasamanyatamaṃ teṣāṃ trivṛcchyāmāprayojitam|
yathābalaṃ pāyayettu susnigdhaṃ sveditaṃ naram||
śyāmāyāstrivṛtāyāśca kṛtvā cūrṇāni sūkṣmaśaḥ|
tridhā vibhajya tatraikaṃ svayaṃ guptaṃ nidhāpayet||
rasena tu priyaṅgūṇāṃ dvau bhāgau ścyotayedbhiṣak|
bhājane tu parisrāvya suprasannamanāvilam||
pariyogamadhiśritya pācayenmṛdunāgninā|
tasmiṃstathā pacyamāne cūrṇānyetāni dhāpayet||
priyaṅguṃ tagaraṃ caiva tālīsaṃ naladaṃ tathā|
hīberaṃ caiva mustāṃ ca balāṃ kālānusāribām||
aśokapuṣpacūrṇāni padmasaugandhikāni ca|
nalinaṃ puṇḍarīkaṃ ca pippalīmūlameva ca||
madhunā saha saṃsṛjya lihyātpāṇitalonmitam|
susnigdho jīrṇabhaktaśca yathāśāstraṃ yathābalam||
viricyate tena sādhu nāyogo nātiyogitā|
candanaṃ padmakaṃ nīpaṃ sālaṃ saptacchadaṃ tathā||
punarnavāṃ tu palalaṃ kirātaṃ tiktameva vā|
ye cānye tiktamadhurā rasāḥ pūrvaṃ prakīrtitāḥ||
teṣāṃ kaṣāyairyojyaṃ syātpaittikānāṃ virecanam|
pārāvatān kapotāṃśca krauñcabarhiṇakukkuṭān||
kuraṅgān hariṇāneṇān haritān kālapucchakān|
vartakān tittirīṃścaiva śaśalāvakapiñjalān||
rasaistairupayuñjīta paittikasya virecanam|
sārāḥ phalāni cūrṇāni patrāṇi ca payāṃsi ca||
ya uktāḥ sthāvarāḥ pūrvaṃ tathaiva mṛgapakṣiṇaḥ|
pānāni phalaniṣkvāthakaṣāyādidravān rasān||
lehān bhakṣyāṃśca sānnādīn tebhyaḥ samabhikalpayet|
parūṣakasya drākṣāyā rasaṃ samadhuśarkaram||
trivṛdakṣasamāyuktaṃ pītvā sādhu viricyate|
sādhayeccharkarāyuktaṃ sahaiva madhunā bhiṣak||
śīte tasmiṃstrivṛccūrṇaṃ yathākoṣṭhaṃ samāvapet|
tataḥ kampillakāccūrṇaṃ dve ca te tindukasya ca||
āragvadhaphalānmadhyabhāgāścākṣasamāstathā|
trivṛccūrṇasya dharaṇaṃ śarkarā madhukaṃ madhu||
maricaṃ nāgapuṣpaṃ ca tvakpatraṃ ca sucūrṇitam|
etatsarvaṃ samāyojya modakaṃ kārayedbhiṣak||
atha vā kārayellehaṃ tena sādhu viricyate|
drākṣārasasya kuḍavaṃ parūṣakarasasya ca||
madhunaḥ kuḍavārdhaṃ ca śarkarāpalameva ca|
trivṛccūrṇakṛto lehastena sādhu viricyate||
etadutsannadoṣāṇāmīśvarāṇāṃ virecanam|
śarkarāmodakaṃ vāpi guḍakaṃ māṣapūrakam||
anena vidhinā kuryāt paittikānāṃ virecanam|
trivṛccūrṇāni pippalyāḥ phalānyāragvadhasya ca||
kalkānakṣasamānetān pibeddhīro virecanam|
trivṛccūrṇasya dharaṇaṃ śarkarā madhukaṃ madhu||
etadakṣasamāyuktaṃ pītvā sādhu viricyate|
trivṛccūrṇāni pippalyo yavakṣāro mahauṣadham||
śleṣmakopaśamārthāya lihyāttena viricyate|
avimūtramajāmūtramuṣṭrasya mahiṣasya ca||
mṛgasya mūtraṃ gomūtraṃ gardabhasya dvipasya ca|
etānyaṣṭau prayuñjīta pṛthagvā yadi vā saha||
trivṛccūrṇavimiśrāṇi vilihyānmadhunāpi vā|
ajātāni ca madyāni tīkṣṇāni niśi tāni ca||
trivṛccūrṇavimiśrāṇi pītvā sādhu viricyate|
trivṛccūrṇaṃ yathākoṣṭhaṃ ślaiṣmikaḥ pātumarhati||
trivṛtāyāḥ kaṣāyeṇa sakṣaudraṃ sāmlavetasam|
atha vā dadhimaṇḍena kolakānāṃ rasena vā||
kulutthamudgayūṣairvā satīnaciribilvayoḥ|
yavakolakayūṣeṇa pibetkaulatthameva vā||
yuktaṃ vā taintraṇīkena sāmlavetasadāḍimam|
yūṣeṇa śigrukolāhvāmūlakānāṃ rasena vā||
rasenāmalakānāṃ vā bījapūrārjakasya vā|
kapittharasamaṇḍena karamardarasena vā||
yaccānyadapi vā kiñcitphalamamlamiti smṛtam|
pibettatsvarasairvāpi pañcamūlīrasena vā||
kaṭukalkavimiśraṃ vā yavakṣāreṇa saṃyutam|
mṛdvīkāphalakalkena lehārthamupakalpayet||
pratyekasya phalānāṃ ca pūrvoktānāṃ cikitsakaḥ|
sujātānāṃ supakvānāṃ pratyagraṃ grāhayedrasam||
tatrāmlalvaṇībhūte viripyātsaindhave bhavet|
lehe'smin trivṛtaścūrṇaṃ yathākoṣṭhaṃ samāvapet||
sakṣaudraṃ saguḍaṃ vāpi lihyātsakaṭukaṃ tathā|
etena vidhinā kuryānmodakān ṣāḍabānrasān||
pānāni ca phalāmlāni bhakṣyaṃ bhojyaṃ ca saṃskṛtam|
etadrājñāṃ samākhyātamīśvarāṇāṃ virecanam||
vidhimevaṃ prayuñjīta tvimaṃ cānyaṃ cikitsakaḥ|
śāṇamāmalakānāṃ ca vibhītakaphalāni ca||
tilvakassaptalā dantī śaṅkhinī caturaṅgulaḥ|
harītakīnāmekaṃ ca yavakṣāro vacaiva hi||
pippalīnāṃ ca śāṇassyādviḍaṅgācchāṇa eva ca|
trivṛccūrṇasya triśśāṇāstatsarvamavacūrṇayet||
tāni cūrṇāni madhunā sarpiṣā vā guḍena vā|
lehaṃ vā vidhinā ... ... ... ... ||
<8>
riktakoṣṭhasya vamane mārutaḥ samprakupyati|
plīhā pracalati sthānāttasmāddoṣānnihantyasūn||
peyāṃ maṇḍaṃ pibettasmātsusnigdhaṃ lavaṇānvitam|
saumye tithau muhūrte ca nakṣatre karaṇe śubhe||
śuklavastraḥ śucirbhūtvā hyarcayitvā dvijanmanaḥ|
tato'smai vamanaṃ deyaṃ pūjayitvā bhiṣagvarān||
prāṅmukhena tu pātavyaṃ nivāte śaraṇe śubhe|
na cāsya kuryādāyāsaṃ maṇḍaṃ bhojyaṃ praśasyate||
vidhireṣa bhavetkāryo vamane ca virecane|
niṣkvāthasya trayaḥ prasthāḥ māgadhāstu kanīyasi||
jyeṣṭhe tu puruṣe jyeṣṭhaṃ vamanaṃ na kanīyasi|
catuḥprasthaṃ tu madhyaṃ syājjyeṣṭhaṃ prasthāstu te nava||
kanīyo madhyamaṃ jyeṣṭhaṃ trividhāni pṛthak pṛthak|
apītapūrvaṃ vamanaṃ vireko vāpi yena tu||
kanīyasā pramāṇena parīkṣeta ca buddhimān|
tripalaṃ śreṣṭhamākhyātaṃ kanīyastu palaṃ bhavet||
madhyaṃ dve pale vidyāditi me niścitā matiḥ|
viṣakuṣṭhahatā ye tu kāsaśvāsaprapīḍitāḥ||
taruṇā balavantaśca vāmayeduttamena tān|
madhyame prāṇavayasi madhyamaṃ tatra kārayet||
kanīyasi kanīystu tathā prāṇe kanīyasi|
viparītaṃ prayuñjāno na rogānvijayiṣyati||
upadravānvā janayedāturaṃ vā vināśayet|
aṅkolasyātha nimbasya niṣkvāthaṃ sādhu yojayet||
sarpiṣā bheṣajaiścemaiḥ sukhoṣṇaṃ śītameva vā|
madanasya phalaṃ piṣṭvā devadālīphalaissaha||
nimbapatrāṇi sārdrāṇi saindhavaṃ maricāni ca|
saindhavena samāyuktaṃ takramaṇḍena vā punaḥ||
bandhujīvo'tha pippalyo madanaṃ sarpireva ca|
tatkaṣāyeṇa saṃyuktaṃ pradhānaṃ vamanauṣadham||
madanaṃ bandhujīvaśca śatapuṣpā śukānanā|
saindhavena samāyuktaṃ pradhānaṃ vamanauṣadham||
madanasya ca patrāṇi puṣpāṇi saphalāni ca|
saindhavaṃ bandhujīvaśca rājakośātakāni ca||
evaṃ pracchardano yogaḥ pradhānaṃ vamanauṣadham|
śreṣṭhaśca viṣayukteṣu kāsaśvāse tu pūjitaḥ||
yadā tu vamanaṃ pītaṃ na chardayati mānavam|
kiṃ tatra bheṣajaṃ kuryāt kathaṃ vaidyaḥ samācaret||
darśayettasya tu bhiṣak pālitāni pṛthak pṛthak|
makṣikākeśacūrṇāni tacca maṇḍe niyojayet||
etena tu prayogeṇa kṣipraṃ chardayate naraḥ|
eṣā kriyā samākhyātā durbalānāṃ viśeṣataḥ||
hṛdayaṃ pṛṣṭhadeśaṃ ca vigṛhya ca vamennaraḥ|
tathāsya calati sthānātpītaṃ hyasminnupakrame||
kaphaṃ hi pūrvaṃ vamati tataḥ pittamanantaram|
pittasthānte bhavedvātaḥ tato vamati śoṇitam||
lakṣaṇāni tu vakṣyāmi samyagvāntasya dehinaḥ|
laghurāmāśaye śuddhirhṛcchuddhiścāpyatandritā||
kṣutpipāse yathākālaṃ srotaḥśuddhiryathāgnitā|
ślaiṣmikāṇāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ vyādhīnāṃ vinivartanam||
na ca vairasyamāsyasya samyagvāntasya lakṣaṇam|
hṛllāso'tha sirāharṣo manyā kaṇṭhaśca dīryate||
atyudgārastamaḥ kāso hikkā śvāsaḥ svarakṣayaḥ|
vātajvaraḥ śuṣkavamistṛṣṇā śūlo hyanidratā||
āsyahṛcchoṣaṇaṃ caiva kṣatācca rudhirāgamaḥ|
indriyāṇyuparudhyante mūrcchā cāsyopajāyate||
hanustambhaśca kampaśca hyativānte pravartate|
ūrdhvaraktī pramehī ca gulmī cārśobhagandarī||
udarī pāṇḍurogī ca hṛdrogī kṛmikoṣṭhakaḥ|
apasmārī viṣārtaśca śophastambhaśca vātikaḥ||
viṣamajvaravīsarpī vātaśoṇitikaśca yaḥ|
arśo'tiviṣamāgniśca kuṣṭhaśvayathupīḍitaḥ||
udāvartī tathonmādī pipluvyaṅganipīḍitaḥ|
pittaśleṣmasamutthānā vyākulā ye ca dhātavaḥ||
sannipātasamutthāśca yoniduṣṭāśca yāḥ striyaḥ|
śūlārtā raktagulminyo yāśca saumyena pīḍitāḥ||
asnigdhāraktatāṅgānāṃ lāghavaṃ gātramārdavam|
hṛdayodgāramūtrāṇāṃ śuddhirvātānulomataḥ||
prasannasvaravarṇatvaṃ bubhukṣā vyādhinigrahaḥ|
śleṣmaṇā ca svayaṃ sthānaṃ suviriktasya laksaṇam||
udaraṃ vātasampūrṇaṃ kaṇḍūmaṇḍalasambhavaḥ|
piṭakājanma gātreṣu bhramaṇaṃ jvarasamplavaḥ||
hṛdayāveṣṭanaṃ vahneḥ sādassyātkukṣigauravam|
kṛcchraviṇmūtravātatvaṃ durviriktasya lakṣaṇam||
pārśvaśūlaṃ gudabhraṃśaḥ śākhāsaṃkocasambhramaḥ|
vātavṛddhirvisaṃjñatvaṃ gudaśūlastṛṣā klamaḥ||
śūnyatā jarjarībhāvastataścimacimāyanam|
rūpāṇyativiriktānāṃ śūlaḥ śophaśca jāyate||
gaurave durviriktasya yadajīrṇaṃ tadullikhet|
sātmyaṃ tu peyaṃ bhaiṣajyaṃ nirūho gāḍhavarcasām||
rūpāṇyativiriktasya haretkṣīraghṛtādibhiḥ|
snehaiścaivopanāhaiśca sābhyaṅgaiśca bhiṣagjayet|| ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ...
||dvitīyo'dhyāyaḥ||
... ... ... ... ... ... ... ...
avapīḍānuvāsāśca dhūmāḥ pradhamanāni ca|
caturvidhaṃ nasyamāhuḥ pṛthaktvena tu me śṛṇu|| 
galagraho rohiṇikā śaṅkhako'rdhāvabhedakaḥ|
pratiśyāyaśca kāsaśca śvāso hikkā biḍālikā||
mukharogo hyapasmāro dantatālukavidradhī|
dantapuppuṭakaścaiva pūtināsāmukhaṃ tathā||
abhiṣyandāśca catvārastandrā ye netrasaṃbhavāḥ|
sārbudāścāpyadhīmanthāḥ nāsārśaśca bhagandarāḥ||
upajihvā galagranthiḥ kaṇṭhaśālūka eva ca|
galaśuṇṭhikālasake kāryaṃ śīrṣavirecanam||
maricāni vilaṅgāni pippalyo'tha phaṇirjakaḥ|
kṣavako yavanaścaiva śigrubījaṃ mayūrakaḥ||
hiṅgu sauvarcalaṃ bhārṅgī naktamālaphalāni ca|
jyotiṣmatī śṛṅgiberaṃ śairīṣaṃ bījameva ca||
etacchīrṣavirekārthamuktavyādhiṣu yojayet|
avapīḍe'tha dhūme vā dvandvaśaḥ sarvaśo'pi vā||
etairevauṣadhagaṇaistailaṃ dhīro vipācayet|
avimūtreṇa saṃyuktaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ śīrṣavirecanam||
dhūmaṃ ca sarvagandhānāṃ natakuṣṭhavivarjitam|
dhūmavartiṃ pibettāvacchreṣṭhaṃ śīrṣavirecanam||
śirasastu virekeṇa karṇajihvākṣināsikāḥ|
viśudhyante yathādoṣaṃ mukhanāsāvaheṣu ca||
vivṛtiḥ kheṣu viśadā na ca śleṣmā prasicyate|
samyak śirasi saṃśuddhe vedanā copaśāmyati||
vyādhau ca bhaṅgacūrṇatvaṃ srotasāṃ suviśuddhatā|
tathaivātiviśuddheṣu srotassvāyasyate śiraḥ||
dantacāle hanustambhe manyāstambhe śirograhe|
vādhirye karṇaśūle ca karṇamūlācca bhedane||
apatantre keśasāde'prabodhe dṛṣṭivibhrame|
hikkānetramukhāsrāve svarabhede'tha vāggrahe||
gaṇḍoṣṭhamukhacāleṣu kāceṣu timireṣu ca|
daurgandhye mukhanāsābhyāṃ pālitye vāpyakālaje||
ūrdhvajatrugatā ye ca rogāssamparikīrtitāḥ|
nasyakarma hitaṃ teṣāṃ tailaṃ vā sarpireva vā||
jīvakarṣabhakau drākṣā pippalyo madhukaṃ balā|
prapauṇḍarīkaṃ medā ca śarkarā nīlamutpalam||
nidigdhikā cāṃśumatī nīlikā saindhavaṃ vacā|
rāsnā śvadaṃṣṭrā mañjiṣṭhā bṛhatī sapunarnavā||
ebhiḥ palasamaiḥ siddhaṃ tailaṃ vā yadi vā ghṛtam|
caturguṇena payasā nasyaṃ tatparamucyate||
nasyataḥ snehapānena kṛṣṇaromā dṛḍhendriyaḥ|
avalīpalitavyaṅgaḥ cakṣuṣmāñjāyate naraḥ||
karṇākṣināsāvaktrāṇāṃ snehenāvṛtya tarpaṇam|
indriyāṇāṃ vimuktatvaṃ prasādaḥ svaravarṇayoḥ||
srotasāṃ vimalatvaṃ ca jñeyaṃ saṃsnigdhalakṣaṇam|
asnigdhaliṅgaṃ rūkṣatvaṃ srotasāṃ riktatā tathā||
śiraso guruduḥkhatvaṃ srotasāṃ snehapūrṇatā|
karṇatālūpadehaśca nasyātisnigdhalakṣaṇam||
nājīrṇe nārdraśirasi panthānaṃ gantumicchataḥ|
na pītamātre pānīye jvaritasya na dāpayet||
na maithunaṃ gatavataḥ madyapītasya naiva ca|
nātibhuktasya nāsnigdhasvinnasyānirvṛtestathā||
samyaṅ nirūḍhasya narugujā samyak praśāmyati|
yato mūtrapurīṣāmyāṃ vāto hyasya nivartate||
nirūḍhaṃ taṃ bhiṣagvidyādanirūḍhasya lakṣaṇam|
tathaiva vyādhirādhmānaṃ śūlaṃ viṇmūtranigrahaḥ||
pūrṇakukṣitvamānāho durnirūḍhasya lakṣaṇam|
nirūḍhasya punaryasya śoṇitaṃ sampraduṣyati||
atīva tu nirūḍhasya niṣpurīṣasya mārutaḥ|
śūlenāveṣṭyamanasya sarvagātrāṇi pīḍayet||
iti pañcavidhaṃ coktaṃ vyākhyātaṃ samupācaret|
pañcakarmavidhānajño rājārho bhiṣagucyate|| ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele dvitīyo'dhyāyaḥ||
||caturtho'dhyāyaḥ||
athāto daśavyāpadīyāṃ vamanavirecanasiddhiṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
adhobhāgordhvabhāgāni bheṣajāni prayatnataḥ|
kalpayenmatimānvaidyo viṣaśastranibhāni tu||
kaṭutiktakaṣāyāṇi vidāhīnītarāṇi ca|
sūkṣmāṇyuṣṇāni tīkṣṇāni viṣyandīni gurūṇi ca||
tāni samprāpya hṛdayaṃ dhamanīḥ pratipadya hi|
tiryagūrdhvamadhaścaiva doṣānniṣyandayantyapi||
sūkṣmāñcharīrāvayavān saukṣmyādanusarinti ca|
visrāvayanti dhātūṃśca vimārgaurapi dehinām||
ānayantīva hṛdayaṃ doṣāṃstānatimūrcchitān|
āmāśayaṃ pūriyitvāhyūrdhvabhāgabhavāṃstathā||
evameva ca biṣyandīnyāpūryāmāśayaṃ punaḥ|
adhobhāge pravartante vikāreṣviha dehinām||
viṣyandayanti doṣāṃśca yāvattiṣṭhanti dehinām|
saṃśodhayantyataḥ paścādūrdhvaṃ vā yadi vāpyadhaḥ||
teṣāṃ tu duṣprayuktānāṃ vibhrāntānāṃ vidhāvatām|
ayogāccātiyogācca jāyante vyāpado daśa||
parisrāvastathādhmānaṃ vibandho gudaniḥsravaḥ|
hṛdrogaścaiva śūlaṃ ca tathaiva parikartikā||
jīvādānaṃ śirorogo daśamī ca pravāhikā|
tāsāmekaikatassiddhiṃ vyāpadāṃ tu nibodha me||
athaiti pītaṃ vamanaṃ tacca tīkṣṇataraṃ pibet|
anupānānna siddhiśca saṃśodhyāsthāpayennaram||
asidhyati vireke'pi bhūyastamanuvāsayet|
asminnapi jarāṃ yāti tryahādūrdhvaṃ pibed ghṛtam||
snehasvedopapannaśca laghu ca pratibhojitaḥ|
virecanaṃ ca vamanaṃ narastīkṣṇataraṃ pibet||
yadā virecanaṃ pītaṃ vamanāyopapadyate|
prayoga evaṃ vamane vireke'pi ca yaḥ smṛtaḥ||
snehasvedavimuktaṃ ca krūrakoṣṭhaṃ ca dehinam|
ajīrṇe cauṣadhaṃ pītamādhyāpayati mānavam||
laṅghitasya subhaktasya rūkṣasyānaśanasya ca|
parikartaḥ parisrāvaḥ pralepo'sveditasya ca||
snehasvedavimuktasya mṛdukoṣṭhasya dehinaḥ|
doṣāstathāvaśiṣṭāstu janayanti pravāhikām||
atiraukṣyādatisvehādauṣadhānāṃ ca sambhramāt|
nṛṇāṃ śūlaṃ prabhavati gudasraṃsaśca dāruṇaḥ||
vamane recane vāpi pīte vegavidhārite|
vātapittakaphāḥ kruddhāḥ kurvanti hṛdayagraham||
tasya gātrāṇi sīdanti klamaḥ śūlaśca dāruṇaḥ|
śirograhaśca koṣṭhārtirvisaṃjñaśca bhavatyapi||
svinnasya mṛdukoṣṭhasya snehitasya virecanam|
atimātrauṣadhaṃ pītaṃ jīvādānāya kalpate||
sṛjecchuklaṃ sa śakṛtā kṛṣṇaṃ vā raktameva vā|
lodhrapuṣpasavarṇaṃ vā bhaṇḍipuṣpanibhaṃ tathā||
pravirikto yadā śītāṃ kriyāṃ samupasevate|
śaraṇāni ca śītāni vāri śītaṃ ca sevate||
srotāṃsi kupito vāyustasya cāvṛtya tiṣṭhati|
kharībhavati tenāsya doṣāḥ skandanti dehinaḥ||
vātamūtrapurīṣāṇāmapravṛttistṛṣā jvaraḥ|
vibandha eṣa vyākhyāto bhiṣajāṃ buddhivibhramāt||
asnigdhaṃ tu yadā rūkṣamauṣadhaṃ pāyayed bhiṣak|
gṛhṇāti kupitastasya sarvagātrāṇi mārutaḥ||
kaṭipṛṣṭhaśirogrīvāhṛdayaṃ ca yadānilaḥ|
prapadyate tadā mohaṃ cittanāśaṃ ca gacchati||
daśaitā vyāpadaḥ proktā rūpairnānāvidhaiḥ pṛthak|
cikitsāṃ tāsu kurvīta yathātreyasya śāsanam||
tatrāyoginamabhyajya tailena lavaṇena ca|
saṅkaraprastarasvedairnāḍīsvedairathapi vā||
samyaṅ nirūḍhaṃ viśrāntaṃ pariṣicya sukhāmbunā|
jāṅgalena rasenaiva bhojayenmṛdumodanam||
phalatailena koṣṇena tatastamanuvāsayet|
ādhmānaṃ parikartaśca parisrāvaśca śāmyati||
sālo rubūkastriphalā dantī citraka eva ca|
sarpirebhirvipakvaṃ ca pāyayecca yathābalam||
tatsnehapītaṃ susnigdhaṃ svedakarmopapāditam|
vamanaṃ recanaṃ vāpi pāyayettīkṣṇamauṣadham||
hṛdrogo gātraharṣaśca śīrṣaskandhagrahastathā|
vibandhaḥ parikartaśca śūlaścānena śāmyati||
parikartaḥ parisrāvo yathātattvena śāmyati|
kaṣāyavastiṃ praṇayetpicchāvastimathāpi vā||
śṛtaṃ madhu ghṛtaṃ vāpi kṣīravastiṃ pradāpayet|
jīvanīyopasiddhena sarpiṣā cānuvāsayet||
eraṇḍamūlaṃ triphalā drākṣā kāśmaryameva ca|
ebhiḥ śṛtaṃ payo dadyādvividhairapi miśrayet||
yāvaśūko bhavetkṣāraḥ saindhavaṃ sāmlavetasam|
hiṅgu dāḍimasāraśca pippalyo viśvabheṣajam||
sarpiṣā payasā vāpi madyenoṣṇodakena vā|
pītaṃ nivartayecchūlaṃ tīvraṃ ca parikartikām||
devadāru sabilvaṃ ca rāsnā karkaṭakāhvayā|
eraṇḍo'pyatha pūtiśca tailamebhirvipācayet||
tenānuvāsayeccainaṃ tadā sampadyate sukham|
gātrastambhaśca śūlaśca parikartaśca śāmyati||
upadravāṇāmeteṣāṃ śāntiryadi na vidyate|
teṣāṃ cikitsitaṃ kuryādyathāsvaṃ svaṃ cikitsitam||
evaṃ cikitsitaṃ proktamayoge vistareṇa me|
atiyoge tu vakṣyāmi vyāpadāṃ yaccikitsitam||
atipravṛttau vamane virecanamupācaret|
virecane cātiyukte vamanaṃ pāyayed bhiṣak||
vamane cātiyukte tu kāryamāsthāpanaṃ bhavet|
ubhayoratiyoge tu stambhanaṃ hitamucyate||
ullikhetkṣaudrayuktena sa narastaṇḍulāmbunā|
pādau ca secayeddhastau tathā vāpyavagāhayet||
lāmajjakamṛṇālaiśca candanaiśca sapadmakaiḥ|
sandihyātsarvagātrāṇi kaṣāyaiścāvasecayet||
dhātakī madhukaṃ lodhraṃ padmaṃ mocarasastathā|
nīlotpalaṃ samaṅgā ca kaṭphalaṃ padmakesaram||
etairlohitaśālīnāṃ siddhāṃ peyāṃ suśītalām|
pibenmākṣikasaṃyuktaṃ tadasya sthāpanaṃ param||
lākṣā lodhrassamaṅgā ca mākṣikaṃ kamalotpalam|
ājena payasā pītaṃ raktastambhanamucyate||
rasaṃ kapitthapatrasya pāyayenmadhusaṃyutam|
payasā bādaraṃ drākṣāṃ madhunā ca pibennaraḥ||
rasāśca jāṅgalā deyā vyaktā dāḍimasārakaiḥ|
ghṛtadāḍimasaṃyuktā deyāḥ syuḥ kṣīravastayaḥ||
atipravṛtte rakte tu raktamevānuvāsayet|
śāśaṃ mārgamathaurabhraṃ chāgaṃ māhiṣameva vā||
odanaṃ payasā''āmṛdya paripūtaṃ samākṣikam|
pibedikṣurasairvāpi parimardyaudanaṃ punaḥ||
nyagrodhodumbaraplakṣavetasāśvatthajaistathā|
śṛṅgairathaiṣāṃ kṣīreṇa yavāgūḥ sthāpanaṃ param||
padmaṃ śālmaliniryāsaḥ samaṅgā dhātakī tathā|
jambvāmrasāro madhukaḥ lājā lākṣā priyaṅgavaḥ||
etaiḥ śṛtaṃ payaḥ peyaṃ yavāgūṃ cāpi pāyayet|
taṇḍulodakayuktāni pibedvā madhunā saha||
odanaḥ koradūṣāṇāṃ madhunā sasyasaṃbhṛtaḥ|
palaṅkaṣāsamāyuktaścātisāraṃ nivartayet||
saṃyuktaṃ tilatailena saptarātrasthitaṃ dadhi|
koradūṣaudanopetamatisārapraṇāśanam||
saptarātraṃ sthite takre mūlakaṃ sādhayedbhiṣak|
koradūṣaudano yojyaḥ tenātīsāranāśanaḥ||
vaṭāṅkuro mocaraso lodhramāmrāsthikandakaḥ|
taruṇāni ca bilvāni dhātakīkusumāni ca||
pañcarātrasthite takre yūṣamebhistu kārayet|
koradūṣaudano yojyaḥ tathātīsāranāśanaḥ||
nīlotpalaṃ nīlatilāḥ samaṅgā lodhrameva ca|
kṣīraṃ vipācayedebhiratīsāravināśanam||
tilatailaṃ tathā lodhramājaṃ kṣīraṃ vipācayet|
pravāhikā gudabhraṃśaḥ sarvametena śāmyati||
balā cātibalā bilvameraṇḍaścorakastathā|
ebhirvipācayetkṣīraṃ kaphapittapraṇāśanam||
hanustambhe ca pātavyamarśaḥśūle ca dāruṇe|
anenaiva ca kalkena kuryādācchāditaṃ śiraḥ||
tena mūrcchā bhramaścaiva praśāmyati śarīriṇām|
upadravāśca ye'pyanye teṣāṃ tattaccikitsitam||
śarkarāpadmakośīranyagrodhodumbarāstathā|
ebhirvipācayetkṣīraṃ tṛṣṇādāhapraṇāśanam||
kṣīravastiṃ vidadyācca picchāvastimathāpi vā|
atiyoge praśasyante śītāśca ghṛtavastayaḥ||
jīvādānacikitsāyāṃ yatpūrvaṃ parikīrtitam|
tatkartavyaṃ gudabhraṃśe tathā sampadyate sukham||
vātaśūle tu sañjāte saṅkaraprastarādikān|
svedāṃścaivopadehāṃśca svabhyaktānāṃ prayojayet||
vacāṃ śvadaṃṣṭrāṃ pūtīkāṃ pañcamūlīṃ ca sādhitām|
yadi vā''āsthāpanaṃ dadyātsukhoṣṇaṃ cānuvāsanam||
kṣāraiḥ kṣāraghṛtaiścāpi śūlaghnaissamupācaret|
dādhikaṃ ṣaṭpalaṃ vāpi pāyayenmātrayā ghṛtam||
evameva vibandhe'pi vidhiṃ kuryāccikitsakaḥ|
yāśca vyāpattayaḥ paścādvamane ca virecane||
tāścikitseta matimān yathā sve sve cikitsite|
grāmyān paudakaṃ māṃsaṃ snehito yo'pi sevate||
kaphe pravṛddhe tasyāśu vireko vamanaṃ bhavet|
ajīrṇe cauṣadhaṃ pītamalpaṃ vā yadi vā bahu||
utkleśe cordhvavāte ca duryukte vāpi bheṣaje|
evaṃ virecanaṃ pītaṃ vamanāyopapadyate||
karśite rūkṣite vāpi sukumāre sudāruṇe|
asaṃjātakaphasyaiva na ca snigdhasya dehinaḥ||
chardanaṃ vā sthitaṃ kṣubdhaṃ virekāyopapādayet|
tatrānupānamevāhuḥ kecitkecittvasāmpratam||
bheṣajaṃ hyatimātraṃ tu jīvādānāya kalpate|
ūrdhvaṃ daśāhātparataḥ snehasvedopapāditam||
pāyayettīkṣṇamevaitaṃ bheṣajaṃ kalpakalpitam|
siddhirvirecanasyaiṣā vamanasya ca kīrtitā|
vyāpadaśca cikitsā yā yathāvadanupūrvaśaḥ|| ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele siddhisthāne caturtho'dhyāyaḥ||
||pañcamo'dhyāyaḥ||
athāto vastimātrīyāṃ siddhiṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
saṃsṛṣṭabhakte śaṃsanti vastikarma cikitsakāḥ|
virikteṣvapare vaidyā nirūḍhe tvapare janāḥ||
trīṇyetānyavarāṇyāhuḥ na śaṃsanti viśāradāḥ|
mataṃ teṣāmahaṃ samyag vyāhaniṣyāmi hetubhiḥ||
satatantu viriktasya śītībhavati pāvakaḥ|
hṛdāśrayāṇi srotāṃsi gudakoṣṭhāśrayāṇi ca||
pravṛddho mārutastasya pakvādhānaṃ pradhavati|
śītīkaroti kāyāgniṃ balaṃ cāsyāpakarṣati||
srotassu śuddhāśuddheṣu yadi vastiḥ pradīyate|
sa gambhīro gataḥ kāye sarvaśo na nivartate||
chardiṃ pipāsāṃ hṛdrogaṃ pāṇḍutvaṃ śvayathuṃ tathā|
janayediha tasmāddhi viriktaṃ nānuvāsayet||
saṃsṛṣṭabhaktasya punaḥ jātadoṣasya dehinaḥ|
srotassu malalipteṣu bhavetsneho nirarthakaḥ||
tīkṣṇetaranirūheṇa nirūḍhasya śarīriṇaḥ|
samutkliṣṭeṣu doṣeṣu snehavastirna śasyate||
keciccāpi śarīrasya rātriparyuṣitasya ca|
praśāntā doṣasaṃjñānaḥ praśaṃsantyanuvāsanam||
vastikarmavidhānaṃ tu śṛṇu me nirupadravam|
snehitasya yathānyāyaṃ viriktasya ca kalpaśaḥ||
saṃsṛṣṭabhaktasya tathā punaśca snehitasya ca|
pañcarātrātparaṃ tasya kārayedanuvāsanam||
noparyasnigdhakoṣṭhasya kartavyamanuvāsanam|
vāto hyūrīkṛtastasya hṛdaye paritiṣṭhati||
ucitādbhaktamānādapyardhamātraṃ tu bhojayet|
tanunā mudgayūṣeṇa jāṅgalena rasena vā||
bhuktvā kṛtasamāśvāsaḥ śakṛnmūtre visṛjya ca|
savyena pārśvena naraḥ saṃviśecchayane same||
saṃkocayeddakṣiṇaṃ ca sakthi vāmaṃ prasārayet|
vāmopadhānaṃ kurvīta śirasyupahitaṃ naraḥ||
athāsya praṇayedvastiṃ pṛṣṭhavaṃśānugaṃ sukham|
yathākarmabalaṃ caiva na drutaṃ na vilambitam||
nirvyalīko mṛdurvastirvyapanītānilastathā|
gāḍhānilānubandho'syāpraśasto vastikarmaṇi||
netraṃ vastimukhe dhṛtvā samyagekena pāṇinā|
prapīḍayeccāpareṇa hastau cāpi na kampayet||
pūrvaṃ ca gudamabhyajya yantraṃ hastau ca mānavaḥ|
pravartayedathottānaṃ pādau vāsya prasārayet||
muhūrtānna nigṛhṇīyāddatte vastau yathāsukham|
tataḥ paramato vegānāgatānna vidhārayet||
avibandhassavātaśca sapurīṣassa sidhyati|
dattamātre punaryasya tailavastirnivartate||
na sa karyakaraḥ proktastasyānyaṃ praṇidhāpayet|
tṛṣitassa tathā rātriṃ kālyamuṣṇodakaṃ pibet||
ekāntare ca dātavyo bastirna satataṃ nṛṇām|
abhyaṅgonmardanaṃ caiva koṣṇatoyāvasecanam||
uṣṇodakānupānaṃ ca vastikarmaṇi śasyate|
caturṣu triṣu vā vaidyo yāvadvā snigdhatā punaḥ||
snehavastiṣu datteṣu kuryādāsthāpanaṃ tataḥ|
ārdrīkṛteṣu srotassu snehena vihitena ca||
śuddhaye srotasāṃ dadyānnirūhānyuktitaḥ sukham|
aśuddhe maline koṣṭhe snehavastiḥ prayojitaḥ||
pradarśayati vaiśiṣyaṃ śukle parvavyayīkṛtam| virecanopacāraśca kartavyo vastikarmaṇi||
yavāgūpratipānaṃ ca bastikarmaṇi varjayet|
caturviṃśatisaṅkhyātāḥ nirūhāssānuvāsanāḥ||
etatkarmeti nirdiṣṭamūrdhvavāte pradāpayet|
mṛdumadhyamatīkṣṇeṣu rogeṣu kuśalo bhiṣak||
mṛdumadhyamatīkṣṇaṃ ca vastikarma ca kārayet|
grāmyānūpaudakaṃ māṃsaṃ piṣṭānnaṃ palalaṃ dadhi||
amlaśākavikārāṃśca bhakṣyāṇi vividhāni ca|
śītodakasya pānaṃ ca tathā śītāvagāhanam||
gurūṇi cānnapānāni vastikarmaṇi varjayet|
śāliṣaṣṭikamudgāṃśca bhojanārthaṃ pradāpayet||
vyañjanārthaṃ pradeyāḥ syuḥ śaśatittirilāvukāḥ|
jñānavānsumitāhāro vaidyaśuśrūṣureva ca|
yaśca vastimupāsīta sa jīveccharadāṃ śatam|| ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele siddhisthāne pañcamo'dhyāyaḥ||
||ṣaṣṭo'dhyāyaḥ||
athāta upakalpasiddhiṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
upakalpamato vasternetraṃ snehaṃ ca sarvaśaḥ|
teṣāṃ prayogayuktiṃ ca kriyāṃ karma ca me śṛṇu||
bālānāmatha vṛddhānāṃ yuvamadhyamayostathā|
svasthānāmāturāṇāṃ ca vastikarma praśasyate||
sauvarṇaṃ rājataṃ tāmraṃ kāṃsyaṃ trapujamāyasam|
śṛṅgavaṃśāsthivaṃśāgrāṇyāhurnetrāṇi paṇḍitāḥ||
yathākramamathaitāni pradhānānītarāṇi ca|
uttamādhamamadhyeṣu manuṣyeṣu prayojayet||
gulikāsyaṃ tu netraṃ syādanūnadvādaśāṅgulam|
aṅgule karṇikāvāsaḥ tāṃ ca na kramayedgude||
tatra gopucchasaṃsthānaṃ suślakṣṇaṃ sukhakarṇikam|
pradeśinīparīṇāhaṃ dānaṃ tena praśasyate||
tatra mātrāśataṃ sthitvā svenaiva vinivartate|
yadyakāle navasneho madyamāṃsaprakalpitaḥ||
nyūnakālanivṛttaśca śīghratvānna nirūhati|
tatastu madhyamaḥ kiñcinnivarteta śataistribhiḥ||
evaṃ kālagatikrānto yo vastirna nivartate|
tīkṣṇenāsthāpanenaivaṃ kṣipraṃ pratinivartate||
tasmāttīkṣṇataraṃ vastiṃ pūrvamevopakalpayet|
prāṇasaṃdhāraṇe hyuccairvibandho na praśasyate||
tasmātkālaṃ ca yo vyādhiṃ nirūhāṇāṃ balābalam|
pūrṇarūpaṃ ca yo vetti sa mohaṃ na nigacchati||
na tathā hyanyato hanyānnāśanirna hutāśanaḥ|
duṣprayukto yathā vastiḥ prāṇaissadyo viyojayet||
śastaḥ prāṇihito vastissadyo rogaharastathā|
samyaṅ nivṛtto, rogaughairhyanivṛttastu prāṇahā||
sa nivṛttastu vegena doṣān gambhīramāsthitān|
unmārgasthānnayenmārgaṃ mārgasthāṃścānulomayet||
vastivego'nilaṃ hanti parjanyaṃ ca yathānilaḥ|
kaphapittavikārāhi sarve vātavaśānugāḥ||
aśītirvātikā rogāścatvāriṃśattu pittajāḥ|
viṃśatiḥ ślaiṣmikāścāpi saṃsṛṣṭānāmasaṃgrahāt||
sarveṣāmeva caiteṣāṃ rogāṇāṃ saṃgrahaissadā|
arditānāṃ hi bhūtānāṃ vastiragryaṃ parāyaṇam||
anilaprabhavā hyete sarve vastivaśānugāḥ|
tasmādardhaṃ cikitsāyāḥ sarvaṃ vā vastirucyate||
yastu vastiṃ niṣeveta cāturmāsyaṃ sadā naraḥ|
rogāssaṃvarjayeyustaṃ siṃhāvāsaṃ yathā gajāḥ||
ete guṇaviśeṣāśca vyākhyātā vastikarmaṇi|
nirūhasyāsya sāmarthyamupacāraṃ ca me śṛṇu||
svinne paryuṣite jīrṇe tvatyantaṃ ca nirūhayet|
so'sya mūtraṃ purīṣaṃ ca śleṣmāṇaṃ cāpakarṣati||
vātānulomyaṃ kurute balaṃ sañjanayatyapi|
viṣasthiraṃ doṣabalaṃ yacca nāḍīṣu saṃsthitam||
viśodhayati tatsarvaṃ nirūhaḥ sa prayojitaḥ|
rogānīkasya sarvasya paro nāsti hi tatsamaḥ||
vyākulānāṃ ca keśānāṃ sīmantakaraṇaṃ yathā|
tathā bhṛśaṃ vyākulānāṃ doṣāṇāṃ syānnirūhataḥ||
nirūho hi samāyuktassamyagvīryassamairguṇaiḥ|
vyādhīnathoddharetkṣipraṃ puṇyaṃ yuktaṃ naraṃ yathā||
kṛcchramūtrapurīṣāṇāṃ śūlināṃ vātagulminām|
virecanādyudāvarte hṛdroge pārśvaśūlini||
udareṣu prameheṣu kuṣṭheṣu kṛmikoṣṭhiṣu|
śleṣmaṇā saha saṃsṛṣṭe śoṇitena ca dūṣite||
saṃsṛṣṭeṣu ca sarveṣu sannipātodbhaveṣu ca|
gambhīrā dhātugā ye ca teṣāṃ śreṣṭhaṃ nirūhaṇam||
yadā kaphaṃ viricyeta śaṅkhasphaṭikasannibham|
ṛte mūtrapurīṣābhyāṃ tannirūhasya lakṣaṇam||
suśodhitaṃ nirūheṇa nātiparyuṣitaṃ tathā|
sukhoṣitaṃ samāśvastaṃ bhojitaṃ cānuvāsayet||
ekato vastikarmāṇi hyekato'nyaccikitsitam|
amṛtārthaṃ vastikarma kurute suprayojitam||
śuklaṃ bahvavarodhasya na kṣayaṃ yāti dehinaḥ|
strīṣu pradharṣaṇaṃ kuryānnarāṇāṃ kṣīṇaretasām||
vastiprayogāt ṣaṇḍo'pi pumān bhavati sarvaśaḥ|
kṛśānāmapi martyānāṃ māṃsasaṃjananaṃ smṛtam|| ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele siddhisthāne ṣaṣṭho'dhyāyaḥ||
||saptamo'dhyāyaḥ||
athātaḥ phalamātrāsiddhiṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
phalamātraṃ nirūheṣu śreṣṭhamāhurmanīṣiṇaḥ|
teṣāṃ tu madanaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ pravadanti cikitsakāḥ||
jīmūtakaṃ praśaṃsanti keciddhāmārgavaṃ tathā|
kṛtavedhanamityeke kuṭajaṃ prāhurapyatha||
garhitāssarva evaite vādāḥ pratyekaśaḥ smṛtāḥ|
bheṣajānāṃ ca saṅghāto nirūheṣu praśasyate||
vātapittakaphagnāṃstu nirūhāṃścānupūrvaśaḥ|
vyākhyāsyāmo yathānyāyaṃ sannipātahitāśca ye||
dvai pañcamūlyau kolāni naladaśśuṣkamūlakam|
dvipalīnāni sarvāṇi jaladroṇe vipācayet||
aṣṭabhāgāvaśeṣaṃ tu kaṣāyamavatārayet|
kalkapeṣyāṇi cemāni bheṣajāni samāvapet||
nikuñcikāṃ kharapuṣpāṃ pippalīṃ saindhavaṃ vacām|
trapusorvārubījāni śatapuṣpāṃ yavānikām||
kuṣṭhaṃ ca tagaraṃ caiva yaṣṭīmadhukameva ca|
sakṣīrassakaṣāyaśca sagomūtraśca kīrtitaḥ||
satailaśca nirūhaḥ syādvātarogavināśanaḥ|
paṭolapatramūlaṃ ca picumandaṃ śatāvarīm||
trāyamāṇāṃ balāṃ rāsnāṃ bṛhatīṃ kaṇṭakārikām|
guḍūcīṃ triphalāṃ śyāmāṃ sāribāṃ vaṃśakatvacam||
dvipalīnān pṛthagbhāgān pūrvakalpena sādhayet|
madhukaṃ vaṃśakaphalaṃ śatapuṣpāṃ durālabhām||
mahāsahāmaṃśumatīṃ kalkaṃ kuryātpṛthak pṛthak|
kṣīreṇātha kaṣāyeṇa ghṛtena madhunā tathā||
kalpamiśraḥ sumathito nirūhaḥ pittanāśanaḥ|
daśamūlī karañjau dvau dantī citrakameva ca||
ekāṣṭhīlā turaṅgī ca triphalā devadāru ca|
pūtikaṃ rauhiṣaṃ śyāmā mūrvā rājatṛṇāni ca||
pṛthak pañcapalānetān sādhayetsarvakalpavit|
adhobhāgordhvabhāgdravyaissādhayetsādhu yojitaiḥ||
mūtratailayutaḥ śreṣṭhaḥ nirūhaḥ śleṣmanāśanaḥ|
aśvagandhāṃ balāṃ rāsnāṃ śvadaṃṣṭrāṃ sapunarnavām||
paṭolapañcamūlyau ca bhārṅgīṃ śyāmāṃ śatāvarīm|
guḍūcīṃ triphalāṃ śigruṃ karṇikāyāḥ phalāni ca||
uśīraṃ rajanīpatraṃ pūtikaṃ kattṛṇāni ca|
pṛthak pañcapalānetān khaṇḍaśaśchedayed bhiṣak||
jaladroṇe vipaktavyamaṣṭabhāgāvaśeṣitam|
madhukaṃ śatapuṣpāṃ ca saindhavaṃ sarṣapān guḍam||
hapuṣāmajamodāṃ ca madanasya phalāni ca|
vacāmaśokabījāni pippalīkuṣṭhameva ca||
etāni kalkapeṣyāṇi bheṣajāni pṛthak pṛthak|
kṣīratailasamāyukto ghṛtamūtrasamanvitaḥ||
khajena mathitaḥ pūtaḥ sa nirūhaḥ praśasyate|
ekena yadi vā dvābhyāṃ tribhirvātipramāṇataḥ||
kiṃcinnyūnātiriktaṃ vā jñātvā vyādhibalābalam|
kuryāt, viṃśativarṣāṇāṃ tatra prasthapramāṇataḥ||
atha dvādaśavarṣāṇāmardhaprasthaṃ prayojayet|
kaṣāyakuḍavaṃ cātra ṣaḍvarṣāṇāṃ prayojayet||
yuktaṃ lavaṇatailabhyāṃ kṣīreṇa madhunā tathā|
kaṣāyavastiṃ kurvīta svehād dviprasṛtaṃ bhiṣak||
prasṛtaṃ madhyame kuryātprasṛtārdhaṃ kanīyasi|
nātiśītaṃ na vātyuṣṇaṃ kaṣāyamupakalpayet||
vāyuḥ kupyati śītatvādatyuṣṇaṃ tu gudaṃ dahet|
atisāndrātigrathito doṣeṇa sa hi mūrchati||
madapravego bhavati vyāpaccāsyopajāyate|
athavāpi tanuryasya kaṣāyo yojito bhavet||
so'lpaṃ harati doṣaṃ ca na ca saṃśodhayed gudam|
parikartaṃ ca śūlaṃ ca janayecca pravāhikām||
atyarthamadhuro datto vidagdhaḥ stambhavedanām|
grahaṇīṃ raktamarśāṃsi pāṇḍurogaṃ bhagandaram||
atitīkṣṇastu duryuktaḥ kṣipraṃ pratinivartate|
na nirharati doṣāṃśca jvaraṃ mūrchāṃ karoti ca||
jvaritaṃ dahyamānaṃ ca mūrchamānaṃ ca mānavam|
śītairmadhurasaṃyuktairyojayetkṣīravastibhiḥ||
yasyātha lavaṇaḥ koṣṭhe nirūhaḥ paittikīṃ rujām|
janayettatra peṣyaṃ syānmadhureṇa nirūhaṇam||
nirūho madhuro yasya śleṣmāṇaṃ parikopayet|
kaṭukaiśca kaṣāyaistaṃ nirūhedyasya mārutaḥ||
prakupyedadhikaṃ tatra mārutaghnaṃ nirūhaṇam|
tasmāllavaṇayuktāni dravyāṇi madhurāṇi ca||
bhiṣagdadyānna śītāni tathoṣṇāni vivecayet|
pitte śīto nirūhaḥ syāduṣṇastu kaphavātayoḥ||
praśleṣmaṇi pravāte vā pyuṣṇamāsthāpanaṃ bhavet|
madanasya phalaṃ piṣṭvā tailenācchena sādhayet||
tannirūhe praṇaṃsanti phalatailaṃ cikitsakāḥ|
gomūtrayuktametattu phalatailanirūhaṇam||
nirūhaṃ lavaṇaṃ kuryācchreṣṭhamānāhabhedanam|
bilvairaṇḍaphalaṃ vāpi paced dviprasthasammitam||
amlaṃ salavaṇaṃ sādhyamaṣṭabhāgāvaśeṣitam|
kaṣāye tatra pīlūni kalkapeṣyāṇi yojayet||
phalatailayuto vastirdeya ānāhabhedanaḥ|
eṣa sarvānudāvartān śophagulmān bhinattyapi||
athaiva vastirādhmānamudāvartaṃ vināśayet|
oṣṭhajihvaṃ pṛṣṭhaccūrṇaṃ bṛhatīṃ kaṇṭakārikām||
candanaṃ padmakaṃ caiva vṛntaṃ puṣpaṃ ca śālmaleḥ|
khaṇḍitānāṃ yavānāntu kuḍavaṃ tatra yojayet||
tadaikadhyaṃ pacetsarvaṃ jale cāṣṭaguṇe bhiṣak|
ghṛtena madhunā caiva picchāṃ saṃyojya śaktitaḥ||
nirvāhikāyāṃ śaṃsanti picchāvastimimaṃ budhāḥ|
atastu ṣoḍaśī caiva pale dve ca punarnave||
kaṭāhake pañcamūlīṃ yavaiḥ saha vipācayet|
upodakāyāḥ kṣuṇṇāyāḥ svarasaṃ ca supīḍitam||
yuktaṃ tena kaṣāyeṇa ghṛtena saha yojayet|
mākṣikeṇa ca saṃyukto vastistu yavamūrcchitaḥ||
picchāvastiriti khyātaḥ śūle nirvāhikāsu ca|
taruṇaṃ śiṃśupāpatraṃ karbudārasya pallavam||
kṣuṇṇaissaha yavaissiddhaiḥ saṃpiṣṭo ghṛtasaṃyutaḥ|
mākṣikakṣīrasaṃyuktaḥ sa vastiryavamūrcchitaḥ||
nirvāhikāṃ vātaśūlaṃ kṣatakṣīṇasya nāśayet|
ete nirūhā vyākhyātāḥ sarvarogavināśanāḥ|
vyākuleṣu vikāraistu tān bhiṣak saṃprayojayet|| ityāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
iti bhele saptamo'dhyāyaḥ||
||aṣṭamo'dhyāyaḥ||
athāto daśavyāpadāmāsthāpanānuvāsanasiddhiṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti ha smāha bhagavānātreyaḥ|
snehavastirnirūhaśca dvividhaṃ yāti vibhramam|
na yujyate vā duryukto yukto vāpyatipūryate||
teṣāṃ vipratipannānāṃ vastīnāṃ vyāpado daśa|
tāsāṃ rūpaṃ cikitsāṃ ca pravakṣyāmyanupūrvaśaḥ||
na māti vā gude yukto vātādigadapīḍite|
purīṣeṇāvṛte vāpi sa na gāḍhaṃ prapīḍitaḥ||
arśasā pratisaṃruddhaḥ śleṣmaṇā pihite gude|
guruyantrayuto vāpi netre vā jīrṇasarpite||
na gacchati gude vastirvibhrānteṣu ca dhātuṣu|
tamayogamiti brūyādatiyogaṃ nibodha me||
vātābhibhūtapitte vā śleṣmaṇā vā'hitaśinaḥ|
purīṣeṇābhibhūtaśca dūraṃ cātiprapīḍitaḥ||
na pratyāyātyadho vastirbhītasyonmathitasya vā|
gudavalyāṃ tṛtīyāyāmarśobhirvā nivāritaḥ||
virecitasya vāntasya madyaśokākulasya ca|
na pratyāyātyadho vastirūrdhvaṃ vātipravartate||
teṣāmupadravā ghorā jāyante vastivibhramāt|
jīvādānaṃ tṛṣācchardiḥ hṛdrogaḥ śvayathustathā||
śūlamarśāṃsi pāṇḍutvaṃ śvāso'tha parikartikā|
eṣāṃ pratyekaśo rūpaṃ cikitsāṃ ca nibodha me||
gurukoṣṭhasya rūkṣasya bahudoṣasya dehinaḥ|
asnigdhasya guruvyādheryadi vastiḥ pradīyate||
atyalpo vātha rūkṣo vā śītavīryauṣadhastathā|
lavaṇasneharikto vā tanussāndro'tha vā bhṛśam||
sambaddhabahudoṣasya doṣānutkleśayatyapi|
tatrasthā yatra sarpanti doṣāste pīḍitā bhṛśam||
kaṭāvasthyūrujaṅghe ca pādapṛṣṭhodare tathā|
prasyandante bhaveccāsya saśūlamudakodaram||
vastiśūlamudāvarto gudaśoṣo jvarastathā|
niṣṭhīvakāsahṛllāsā bhaktadveṣo vivarṇatā||
snigdhasya tasya svinnasya saṃkaraprastarādibhiḥ|
nirūhaṃ kārayettīkṣṇamamoghāprativāraṇam||
bilvāgnimanthasyonākakāśmaryāśca sapāṭalāḥ|
rāsnā balāgokṣurakayavakolakulutthakāḥ||
etatsarvaṃ samāhṛtya jaladroṇe vipācayet|
caturbhāgāvaśiṣṭaṃ taṃ kaṣāyamavatārayet||
kalkena sarṣapāṇāṃ ca pīlūnāṃ madanasya ca|
sayavāsakaṣāyaṃ tu tattailalavaṇena ca||
mūtrāṇi ca yathālābhamatha samyaṅ nirūpayet|
yathā tṛtibalā ... ... ... ... ||
tatra vāditraśabdāṃśca śreyasaśca svanānapi|
śāntiṃ puṇyāhaghoṣāṃśca kuryāccāpi pradakṣiṇam||
taṃ nidhāya śubhe kumbhe vastiṃ dadyātpramāṇataḥ|
devaprasādato vastirarditānāṃ mahāguṇaḥ||
sarvarogaharo vṛṣyaḥ kṣatakṣīṇarujāpahaḥ|
vātapittasamutthānā rogā ye parikīrtitāḥ||
kāsadāhajvarādyāṃstānnāśayedaviśaṅkitaḥ|
tribhāgamākṣiko hyeṣa valīpalitanāśanaḥ||
rūpavarṇakaraḥ puṃsāṃ balamāṃsavivardhanaḥ|
śatāvarīṃ sahacaraṃ rāsnāṃ gokṣurakaṃ balām||
śataṃ śataṃ kalpayitvā toyadroṇaśate pacet|
droṇāvaśeṣitaṃ tacca paripūtaṃ nidhāpayet||
barhikukkuṭahaṃsānāṃ māṃsānyapi pṛthak pṛthak|
vasā māhiṣavārāhameṣāṇāṃ prasthasammitam||
vidāryāmalakekṣūṇāṃ rasaprasthāḥ pṛthak pṛthak|
tatra tailaghṛtaprasthau kṣīramaṣṭaguṇaṃ bhavet||
tadgomayāgninā yuktyā kalkenānena sādhayet|
tugākṣīryāmalakyau ca drākṣāmadhukacandanaiḥ||
madhūlikāpaṭolābhyāṃ patraṃ nīlotpalasya ca|
viśālāṃ ca mṛṇālaṃ ca kapikacchuphalāni ca||
kharjūraudanapākyau ca śītapākīṃ nidigdhikām|
salāmajjatvacaṃ patraṃ hvīberaṃ samahāsaham||
jīvakarṣabhakau medāṃ pippalīścāpi saṃharet|
tatsiddhaṃ śītalīkṛtya kṣaudraprasthe niyojayet||
tatra vāditraśabdāṃśca kuryātsvastyayanāni ca|
gajaskandhaṃ samāropya śvetacchatrānupālitam||
celāvṛtaṃ ca kurvīta sādhuśabdāṃśca sarvaśaḥ|
tato'smin dāpayedvastiṃ puṇye'hani rasāyanam||
vastinānena dattena gacchennārīśataṃ naraḥ|
na vātra yantraṇā kāciduktā nārīvihāragā||
vṛṣyo balakaro vastirāpta āyurvivardhanaḥ|
valīpalitakhālityaṃ sevamāno niyacchati||
ṣaṇḍāṃśca puruṣān kuryāllakṣaṇāḍhyān guṇānvitān|
kṣīṇān kṣatānnaṣṭaśuklān viṣamajvarapīḍitān||
yonīnāṃ vyāpado vandhyāṃ vastireṣa niyacchati|
guḍūcīsvarasaprasthaṃ grāhayedyantrapīḍitam||
śatāvarīrasaprasthaṃ tadvatsahacarasya ca|
vidāryāmalakekṣūṇāṃ drākṣākharjūrayorapi||
pṛthak kuryādrasaprasthaṃ prasthau dvau tailasarpiṣoḥ|
ājamāhiṣagavyāni kṣīrāṇi dviguṇāni ca||
kṣuṇṇapiṣṭāni cemāni peṣayitvā vipācayet|
vadhūṭikāmuccaṭāṃ ca madhukaṃ pippalīmapi||
śṛṅgāṭakaṃ puṣkarikāṃ kāṇḍaṃ nīlotpalasya ca|
jīvakarṣabhakau medāṃ balāṃ nīlotpalāni ca||
tugākṣīrīṃ mahāmedāṃ puṇḍarīkasya kesaram|
vṛṣameṣacakorāṇāṃ haṃsakukkuṭayoriha||
jīvañjīvakamattākṣakurarīkrauñcabarhiṇām|
vasā majjā ca sattvānāṃ meṣamāṃsarasaissaha||
tribhāgaṃ mākṣikaṃ caiva yuñjyātsamyagvipācite|
śaṅkhabherīninādaiśca paṭahairvā murīsvanaiḥ||
āropayedgajaskandhe śvetacchatradhvajāyutam|
siddhānāṃ paramaṃ devaṃ maharṣigaṇasevitam||
śaraṇaṃ sarvabhūtānāmarcayed vṛṣabhadhvajam|
āśīrbhirmaṅgalāśaṃsistutibhirdevatārcanaiḥ||
rasāyanāni sidhyanti viparītāni cānyathā|
taṃ snehaṃ gamayedvastiṃ rāsāyanikamuttamam||
athāhāravihāreṣu na cainaṃ yantrayedbhiṣak|
vyāpannayonayo vandhyā raktagulminya eva ca||
yā vā'patyaṃ mṛtaṃ sūte yāśca naṣṭārtavāstathā|
naṣṭaśuklakṣatakṣīṇā viṣamajvarapīḍitāḥ||
prakṣīṇamāṃsarudhirā valīpalitapīḍitāḥ|
āśu praśamayedrogān tānāyurmāṃsavardhanam||
rasāyanamidaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ narāṇāmamṛtopamam|
rasāyanavidhānāni proktānyetāni yāni ca||
sahasraśatapākāni kāryāṇi vibhave sati|
madhukaṃ saindhavaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ śatapuṣpā hareṇavaḥ||
madanāni ca rāsnā ca bhārṅgī, nīparakajvalam|
mari ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ... ||
māyūraṃ kaukkuṭaṃ vāpi veśavāraṃ sukuṭṭitam|
sahitaṃ daśamūlyā tu jaladroṇe vipācayet||
rasasya tasya pūtasya prasthārdhaṃ ca pradāpayet|
vasātailaghṛtānāṃ tu madhunaḥ prasṛtadvayam||
hapuṣāśatapuṣpāṇāṃ mustānāṃ cāpi kārṣikam|
kalkapeṣyaṃ punardadyādvastiṃ salavaṇaṃ sukham||
pādagulphorujaṅghāsu trikavaṅkṣaṇabastiṣu|
śiśne vṛṣaṇayoścaiva vātarogaṃ niyacchati||
mṛgāṇāṃ tittirāṇāṃ ca tathaiva bilavāsinām|
ānūpānāṃ khagānāṃ ca kalpamevaṃ prayojayet||
tulyaṃ madhu ca tailaṃ ca tasmāduṣṇodakaṃ samam|
dvau karṣau śatapuṣpāṇāṃ karṣaśca lavaṇottamaḥ||
eṣa rāsāyano vastirdīpanīyo'tha bṛṃhaṇaḥ|
balavarṇakaro vṛṣyo yāpano nirupadravaḥ||
gulmodavartitādhmānapramehakṛmikoṣṭhinām|
nityaṃ gāḍhapurīṣāṇāṃ vastireṣa hitaḥ smṛtaḥ||
ghṛtaṃ madhu ca tailaṃ ca teṣāṃ mānaṃ samaṃ bhavet|
pūrvakalkena deyāni sa vastirbalavarṇakṛt||
vastau meḍhragate doṣe vastyasātmyacikitsite|
paittike mūtrakṛcchre ca pittavyādhiṣu cottamaḥ||
tulyaṃ ghṛtamatha kṣaudraṃ tayormāṃsarasassamaḥ|
akṣamātraṃ ca mustānāṃ sa vastiḥ pūrvakalpavat||
gulphorujānupṛṣṭheṣu sarvataḥ stambhiteṣu ca|
balāsapādaharṣeṣu vastiḥ praśamanaḥ smṛtaḥ||
vastau vṛṣaṇameḍhreṣu śūlamāśu niyacchati|
trikajānūrupṛṣṭhādivedanāṃ cāpakarṣati||
mṛdutvānna nivatante kṣubhyante vastayaśca vā|
tīkṣṇairāsthāpanaṃ kāryaṃ kṣipraṃ pratinivartayet||
mṛdutvādyāpanānāṃ tu kecidvibhramabhīravaḥ|
dīpanānāṃ kaṣāyeṇa yojayanti cikitsakāḥ||
evamanyeṣu vibhrāntau sanirūheṣu vā punaḥ|
vastayaḥ kalpadṛṣṭā hi yathā kāle sukhāvahāḥ||
kṣīṇātiriktā dūṣyante tasmādyogaṃ samāharet|
nityaṃ nārīvihārāṇāṃ kṣīṇānāmalparetasām||
evameva vidhānaṃ ca retobalavivardhanam|
svecchāvihārān vakṣyāmi vastīn śuklavivardhanān||
rasaḥ karkaṭakānāṃ tu śarkarāmastusaṃyutaḥ|
ghṛtasaubarcalayuto vastirvṛṣyatamaḥ smṛtaḥ||
haṃsasārasalāvānāṃ tittirikrauñcabarhiṇām|
kalpenānena kartavyā vājīkaraṇavastayaḥ||
payaḥ kukkuṭamāṃsena vipakvaṃ madhusaṃyutam|
caṭakāṇḍarasaścaiva kukkuṭāṇḍarasastathā||
ghṛtamākṣikasaṃyuktaḥ śarkarāsaindhavairyutaḥ|
eṣa vastiḥ smṛto vṛṣyaḥ māṃsaśuklavivardhanaḥ||
yataśśataṃ vā ṣaṣṭiṃ vā nārīrgacchati mānavaḥ|
eṣa varṇabalotsāhaḥ sthavire'pi pradṛśyate||
timiṅgile vā makare pāṭhīne nakramīnayoḥ|
dugdhaṃ kūrme'thakumbhīre kalpayecchiśumāravat||
vārāhabastavṛṣaṇau vṛṣaṇau govṛṣasya ca|
caṭakarkaṭakāṃścaiva kṣīreṇa saha sādhayet||
tannirūhaṃ tu śukreṇa bastānāmatha miśrayet|
uccaṭekṣurakaṃ cātra kalkapeṣyaṃ samāvapet||
ghṛtamākṣikasaṃyuktaṃ praṇītaḥ śarkarāyutaḥ|
kiṃcillavaṇito vastirgamayetstrīśatānyapi||
eṣa nārīvihārāṇāṃ bahvantaḥpuravāsinām|
retobalakaraḥ śreṣṭhaḥ krīḍāvastiranuttamaḥ||
ātmaguptāphalaiḥ kṣīramuccaṭekṣurakaiḥ śṛtam|
bhojane vānupāne vā vṛṣyārthaṃ samprayojayet||
rasāyanā vastayastu ye mayā pūrvadarśitāḥ|
tānetairmāṃsaniryūhairyojayetkalpakalpitān||
babhrusūkarakhaḍgāśca chāgagomahiṣāstathā|
krauñcakāraṇḍavāḥ krauñcacakravākabakāstathā||
nirūhameṣāṃ saṃhṛtya lābhatassādhu yojayet|
kṣīraṃ panasabījāni kapikacchūphalāni ca||
uccaṭekṣurabījāni madhukaṃ tālamastakam|
pippalyaśśāribā drākṣā kharjūro madanānyapi||
jīvakarṣabhakau medā bījaṃ nīlotpalasya ca|
garbheṇānena saṃsiddhaṃ caṭakāṇḍarasāyutam||
śarkarāmadhusaṃyuktaṃ ... ... ... ... ... ||
